GENERAL PART

Chapter One.

TASK AND LIMITS OF APPLICATION OF THE CRIMINAL CODE

### Section I.

Task of the Criminal Code

#### Art. 1.

(1) (Amd. - SG No. 1 of 1991) The Criminal Code has the task of protecting from criminal infringements the personality and rights of citizens and the entire legal order established in the country.

(2) To accomplish this task, the Criminal Code determines which socially dangerous acts are crimes and what punishments are imposed for them, and establishes the cases when, instead of punishment, measures of social influence and education may be imposed.

### Section II.

Limits of application of the Criminal Code

Art. 2. (1) For every crime, the law which was in force at the time of its commission shall be applied.

(2) If different laws follow until the sentence enters into force, the law which is most favorable to the perpetrator shall be applied.

#### Art. 3.

(1) The Criminal Code shall be applied to all crimes committed on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria.

(2) The question of the responsibility of foreigners who enjoy immunity with respect to the criminal jurisdiction of the Republic of Bulgaria shall be resolved in accordance with the norms of international law accepted by it.

#### Art. 4.

(1) The Criminal Code shall be applied to Bulgarian citizens for crimes committed by them abroad.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) A citizen of the Republic of Bulgaria may not be extradited to another state or to an international court for the purposes of criminal prosecution, unless this is provided for in an international treaty ratified, promulgated and entered into force for the Republic of Bulgaria.

#### Art. 5.

The Criminal Code shall also be applied to foreigners who have committed abroad crimes of a general nature which affect the interests of the Republic of Bulgaria or of a Bulgarian citizen.

#### Art. 6.

(1) The Criminal Code shall also be applied with respect to foreigners who have committed abroad a crime against peace and humanity which affects the interests of another state or foreign citizens.

(2) The Criminal Code shall also be applied to other crimes committed by foreigners abroad when this is provided for in an international agreement in which the Republic of Bulgaria participates.

#### Art. 7.

In the cases of Art. 4 and 5, pre-trial detention and punishment served abroad shall be deducted. When the two punishments are of different kinds, the punishment served abroad shall be taken into account in determining the punishment by the court.

#### Art. 8.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 8 - SG No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) The sentence of a foreign court for a crime to which the Bulgarian Criminal Code applies shall be taken into account in the cases established by an international agreement in which the Republic of Bulgaria participates.

(2) (New - SG No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) A sentence that has entered into force, rendered in another Member State of the European Union, for an act which constitutes a crime under the Bulgarian Criminal Code, shall be taken into account in any criminal proceedings conducted against the same person in the Republic of Bulgaria.

Chapter Two.

CRIME

### Section I.

General provisions

#### Чл. 9.

(1) A crime is that socially dangerous act (action or omission) which has been committed culpably and is declared punishable by law.

(2) An act is not criminal if, although it formally constitutes the elements of a crime provided for in the law, due to its insignificance it is not socially dangerous or its social danger is manifestly negligible.

#### Чл. 10.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 50 от 1995 г.) A socially dangerous act is one that endangers or harms the individual, the rights of citizens, property, the legal order established by the Constitution of the Republic of Bulgaria, or other interests protected by law.

#### Чл. 11.

(1) A socially dangerous act is committed culpably when it is intentional or negligent.

(2) An act is intentional when the perpetrator was aware of its socially dangerous character, foresaw its socially dangerous consequences, and desired or permitted the occurrence of those consequences.

(3) An act is negligent when the perpetrator did not foresee the occurrence of the socially dangerous consequences but was obliged and able to foresee them, or when he foresaw the occurrence of those consequences but thought to prevent them.

(4) Negligent acts are punishable only in cases provided for by law.

(5) When the law qualifies the act as a more serious crime due to the occurrence of additional socially dangerous consequences, if intent is not required for those consequences, the perpetrator is liable for the more serious crime when he acted negligently with respect to them.

#### Чл. 12.

(1) An act committed in necessary defense is not socially dangerous - in order to protect state or public interests, the person or rights of the defender or of another from an immediate unlawful attack by causing harm to the attacker within the necessary limits.

(2) There is an exceeding of the limits of necessary defense when the defense manifestly does not correspond to the character and danger of the attack.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) There is no exceeding of the limits of necessary defense if the attack was committed by forcible entry or breaking into a dwelling.

(4) (Предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) The perpetrator is not punished when he commits the act while exceeding the limits of necessary defense if this is due to fright or confusion.

Чл. 12а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) (1) Causing harm to a person who has committed a crime during his apprehension for delivery to the authorities and prevention of the possibility of committing another crime is not socially dangerous if there is no other way to apprehend him and if no exceeding of the necessary and lawful measures was permitted during that apprehension.

(2) The necessary measures for apprehending a person who has committed a crime are exceeded when there is a manifest disproportion between the character and degree of social danger of the crime committed by the apprehended person and the circumstances of the apprehension, as well as when manifestly excessive harm is caused to the person without necessity. In these cases criminal liability is borne only in cases of intentional causing of harm.

Чл. 12б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 32 от 2010 г., в сила от 28.05.2010 г.) An act committed by a person who acted as an undercover officer within the powers assigned to him by law is not socially dangerous.

#### Чл. 13.

(1) An act committed by someone in extreme necessity is not socially dangerous - in order to save state or public interests, as well as his own or another's personal or property interests from imminent danger which the perpetrator could not avoid in another way, if the harm caused by the act is less significant than that prevented.

(2) There is no extreme necessity when the very avoidance of the danger constitutes a crime.

Чл. 13а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) An act committed in justified economic risk is not socially dangerous - in order to achieve a substantial socially beneficial result or to avoid significant harm, if it does not contradict an express prohibition established by a normative act, corresponds to contemporary scientific-technical achievements and experience, does not endanger the life and health of another, and the perpetrator has done everything depending on him to prevent the harmful consequences that occurred.

(2) When deciding whether the risk is justified, the relationship between the expected positive result and the possible negative consequences, as well as the probability of their occurrence, is also taken into account.

#### Чл. 14.

(1) Ignorance of the factual circumstances that belong to the elements of the crime excludes intent with respect to that crime.

(2) This provision also applies to negligent acts when the very ignorance of the factual circumstances is not due to negligence.

#### Чл. 15.

An act is not committed culpably when the perpetrator was not obliged or could not foresee the occurrence of the socially dangerous consequences (accidental act).

#### Чл. 16.

An act carried out in execution of an unlawful official order given in the established procedure is not committed culpably if it does not obviously impose a crime on the perpetrator.

Чл. 16а. (1) (Нов - ДВ, бр. 84 от 2013 г., предишен текст на чл. 16а - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) An act carried out by a person who is a victim of trafficking in human beings is not committed culpably when he was compelled to carry it out in direct connection with that status.

(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) An act carried out by a minor who is a victim of a crime under Art. 155, 156, 158a and Art. 188, para. 2, or by a minor used for the creation of pornographic material is not committed culpably when he was compelled to carry it out in direct connection with that status.

### Раздел II.

Preparation and Attempt

#### Чл. 17.

(1) Preparation is the preparation of means, the finding of accomplices and in general the creation of conditions for the commission of the intended crime before its execution has begun.

(2) Preparation is punishable only in cases provided for by law.

(3) The perpetrator is not punished when he has voluntarily abandoned the commission of the crime.

#### Чл. 18.

(1) An attempt is the commenced execution of an intentional crime in which the executory act has not been completed or, although completed, the socially dangerous consequences of that crime provided for in the law and desired by the perpetrator have not occurred.

(2) In an attempt the perpetrator is punished with the punishment provided for the completed crime, taking into account the degree of realization of the intention and the reasons why the crime remained uncompleted.

(3) In an attempt the perpetrator is not punished when he has voluntarily: a) abandoned the completion of the execution of the crime, or b) prevented the occurrence of the criminal consequences.

#### Чл. 19.

In the cases of Art. 17, para. 3 and 18, para. 3, if the act in which the preparation or attempt was expressed contains the elements of another crime, the perpetrator is liable for that crime.

### Раздел III.

Complicity

#### Чл. 20.

(1) Accomplices in the commission of an intentional crime are the perpetrators, instigators and aiders.

(2) A perpetrator is one who participates in the actual execution of the crime.

(3) An instigator is one who has intentionally induced another to commit the crime.

(4) An aider is one who has intentionally facilitated the commission of the crime through advice, explanations, promise to provide assistance after the act, removal of obstacles, procurement of means, or in another way.

#### Art. 21.

(1) All accomplices are punished with the penalty provided for the committed crime, taking into account the nature and degree of their participation.

(2) The instigator and the accessory are liable only for that which they intentionally instigated or aided the perpetrator.

(3) When due to a specific personal quality or relationship of the perpetrator the law elevates the committed act to a crime, both the instigator and the accessory are liable for this crime, even when these circumstances are not present for them.

(4) Special circumstances by which the law excludes, reduces or increases the punishment for any of the accomplices are not taken into account for the other accomplices with respect to whom these circumstances are not present.

#### Art. 22.

(1) The instigator and the accessory are not punished if on their own initiative they renounce further participation and prevent the act from being committed or prevent the occurrence of the criminal consequences

(2) In these cases, the provision of Art. is applied accordingly

### 19. Section IV.

Multiple crimes

#### Art. 23.

(1) If several crimes have been committed with one act, or if one person has committed several separate crimes before there is a final judgment for any of them, the court, after determining a punishment for each crime separately, imposes the most severe of them.

(2) (Amd. - SG, iss. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, iss. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, amd. - SG, iss. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) The imposed punishments of public reprimand and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6, 7 and 9 are added to the determined most severe punishment. If deprivation of identical rights has been imposed, the one that is for the longest term is imposed.

(3) When the punishments are of different types and any of them is a fine or confiscation, the court may add it in whole or in part to the most severe punishment.

#### Art. 24.

When the imposed punishments are of the same type, the court may increase the determined overall most severe punishment by at most one half, but the punishment thus increased may not exceed the sum of the individual punishments, nor the maximum amount provided for the respective type of punishment.

#### Art. 25.

(1) The provisions of Art. 23 and 24 apply also when the person has been convicted by separate sentences.

(2) In these cases, if the punishment under any of the sentences has been served in whole or in part, it is deducted if it is of the type of the overall punishment determined to be served.

(3) (Amd. - SG, iss. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) The served punishment of probation is deducted in full from the deprivation of liberty and vice versa, with two days of probation being counted as one day of deprivation of liberty.

(4) (New - SG, iss. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) When under one or more of the sentences the person has been released from serving the imposed punishment under the procedure of Art. 64, para. 1 or of Art. 66, the question of the execution of the overall punishment is decided when determining it.

#### Art. 26.

(Amd. - SG, iss. 62 of 1997, amd. - SG, iss. 92 of 2002) (1) The provisions of Art. 23 - 25 do not apply in cases of continuing crime - when two or more acts that separately constitute one or different compositions of the same crime have been committed over short periods of time, under the same circumstances and with homogeneity of guilt, whereby the subsequent acts appear from an objective and subjective standpoint as a continuation of the preceding ones.

(2) In the case of a continuing crime, the perpetrator is punished according to the acts included in it, taken in their totality, and with the overall criminal result caused by them.

(3) When the individual acts constitute different compositions, the continuing crime is punished under the more severe of them, taking into account the significance of the acts committed under qualifying circumstances and of the qualifying circumstances themselves for the overall criminal activity.

(4) When the qualifying circumstances do not significantly affect the severity of the overall criminal activity, the latter is classified under the lighter composition, with the qualifying circumstances being taken into account when determining the punishment.

(5) When some of the acts are completed and others constitute an attempt and the completed acts do not significantly affect the nature of the overall criminal activity, the perpetrator is punished as for an attempt.

(6) The provisions of this article do not apply with respect to crimes against the person of different citizens and to crimes committed after the indictment for them has been submitted to the court, as well as to crimes committed before the submission of the indictment but not included in it.

#### Art. 27.

(1) (Amd. - SG, iss. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) When a person commits a crime after having been convicted by a final sentence of deprivation of liberty, but before having served this punishment, the court adds to the unserved part in whole or in part the punishment under the second sentence, if it is deprivation of liberty. The determined overall punishment may not be less than the punishment under the second sentence.

(2) (Amd. - SG, iss. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) The punishment under the second sentence is added in full if it is deprivation of liberty for more than five years or if it has been imposed for a crime committed repeatedly or constituting dangerous recidivism.

(3) When the person has committed a crime after serving the punishment under the previous sentence, the imposed punishment for this crime is served in full.

#### Art. 28.

(1) The punishment provided in the special part of this code for a crime committed repeatedly is imposed if the perpetrator has committed a crime after having been convicted by a final sentence for another such crime.

(2) This provision applies also when it concerns crimes of the same type against public and personal property.

#### Art. 29.

(1) The more severe punishments provided in the special part of this code for crimes constituting dangerous recidivism are imposed when the perpetrator: a) (amd. - SG, iss. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) commits the crime after having been convicted for a serious intentional crime to deprivation of liberty of not less than one year, the execution of which has not been postponed under Art. 66; b) (amd. - SG, iss. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) commits the crime after having been convicted two or more times to deprivation of liberty for intentional crimes of general nature, if for at least one of them the execution of the punishment has not been postponed under Art. 66. c) (repealed - SG, iss. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982)

(2) When applying the provisions of the preceding paragraph, crimes committed by the perpetrator as a minor are not taken into account.

(3) (New - SG, iss. 95 of 1975) When for a given crime compositions for repeat commission and for dangerous recidivism are simultaneously provided and the act fulfills the elements of both compositions, the provision for dangerous recidivism is applied.

#### Art. 30.

(1) The rules of Art. 28 and 29 do not apply if five years have elapsed since the serving of the punishment under the previous sentences. Rehabilitation within this period does not exclude their application.

(2) (New - SG, iss. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) In the case of conditional conviction and conditional early release, the period under para. 1 begins to run from the day on which the probationary period has expired.

Chapter Three.

CRIMINALLY LIABLE PERSONS

#### Art. 31.

(1) Criminally liable is an adult person - one who has reached 18 years of age, who in a state of sanity commits a crime.

(2) A minor - one who has reached 14 years of age but has not reached 18 years of age - is criminally liable if they were able to understand the nature and significance of the act and to direct their conduct.

(3) (Am. - SG, No. 107 of 1996) Minors to whom the act cannot be imputed as guilt are placed by decision of the court in an educational boarding school or in another suitable facility, if this is required by the circumstances of the case.

(4) With regard to the criminal liability of minors, the special rules provided for in this code apply.

#### Art. 32.

(1) A young child - one who has not reached 14 years of age - is not criminally liable.

(2) With regard to young children who have committed socially dangerous acts, appropriate educational measures may be applied.

#### Art. 33.

(1) Not criminally liable is a person who acts in a state of insanity - when due to mental underdevelopment or prolonged or short-term disorder of consciousness they were unable to understand the nature or significance of what was done or to direct their conduct.

(2) (Am. - SG, No. 95 of 1975) No punishment is imposed on a person who has committed a crime when, before the pronouncement of the sentence, they fall into a disorder of consciousness as a result of which they cannot understand the nature or significance of their conduct or direct it. Such a person is subject to punishment if they recover.

#### Art. 34.

With regard to the persons specified in the previous article, in the cases provided for in this code, appropriate compulsory medical measures may be applied.

Chapter Four.

PUNISHMENT

### Section I.

General Provisions

#### Art. 35. (1) Criminal liability is personal.

(2) Punishment may be imposed only on a person who has committed a crime provided for in the law.

(3) The punishment is proportionate to the crime.

(4) Punishment for a crime is imposed only by the established courts.

#### Art. 36.

(1) Punishment is imposed with the aim: 1) to correct and re-educate the convicted person to observe the laws and good morals, 2) to have a deterrent effect on them and to deprive them of the opportunity to commit other crimes and 3) to have an educational and deterrent effect on the other members of society.

(2) Punishment cannot have as its purpose the infliction of physical suffering or the degradation of human dignity.

(3) (New - SG, No. 153 of 1998) There is no death penalty in the Republic of Bulgaria.

#### Art. 37. (1) The punishments are:

1. (new - SG, No. 50 of 1995) life imprisonment;

1a. (previous item 1 - SG, No. 50 of 1995) deprivation of liberty;

2. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004) probation;

2a. (previous item 2 - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, rep. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005)

3. confiscation of available property;

4. fine;

5. (rep. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004)

6. deprivation of the right to hold a specific state or public office;

7. deprivation of the right to practice a specific profession or activity;

8. (rep. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004)

9. deprivation of the right to received orders, honorary titles and distinctions;

10. deprivation of military rank;

11. public censure.

(2) (Am. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) For the most serious crimes that threaten the foundations of the republic, as well as for other particularly dangerous intentional crimes, life imprisonment without substitution is provided as a temporary and exceptional measure.

### Section II.

Types of Punishments

#### Art. 38.

(Am. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) (1) The punishment of life imprisonment without substitution, provided for in the special part for a given type of crime, is imposed only if the specific crime committed is exceptionally serious and the purposes specified in Art. 36 cannot be achieved through a lighter punishment.

(2) The punishment of life imprisonment without substitution cannot be imposed on a person who at the time of committing the crime had not reached twenty years of age, and with regard to military personnel, as well as in wartime - eighteen years of age. The punishment of life imprisonment without substitution cannot be imposed on a woman who was in a state of pregnancy at the time of committing the crime or at the time of pronouncement of the sentence.

Art. 38a. (New - SG, No. 50 of 1995) (1) Life imprisonment is the compulsory isolation of the convicted person until the end of their life in places for serving the punishment of deprivation of liberty.

(2) Life imprisonment is imposed when the crime committed is exceptionally serious.

(3) Life imprisonment may be substituted with a punishment of deprivation of liberty for a term of thirty years if the convicted person has served not less than twenty years.

(4) During the serving of the punishment of life imprisonment, working days are not credited.

(5) The served punishment of life imprisonment is credited as deprivation of liberty.

#### Art. 39.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) Deprivation of liberty may be from three months to twenty years.

(2) (Para. 2 rep., previous para. 3, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 50 of 1995, am. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) By way of exception, the punishment of deprivation of liberty may be for a term of up to thirty years in substitution of life imprisonment for multiple crimes under Art. 24 and 27, para. 1, as well as for certain particularly serious intentional crimes in cases specifically indicated in the special part of this code.

#### Art. 40.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, am. - SG, No. 32 of 2016) The punishment of deprivation of liberty is served in prisons, as well as in correctional homes and prison dormitories attached to them.

(2) (Am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, rep. - SG, No. 92 of 2002)

(3) With regard to young adults, special care is taken.

(4) (Suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) With regard to convicted persons with severe psychopathy or suffering from a disorder of consciousness that does not exclude sanity, as well as with regard to convicted persons who are dependent on narcotic substances, appropriate medical care is taken.

#### Чл. 41.

(1) Serving the punishment of deprivation of liberty is accompanied by appropriate, correspondingly paid socially useful labor, through which the re-education of the convicted persons is aimed at, as well as the creation and enhancement of their professional qualifications.

(2) Along with this, other measures for education and training are also applied.

(3) The labor performed is credited for reducing the term of the punishment, whereby two working days are counted as three days of deprivation of liberty.

(4) (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 89 of 1986) When the convicted person during serving the punishment of deprivation of liberty systematically evades socially useful labor, commits an intentional crime or serious violations of the established order and thereby shows that they are not reforming, the court may revoke wholly or partially the crediting of working days from the last two years before commission of the last violation.

(5) (Para. 5 repealed, former para. 6 - SG, issue 89 of 1986) The order and manner of execution of the punishment of deprivation of liberty, including the special care under Art. 40, para. 3, the payment of labor of convicted persons, as well as their placement in employment after their release are regulated by law.

(6) (Amend. - SG, issue 89 of 1974, former para. 7 - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, effective from 01.06.2009, amend. - SG, issue 13 of 2017, effective from 07.02.2017) The initial regime of serving the punishment of deprivation of liberty is determined by the court in accordance with the provisions of this code and the special law.

#### Чл. 42.

(1) In wartime the military court may postpone until the end of military operations the execution of the imposed punishment of deprivation of liberty, by sending the convicted person to the active army. The postponement of execution may be revoked if the convicted person commits a new crime.

(2) On proposal of the commander the court may fully or partially release the convicted person sent to the active army under the procedure of para. 1 from serving the imposed punishment if they prove themselves as a good defender of the homeland.

(3) The court may also without proposal of the commander fully or partially release from serving the imposed punishment those discharged from the active army due to disability.

Чл. 42а. (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) (1) Probation is a set of measures for control and impact without deprivation of liberty, which are imposed together or separately.

(2) (Amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) The probation measures are:

- 1. mandatory registration at current address;
- 2. mandatory periodic meetings with a probation officer;
- 3. restrictions on free movement;
- 4. (amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) inclusion in courses for professional qualification, programs for social impact;
- 5. correctional labor;
- 6. unpaid labor for the benefit of society.
  - (3) (Amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) The probation measures have duration:
- 1. from 6 months to three years - for the measures under para. 2, items 1 - 4;
- 2. from three months to two years - for correctional labor;
- 3. from 100 to 320 hours annually for no more than three consecutive years - for unpaid labor for the benefit of society.
  - (4) (Amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) The measures under para. 2, items 1 and 2 are imposed mandatorily on all persons convicted to probation, and the measures under para. 2, items 5 and 6 are not imposed on minors under 16 years of age.
  - (5) Probation is executed according to a procedure determined by law.

Чл. 42б. (New - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) (1) (Suppl. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, effective from 01.06.2009) The probation measure mandatory registration at current address is appearance and signing by the convicted person before the probation officer or official designated by them in accordance with the periodicity determined by the court, but not less than twice weekly.

(2) The probation measure mandatory periodic meetings with a probation officer is conducted at the probation service on whose territory the current address of the convicted person is located. Exceptionally they may be conducted at another suitable place determined by the probation officer if important reasons require this. The meetings are planned or extraordinary at the request of the probation officer or the convicted person.

(3) The probation measure restrictions on free movement is imposition of one or several of the following prohibitions for:

- 1. visiting precisely defined places, districts and establishments in the sentence;
- 2. leaving the settlement for more than 24 hours without permission from the probation officer or the prosecutor;
- 3. leaving the dwelling they occupy for a specified period of the day.
  - (4) (Amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) The probation measure inclusion in courses for professional qualification, programs for social impact is aimed at labor integration or building social habits and skills for lawful behavior of the convicted person.
  - (5) The probation measure unpaid labor is labor performed for the benefit of society without restriction of the freedom of the convicted person.

#### Чл. 43.

(Amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) (1) The probation measure correctional labor is executed at the workplace of the convicted person and includes deductions from their remuneration from 10 to 25 percent for the benefit of the state. The time during which the measure is served is not credited as work experience.

(2) When the convicted person becomes unemployed, the court replaces the remainder of the correctional labor with unpaid labor for the benefit of society, whereby for one day of the remainder one hour of unpaid labor is determined. In this case the term of unpaid labor may be below the minimum under Art. 42a, para. 3, item 3.

(3) The provision of para. 2 applies also when the convicted person leaves the workplace where they are serving the punishment and does not notify the probation officer of their new workplace within one month.

(4) The time during which deductions under para. 1 are not made is not credited as execution of the probation measure correctional labor.

Чл. 43а. (New - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) If the convicted person without valid reason does not fulfill the imposed probation measure, on proposal of the respective probation council the court may:

- 1. impose another probation measure;
- 2. (suppl. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, effective from 01.06.2009) replace wholly or partially the probation with deprivation of liberty whereby two days of probation are replaced with one day of deprivation of liberty; in these cases the term of deprivation of liberty may be below the minimum under Art. 39, para. 1.

#### Чл. 44.

(1) Confiscation is compulsory and gratuitous alienation for the benefit of the state of property belonging to the guilty person or part thereof, of certain properties of the guilty person or parts of such properties.

(2) (Repealed - SG, issue 62 of 1997)

#### Чл. 45.

(1) Confiscation is not ordered if the guilty person does not possess available property that may be subject to this punishment.

(2) Not subject to confiscation are items necessary for the convicted person and their family for personal and household use, items necessary for exercising their occupation, listed in a list adopted by the Council of Ministers, as well as means for maintenance of their family for one year.

#### Чл. 46.

Upon confiscation the state is liable up to the amount of the value of the confiscated property for restoration of damages caused by the crime, and thereafter for the obligations of the convicted person arising before initiation of criminal prosecution, when their remaining available property is not sufficient for restoration of damages and payment of obligations.

#### Art. 47.

(1) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The fine is determined according to the property status, income and family obligations of the offender, and the provisions of Chapter Five are also applied when determining its amount. It may not be less than 100 leva.

(2) The fine is collected from the property remaining from the convicted person, even after his death, if the sentence has entered into force before that.

(3) Things that are not subject to confiscation may not be sold for forced collection of the fine.

#### Art. 48.

(Repealed - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004)

#### Art. 49.

(1) (Amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004) The punishments of deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7, when imposed independently or with another punishment not connected with deprivation of liberty, are imposed for a specified term of up to three years within the limits established in the special part of this code.

(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 54 of 1978) When deprivation of such a right is imposed together with deprivation of liberty, its term may exceed the term of the latter by no more than three years, unless otherwise provided in the special part of this code.

(3) The term begins to run from the entry into force of the sentence, but the convicted person may not enjoy the rights of which he is deprived before serving the sentence of deprivation of liberty.

(4) The term of deprivation of rights is reduced by as much time as the term of deprivation of liberty has been reduced due to pardon, work or deduction of pre-trial detention.

(5) (Amend. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) A person sentenced to life imprisonment without replacement is deprived of the rights indicated in the sentence forever.

#### Art. 50.

(1) The punishments of deprivation of the right to hold a certain state or public position and deprivation of the right to practice a certain profession or activity are imposed in the cases provided by law, if holding the respective position or practicing the respective profession or activity is incompatible with the nature of the crime committed.

(2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004)

(3) (Previous para. 2 - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) The punishments of deprivation of the right to received orders, honorary titles and distinctions and deprivation of military rank may be imposed only upon conviction for serious crimes.

#### Art. 51.

After the expiration of the term, the convicted person may again exercise the rights of which he was deprived by the sentence. This does not apply to the rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 9 and 10, which may be acquired again only according to the established procedure for this.

#### Art. 52.

The punishment of public reprimand consists in public reprimand of the guilty person, which is announced before the respective collective, through the press or in another appropriate manner in accordance with that indicated in the sentence.

#### Art. 53.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 53 - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) Independently of criminal liability, the following are forfeited in favor of the state: a) (suppl. - SG, No. 7 of 2019) things that belong to the guilty person and were intended or served to commit an intentional crime. When the things are missing or have been alienated, their equivalent value is awarded; b) things that belong to the guilty person and were the subject of an intentional crime - in cases expressly provided in the special part of this code.

(2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) The following are also forfeited in favor of the state: a) things that are the object or means of the crime, the possession of which is prohibited, and b) (amend. - SG, No. 7 of 2019) the direct and indirect benefit acquired through the crime, if they are not subject to return or restoration; when the benefit is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value is awarded.

(3) (New - SG, No. 7 of 2019) For the purposes of para. 2, letter "b":

- 1. direct benefit is any economic advantage that occurred as a direct consequence of the crime;
- 2. indirect benefit is any economic advantage that occurred as a result of disposing of the direct benefit, as well as any property received as a result of subsequent full or partial transformation of a direct benefit, including when it has been mixed with property acquired from legal sources; property is subject to forfeiture up to the value of the included direct benefit together with the increases in property that have occurred, if they are directly related to the disposal or transformation of the direct benefit and the inclusion of the direct benefit in the property.

Chapter Five.

DETERMINATION OF THE PUNISHMENT

#### Art. 54.

(1) The court determines the punishment within the limits provided by law for the crime committed, guided by the provisions of the general part of this code and taking into account: the degree of public danger of the act and the offender, the motives for committing the act and other mitigating and aggravating circumstances.

(2) Mitigating circumstances condition the imposition of a lighter punishment, and aggravating circumstances - a heavier punishment.

#### Art. 55.

(1) In case of exceptional or numerous mitigating circumstances, when even the lightest punishment provided by law proves disproportionately severe, the court:

- 1. determines the punishment below the lowest limit;
- 2. replaces: a) (amend. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) life imprisonment - with deprivation of liberty from fifteen to twenty years; b) (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) deprivation of liberty, when the lowest limit is not provided - with probation, and for minors - with probation or public reprimand; c) (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) probation - with a fine from one hundred to five hundred leva.
  - (2) In the cases of item 1 of the previous paragraph, when the punishment is a fine, the court may go below the lowest limit by no more than one half.
  - (3) In these cases, the court may not impose the lighter punishment that the law provides alongside the punishment of deprivation of liberty.
  - (4) (Deleted - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982)

#### Art. 56.

Those that have been taken into account by the law when defining the respective crime are not mitigating and aggravating circumstances.

#### Art. 57.

(1) When the special part of this code provides for the possibility that one of two or more punishments be imposed for the crime committed, the court determines the most appropriate punishment in type and amount, guided by the rules of the previous articles.

(2) When the special part of this code provides for the possibility that two or more punishments be imposed simultaneously for a given crime, the court, guided by the rules of the previous articles, determines the amount of each of them so that they in their totality correspond to the purposes indicated in Art. 36.

#### Чл. 58.

The court may apply the provisions of Art. 55 also in the following cases: a) in case of attempt - due to the incompleteness of the crime, taking into account also the circumstances under Art. 18, para. 2; b) in case of aiding - when the degree of participation of the perpetrator in the crime is small.

Чл. 58а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) (1) When rendering a conviction in the cases under Art. 373, para. 2 of the Code of Criminal Procedure, the court determines the punishment of deprivation of liberty, guided by the provisions of the General Part of this Code and reduces the punishment so determined by one third.

(2) In the cases under Art. 57, para. 1, when the court determines life imprisonment without replacement as the most appropriate type of punishment, it does not impose it, but replaces the punishment of life imprisonment with deprivation of liberty from twenty to thirty years.

(3) The court determines the size of the punishment of deprivation of liberty within the limits of the lowest minimum size and the highest maximum size of the punishment of deprivation of liberty determined under the conditions of para. 2 and the punishment of deprivation of liberty provided in the Special Part of this Code.

(4) In cases where the conditions under para. 1 - 3 and the conditions of Art. 55 are simultaneously present, the court applies only Art. 55, if it is more favorable for the perpetrator.

(5) The rules under para. 1 - 4 do not apply to the punishments under Art. 37, para. 1, items 2 - 11 provided in the Special Part of this Code.

#### Чл. 59.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) The time during which the convicted person was detained or in respect of whom a measure of house arrest was taken, is deducted when executing the punishment of deprivation of liberty or probation, as follows:

- 1. one day of detention is credited as one day of deprivation of liberty or as three days of probation;
- 2. two days of house arrest are credited as one day of deprivation of liberty or as two days of probation.
  - (2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) Detention within the meaning of para. 1, in addition to the measure of detention in custody, is also any other detention under the Code of Criminal Procedure, the Law on the Ministry of Internal Affairs or another law, related to the crime for which the person was convicted or was detained for execution of the punishment.
  - (3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., предишна ал. 2 - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) The provision of the previous paragraph also applies when the convicted person was detained on charges for another crime, for which the proceedings were terminated or ended with an acquittal, if in relation to the acts the provision of Art. 23, para. 1 could be applied.
  - (4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 2016 г.) When executing the punishment of deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7, the time during which for the same act the convicted person was deprived by administrative order or under Art. 69a of the Code of Criminal Procedure of the possibility to exercise these rights is deducted.

Глава шеста.

SPECIAL RULES FOR MINORS

#### Чл. 60.

The punishment of minors is imposed with the aim primarily that they be re-educated and prepared for socially useful work.

#### Чл. 61.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) With respect to a minor who committed due to passion or recklessness a crime that does not represent a great public danger, the prosecutor may decide not to initiate or to terminate the initiated pre-trial proceedings, and the court may decide not to refer for trial or not to convict, if educational measures under the Law on Combating Anti-Social Manifestations of Minors and Juveniles can be successfully applied to him.

(2) In these cases the court may itself impose an educational measure, notifying the local commission for combating anti-social manifestations of minors and juveniles or send it the file for the imposition of such a measure.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 107 от 1996 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) When the prosecutor decides not to initiate pre-trial proceedings or to terminate the initiated pre-trial proceedings, he sends the file to the commission for the imposition of an educational measure.

#### Чл. 62. Only the following punishments may be imposed on minors:

1. deprivation of liberty; 1а. (нова - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) probation;

2. public reprimand;

3. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) deprivation of the right to exercise a particular profession or activity under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

#### Чл. 63. (1) For minors the punishments provided in the Special Part of this Code are replaced:

1. (доп. - ДВ, бр. 50 от 1995 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г.) life imprisonment without replacement and life imprisonment - with deprivation of liberty from three to ten years;

2. deprivation of liberty for more than ten years - with deprivation of liberty up to five years;

3. deprivation of liberty for more than five years - with deprivation of liberty up to three years;

4. deprivation of liberty up to five years inclusive - with deprivation of liberty up to two years, but not more than provided by law;

5. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) fine - with public reprimand;

6. (нова - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2004 г.) probation for minors who have not reached 16 years of age - with public reprimand.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) For minors who have reached sixteen years of age, the punishments provided in the Special Part of this Code are replaced:

- 1. (доп. - ДВ, бр. 50 от 1995 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г.) life imprisonment without replacement, life imprisonment and deprivation of liberty for more than fifteen years - with deprivation of liberty from five to twelve years;
- 2. deprivation of liberty for more than ten years - with deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.
  - (3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) Within the limits under the previous paragraphs the court determines the punishment in accordance with the rules of Chapter Five.

#### Art. 64.

(1) (Am. - SG No. 107 of 1996) When the imposed sentence is deprivation of liberty for less than one year and its execution has not been suspended under Art. 66, the juvenile is released from serving it and the court places him in a correctional boarding school or imposes another educational measure provided for in the Act on Combating Antisocial Behavior of Minors and Juveniles.

(2) (Am. - SG No. 107 of 1996) At the proposal of the prosecutor or the respective local commission for combating antisocial behavior of minors and juveniles, the court may, even after the sentence has been issued, replace the placement in a correctional boarding school with another educational measure.

(3) The rule of para. 1 does not apply: a) when the juvenile has committed a crime during the serving of a sentence of deprivation of liberty and b) when he has been convicted after reaching the age of majority.

(4) The rule of para. 1 also does not apply in cases of repeated conviction if the court finds that for the correction and re-education of the perpetrator it is necessary for him to serve the sentence of deprivation of liberty and when: a) its term is not less than six months or b) if the perpetrator has already served a sentence of deprivation of liberty.

#### Art. 65.

(1) Juveniles serve the sentence of deprivation of liberty in a correctional home until reaching the age of majority.

(2) (Am. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) After reaching the age of majority, they are transferred to a prison or prison hostel. For completion of their education or qualification, at the proposal of the pedagogical council and with the permission of the prosecutor, they may be left in the correctional home until the age of twenty.

Chapter Seven.

RELEASE FROM SERVING AN IMPOSED SENTENCE

### Section I.

Suspended Sentence

#### Art. 66.

(1) (Am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, corr. No. 31 of 1982, am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004) When the court imposes a sentence of deprivation of liberty of up to three years, it may suspend the execution of the imposed sentence for a period of three to five years if the person has not been convicted to deprivation of liberty for a crime of general character and if the court finds that for achieving the objectives of the punishment and above all for the correction of the convicted person, it is not necessary to serve the sentence.

(2) (Am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004) The probationary period cannot exceed the term of the imposed sentence of deprivation of liberty by more than three years.

(3) (Rev. - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004)

(4) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, suppl. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) During the probationary period, the convicted person is obliged to work or study, unless he is obliged to undergo treatment.

#### Art. 67.

(1) When suspending the execution of the sentence, the court may assign to the relevant public organization or labor collective, with their consent, the provision of educational care for the convicted person during the probationary period.

(2) When such consent is lacking or when the court finds it necessary, it assigns to a specific person the educational care for the conditionally convicted person. If the conditionally convicted person has his place of residence in another locality, this person is determined by the respective district court.

(3) (New - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, am. - SG No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG No. 67 of 2023) When the suspended sentence of deprivation of liberty is not less than six months, the court may order one or more of the probation measures under Art. 42a, para. 2 during the probationary period.

(4) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, rev., former para. 3 - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004) When suspending the execution of the sentence with respect to a juvenile, the court notifies the respective local commission, which organizes the provision of educational care.

(5) (Am. - SG No. 95 of 1975, former para. 4 - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) The general control regarding the educational care and behavior of conditionally convicted persons is carried out by the district court of their place of residence.

(6) (Former para. 5 - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) The order and manner of applying the provisions of the preceding paragraphs are regulated by law.

#### Art. 68.

(1) If before the expiration of the probationary period determined by the court the convicted person commits another intentional crime of general character for which, even after that period, he is imposed a sentence of deprivation of liberty, he also serves the suspended sentence.

(2) If under the conditions of para. 1 the convicted person commits a negligent crime, the court may order that the suspended sentence not be served or be served in whole or in part.

(3) (Am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) If the conditionally convicted person does not fulfill without reasonable cause any of the probation measures determined for him under Art. 67, para. 3, the court may, at the proposal of the probation council, replace it with another or order to serve in whole or in part the suspended sentence of deprivation of liberty.

(4) (New - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) If the conditionally convicted person without reasonable cause interrupts the treatment, the court orders to serve in full the suspended sentence of deprivation of liberty.

(5) (Am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, former para. 4 - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) Except for the cases under the preceding paragraphs, the suspended sentence is not served.

#### Art. 69.

(1) With respect to a person conditionally convicted for a crime which he committed as a juvenile, the probationary period is from one to three years.

(2) With respect to such a person in the cases of para. 1 of the preceding article, the court may order that he be released in part or in full from serving the sentence whose execution was suspended.

Art. 69a. (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, am. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) In the cases under Art. 68, para. 2, 3 and 5 and Art. 69, para. 2, if the convicted person during the probationary period commits a new crime of general character for which he is imposed a sentence of deprivation of liberty, or continues not to fulfill without reasonable cause any of the probation measures determined for him under Art. 67, para. 3, he serves the remaining part of the sentence.

### Section II.

Early Release

#### Art. 70.

(1) (Amended - State Gazette, issue 153 of 1998, supplemented - State Gazette, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - State Gazette, issue 27 of 2009, amended - State Gazette, issue 13 of 2017, effective from 07.02.2017) The court may order conditional early release from serving the remaining part of the sentence of imprisonment with respect to a convicted person who has provided evidence of his/her reform and has actually served:

- 1. not less than one half of the imposed sentence;
- 2. not less than two thirds of the imposed sentence in cases of dangerous recidivism.
  - (2) (Amended - State Gazette, issue 92 of 2002, repealed - State Gazette, issue 13 of 2017, effective from 07.02.2017)
  - (3) (Repealed - State Gazette, issue 13 of 2017, effective from 07.02.2017)
  - (4) (Amended - State Gazette, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - State Gazette, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004) The conditional early release also affects the term of the sentences of deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
  - (5) (Amended - State Gazette, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - State Gazette, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004) Upon conditional early release, the court may release the convicted person from serving the imposed sentence of deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 or 7.
  - (6) (Amended - State Gazette, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, supplemented - State Gazette, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - State Gazette, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, supplemented - State Gazette, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - State Gazette, issue 27 of 2009, amended - State Gazette, issue 13 of 2017, effective from 07.02.2017) Upon conditional early release, a probationary period shall be established for the convicted person in the amount of the unserved part of the sentence. Within the probationary period, but for not more than three years, the court may order one of the probation measures under Art. 42a, para. 2, items 1 - 4, taking into account a report from the probation officer.
  - (7) (Supplemented - State Gazette, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - State Gazette, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004) The person released early shall serve separately also the unserved part of the sentence if during the probationary period he/she commits a new intentional crime for which a sentence of imprisonment is provided or does not comply with the imposed probation. If during this period the person released early commits a negligent crime, the court may order that the deferred sentence not be served or be served in full or in part.
  - (8) (Corrected - State Gazette, issue 29 of 1968) In the cases of the preceding paragraph, the convicted person shall serve in full the sentence from which he/she was released under para. 5 of this article.
  - (9) The term for rehabilitation under Art. 86 upon conditional early release begins to run from the moment of expiration of the probationary period.

#### Art. 71.

(1) The court may release early a minor sentenced to imprisonment if he/she has reformed, after having actually served not less than one third of the imposed sentence.

(2) With respect to a person convicted of a crime which he/she committed as a minor, after reaching the age of majority, the provisions of Art. 70 shall apply regarding the effect of early release.

#### Art. 72.

(Repealed - State Gazette, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - State Gazette, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004)

#### Art. 73.

(1) (Amended - State Gazette, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) With respect to persons released early, the court entrusts the organization of supervision and educational care for them during the probationary period to the respective commission, and for minors - to the respective local commission for combating antisocial manifestations of minors and juveniles.

(2) When necessary, the court entrusts the supervision and educational care to a specific public organization with its consent or to a specific person, notifying the supervisory or local commission thereof.

(3) General control and guidance regarding the educational care and behavior of persons released early shall be exercised by the district court of their place of residence.

(4) The procedure and manner of applying the provisions of the preceding paragraphs shall be regulated by law.

### Section III.

Pardon

#### Art. 74.

(Supplemented - State Gazette, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) The President may, through pardon, remit in full or in part the imposed sentence, and the death penalty, life imprisonment without replacement and life imprisonment - remit or commute.

Chapter Eight.

RELEASE FROM CRIMINAL LIABILITY

### Section I.

Conditional release from criminal liability with transfer to public guaranty

#### Art. 75.

(Repealed - State Gazette, issue 62 of 1997)

#### Art. 76.

(Repealed - State Gazette, issue 62 of 1997)

### Section II.

Release from criminal liability with imposition of measures for public influence by the comradely court

#### Art. 77.

(Repealed - State Gazette, issue 105 of 1991).

### Section III.

Release from criminal liability of minors with application of educational measures

#### Art. 78.

In the cases specified in Art. 61, the minor may be released from criminal liability with the application of an appropriate educational measure.

### Section IV.

Release from criminal liability with imposition of an administrative penalty

Art. 78a. (New - State Gazette, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - State Gazette, issue 10 of 1993, amended - State Gazette, issue 62 of 1997, amended - State Gazette, issue 21 of 2000, amended - State Gazette, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - State Gazette, issue 26 of 2010) An adult person shall be released from criminal liability by the court and shall be imposed a penalty from one thousand to five thousand leva when the following conditions are simultaneously present: a) (amended - State Gazette, issue 86 of 2005, effective from 29.04.2006) for the crime a sentence of imprisonment of up to three years or another lighter sentence is provided when it is intentional, or imprisonment of up to five years or another lighter sentence when it is negligent; b) the perpetrator has not been convicted of a crime of general character and has not been released from criminal liability under the procedure of this section; c) the property damages caused by the crime have been compensated.

(2) (Repealed - State Gazette, issue 21 of 2000)

(3) (Repealed - State Gazette, issue 21 of 2000)

(4) The court that imposes the fine under para. 1 may also impose an administrative penalty of deprivation of the right to exercise a specific profession or activity for a period of up to three years if deprivation of such right is provided for the respective crime.

(5) When for the committed crime only a fine or a fine and another lighter sentence is provided, the administrative penalty may not exceed the amount of that fine.

(6) (New - State Gazette, issue 26 of 2010) When the grounds under para. 1 are present and the act was committed by a minor, the court releases him/her from criminal liability by imposing an administrative penalty of public reprimand or an educational measure. The court may also impose an administrative penalty of deprivation of the right to exercise a specific profession or activity for a period of up to three years if deprivation of such right is provided for the respective crime.

(7) (New - State Gazette, issue 86 of 2005, effective from 29.04.2006, amended - State Gazette, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, supplemented - State Gazette, issue 27 of 2009, former para. 6 - State Gazette, issue 26 of 2010, amended - State Gazette, issue 95 of 2016, supplemented - State Gazette, issue 54 of 2017, supplemented - State Gazette, issue 67 of 2023) Paragraphs 1 - 6 shall not apply if the caused injury is serious bodily harm or death, or the perpetrator was in a state of intoxication, or after use of narcotic substances or their analogues, as well as in case of multiple crimes and when the crime was committed against an authority during or in connection with the performance of his/her service, except in cases where it is insult or slander.

### Section V.

Determination of the type of release from criminal liability Art. 78b. (Repealed - State Gazette, issue 62 of 1997)

Chapter Nine.

EXTINCTION OF CRIMINAL PROSECUTION AND OF THE IMPOSED SENTENCE

#### Art. 79. (1) Criminal prosecution and execution of punishment are excluded:

1. when the perpetrator dies;

2. when the statute of limitations provided by law has expired;

3. when amnesty has been granted.

(2) (Amended - SG, no. 74 from 2015 (*)) Criminal prosecution and execution of punishment are not excluded by statute of limitations with respect to:

- 1. crimes against peace and humanity;
- 2. (declared unconstitutional by Constitutional Court Decision No. 12 from 2016 - SG, no. 83 from 2016) serious crimes under chapter two, section I, section II, section IV and section V; chapter three, section I, section II and section III; chapter eleven, section III of the special part of the Criminal Code, committed in the period from September 9, 1944 to November 10, 1989 by members of governing bodies of the Bulgarian Communist Party, as well as by third parties entrusted with leading official or party functions.

#### Art. 80. (1) Criminal prosecution is excluded by statute of limitations when it has not been initiated within:

1. (amended - SG, no. 31 from 1990, amended - SG, no. 153 from 1998) twenty years for acts punishable by life imprisonment without commutation, life imprisonment, and 35 years for murder of two or more persons;

2. fifteen years for acts punishable by deprivation of liberty of more than ten years;

3. ten years for acts punishable by deprivation of liberty of more than three years;

4. (amended - SG, no. 62 from 1997) five years for acts punishable by deprivation of liberty of more than one year, and

5. (amended - SG, no. 26 from 2010) three years for all other cases.

(2) The limitation periods under the preceding paragraph for crimes committed by minors are determined after taking into account the replacement of punishments under Art. 63.

(3) The statute of limitations for prosecution begins from the completion of the crime, in case of attempt and preparation - from the day when the last act was committed, and for crimes that continue uninterruptedly, as well as for continuing crimes - from their termination.

#### Art. 81.

(1) The statute of limitations is suspended when the commencement or continuation of criminal prosecution depends on the resolution of some preliminary question by a judicial act that has entered into force.

(2) The statute of limitations is interrupted by any action of the competent authorities undertaken for prosecution, and only with respect to the person against whom the prosecution is directed. After completion of the action by which the statute of limitations is interrupted, a new limitation period begins to run.

(3) Regardless of the suspension or interruption of the statute of limitations, criminal prosecution is excluded if a period has elapsed that exceeds by one half the period provided for in the preceding article.

#### Art. 82. (1) The imposed punishment is not executed when the following have elapsed:

1. (amended - SG, no. 153 from 1998) twenty years, if the punishment is life imprisonment without commutation or life imprisonment;

2. fifteen years, if the punishment is deprivation of liberty of more than ten years;

3. ten years, if the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to ten years;

4. five years, if the punishment is deprivation of liberty of less than three years, and

5. two years for all other cases.

(2) The statute of limitations for execution of punishment begins to run from the day when the sentence has entered into force, and with respect to punishment whose execution has been postponed under Art. 66 - from the entry into force of the sentence or the ruling under Art. 68.

(3) The statute of limitations is interrupted by any action of the competent authorities undertaken against the convicted person for execution of the sentence. After completion of the action by which the statute of limitations is interrupted, a new limitation period begins to run.

(4) Regardless of the suspension or interruption of the statute of limitations, the punishment is not executed if a period has elapsed that exceeds by one half the period provided for in paragraph 1.

(5) (New - SG, no. 28 from 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) The provision of the preceding paragraph does not apply with respect to a fine when enforcement proceedings have been instituted for its collection.

#### Art. 83.

Amnesty erases the criminal character of a certain type of committed acts or releases from criminal liability and from the consequences of conviction for certain crimes.

#### Art. 84.

(1) For crimes that are prosecuted upon complaint by the victim, criminal prosecution is not initiated, even though the statute of limitations has not expired, if no complaint is filed within a six-month period from the day when the victim learns that the crime has been committed.

(2) If the victim dies before that period has elapsed, the complaint may be filed by his heirs until its expiration.

(3) For these crimes, the punishment is not executed if the complainant has requested this before its execution begins. Chapter ten.

REHABILITATION

#### Art. 85.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 85 - SG, no. 28 from 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) Rehabilitation erases the conviction and abolishes for the future the consequences that the laws associate with the conviction itself, unless in some respect the contrary is established by law or decree.

(2) (New - SG, no. 28 from 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) The provision of the preceding paragraph does not apply with respect to persons convicted of crimes against peace and humanity.

#### Art. 86. (1) Rehabilitation occurs by operation of law in the following cases:

1. when a person has been sentenced conditionally, if during the probationary period he has not committed another crime for which he should serve the postponed punishment;

2. (amended - SG, no. 92 from 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, no. 26 from 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, no. 103 from 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) when sentenced to deprivation of liberty of up to three years or to probation, if within three years from the expiration of the term of the punishment imposed by the sentence or reduced by work or pardon, no other crime punishable by deprivation of liberty or a more severe punishment has been committed;

3. when sentenced jointly or separately to a fine, public reprimand or deprivation of rights, if within one year from the execution of the punishment, no other crime of a general character has been committed, and

4. when sentenced as a minor, if within two years from serving the punishment, no other crime of a general character has been committed for which a punishment of deprivation of liberty has been imposed.

(2) (Amended - SG, no. 28 from 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) Rehabilitation by operation of law does not occur for a crime committed by an adult who has been rehabilitated once.

#### Art. 87.

(1) Outside of the cases under the preceding article, any convicted person may be rehabilitated by the court that issued the sentence as a first instance, if within three years from the expiration of the term of the punishment imposed by the sentence or reduced by work or pardon, no other crime punishable by deprivation of liberty or a more severe punishment has been committed:

- 1. if he has had good conduct and
- 2. if in case of an intentional crime he has restored the damages caused.
  - (2) The court may rehabilitate the convicted person even without having restored the damages caused, if there are valid reasons for this.
  - (3) (Amended - SG, no. 92 from 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, no. 26 from 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, no. 103 from 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) When along with the punishment of deprivation of liberty, a punishment of deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, or probation has also been imposed, in order for rehabilitation to be granted, the term of that punishment must have elapsed. When a fine has been imposed, it must be paid.

#### Чл. 88.

Rehabilitation may also be requested by the heirs of the convicted person after his death, if he had the right to it.

Чл. 88а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г.) (1) (Доп. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г.) When a period equal to that under Art. 82, para. 1 has elapsed since the serving of the sentence and the convicted person has not committed a new intentional crime of general nature for which imprisonment is provided, the conviction and its consequences shall be expunged regardless of what is provided in another law or decree.

(2) When the imposed sentence of imprisonment is more than one year and the person has not been released from serving it under Art. 66, the period under para. 1 may not be less than ten years.

(3) In case of conditional conviction and conditional early release, the period under para. 1 begins to run from the day on which the probationary period has expired.

(4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г.) When the person has committed two or more crimes for which he has not been rehabilitated, the conviction and its consequences shall be expunged after the expiration of the periods provided for in the preceding paragraphs for all convictions.

(5) (Предишна ал. 4 - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г.) The provisions of the preceding paragraphs shall not apply to persons convicted of serious crimes against the Republic and of crimes against peace and humanity.

Глава единадесета.

COMPULSORY MEDICAL MEASURES

#### Чл. 89.

With respect to a person who has committed a socially dangerous act in a state of insanity or who has fallen into such a state before the sentence is pronounced or during the serving of the sentence, the court may order: a) placement with relatives, if they undertake an obligation for his treatment under the supervision of a psycho-neurological dispensary; б) compulsory treatment in an ordinary psycho-neurological institution; в) compulsory treatment in a special psychiatric hospital or in a special ward in an ordinary psycho-neurological institution.

#### Чл. 90.

(1) Compulsory treatment in an ordinary psycho-neurological institution may be ordered by the court with respect to a mentally ill person who, in view of his mental condition and the nature of the socially dangerous act committed, needs hospital care and treatment by compulsory order.

(2) Compulsory treatment in a special psychiatric hospital or in a special ward may be ordered by the court with respect to a mentally ill person who, in view of his mental condition and the nature of the socially dangerous act committed, appears particularly dangerous to society or to his relatives. In these cases, the person shall be kept under enhanced supervision, excluding the possibility of him committing a new socially dangerous act.

#### Чл. 91.

(1) The termination and modification of the imposed compulsory medical measure shall be made by the court when this is required by an occurred change in the condition of the patient or by the needs of his treatment.

(2) In all cases, after the expiration of a six-month period from the placement in the medical institution, the court shall rule on the termination, continuation or replacement of the compulsory treatment.

#### Чл. 92.

(1) When the crime has been committed by a person suffering from alcoholism or other drug addiction, the court may, together with the sentence, also order compulsory treatment.

(2) When a sentence without imprisonment has been imposed, the compulsory treatment shall be carried out in medical institutions with a special treatment and labor regime.

(3) The compulsory treatment of persons convicted to imprisonment shall be carried out during the execution of the sentence. The period of compulsory treatment shall be deducted from the period of imprisonment.

(4) When necessary, the court may order that the treatment continue even after the release of the convicted person from the place of imprisonment - in the medical institutions specified in paragraph two.

(5) Compulsory treatment shall be terminated by the court when its continuation is no longer necessary.

Допълнителна разпоредба

Explanation of certain words

#### Art. 93. The words and expressions indicated below are used in this code in the following meaning:

1. "Official" is one who is entrusted to perform with remuneration or without remuneration, temporarily or permanently: a) service in a state institution with the exception of those performing only material execution activities; b) (amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 62 of 1997, suppl. - SG, issue 43 of 2005, effective from 01.09.2005, amend. - SG, issue 64 of 2007, amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) managerial work or work related to guarding or managing another's property in a state enterprise, cooperative, public organization, other legal entity or with a sole trader, as well as a notary and assistant notary, private bailiff and assistant private bailiff.

2. (amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002) "Authority" are the bodies of state power, the bodies of state administration, the bodies of judicial power, as well as the employees thereof who are charged with the exercise of authoritative functions.

3. "Public representative" is a person designated by a public organization to exercise on the basis of law or another normative act a specific function.

4. (suppl. - SG, issue 51 of 2000) "Public property" is the property of the state, of the municipalities, of the cooperatives, of the public organizations and of other legal entities in which they participate.

5. "Official document" is one that is issued in the established procedure and form by an official in the scope of their service or by a public representative in the scope of the function entrusted to them.

6. "False document" is one that has been given the appearance of representing a specific written statement of another person, and not of the one who actually drafted it.

7. (suppl. - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amend. - SG, issue 153 of 1998) "Serious crime" is one for which the law provides a punishment of deprivation of liberty of more than five years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without substitution.

8. "Particularly serious case" is one in which the crime committed, in view of the harmful consequences that have occurred and other aggravating circumstances, reveals an exceptionally high degree of public danger of the act and the perpetrator.

9. "Minor case" is one in which the crime committed, in view of the absence or insignificance of the harmful consequences or in view of other mitigating circumstances, represents a lower degree of public danger compared to ordinary cases of crime of the respective type.

10. "Close relatives" are spouses, ascendants, descendants (including adopted, step and pre-existing), brothers, sisters and their spouses, relatives in the collateral line up to degree IV.
