11. "Wartime" is the time from the declaration of war or from the actual commencement of military actions until the announcement of their termination.

12. (new - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) A crime is committed "by two or more persons" when at least two persons participated in the actual execution.

13. (new - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) International protection is enjoyed by persons for whom such protection is provided in an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party.

14. (new - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, issue 21 of 2000) "Taxes in large amounts" are those exceeding three thousand leva, and "taxes in particularly large amounts" are those exceeding twelve thousand leva.

15. (new - SG, issue 7 of 1999) "Foreign official" is one who performs: a) service in an institution of a foreign state; b) functions entrusted by a foreign state, including by a foreign state enterprise or organization; c) (suppl. - SG, issue 92 of 2002) service or commission entrusted by an international organization, as well as service in an international parliamentary assembly or international court; d) (new - SG, issue 74 of 2015) functions of an arbitrator entrusted in accordance with the law of a foreign state.

16. (new - SG, issue 21 of 2000, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006)

17. (new - SG, issue 21 of 2000, suppl. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006)

18. (new - SG, issue 21 of 2000, suppl. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006)

19. (new - SG, issue 21 of 2000, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006)

20. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) "Organized criminal group" is a structured permanent association of three or more persons for the purpose of committing in a coordinated manner in the country or abroad crimes for which a punishment of deprivation of liberty of more than three years is provided. The association is structured even without the presence of a formal distribution of functions among the participants, duration of participation or developed structure.

21. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amend. and suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Information system" is any individual device or set of interconnected or similar devices which, in execution of a specific program, provides or one of the elements of which provides automatic data processing, as well as the computer data stored, processed, retrieved or transmitted by such device or group of devices for the purpose of operating with this data and its use, protection and maintenance.

22. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Computer data" is any representation of facts, information or concepts in a form amenable to processing in information systems, including a program that is capable of causing a given information system to perform a specific function.

23. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Provider of computer-information services" is any legal or natural person that offers the possibility of communication through an information system or that processes or stores computer data for this communication service or for its users.

24. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, suppl. - SG, issue 84 of 2023) "Payment instrument" is a tangible or intangible means that allows, independently or in connection with another means, the transfer of money or monetary value.

25. (new - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Computer network" is a set of interconnected information systems or facilities that enables the exchange of computer data.

26. (new - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Computer program" is a series of machine instructions that are capable of causing an information system to perform specific functions.

27. (new - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Computer virus" is a computer program that spreads automatically and against the will or without the knowledge of persons using information systems and is intended to bring information systems or computer networks into states undesired by their users or to the realization of undesired results.

28. (new - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, issue 74 of 2015) "Pornographic material" is material prepared in any manner, indecent, unacceptable or incompatible with public morality, the content of which depicts real or simulated lewd act, copulation, sexual intercourse, including sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism, as well as lascivious display of the sexual organs of a person.

29. (new - SG, issue 107 of 2014, effective from 01.01.2015) "Mandatory insurance contributions for state social insurance or for health insurance in large amounts" are those exceeding three thousand leva, and "mandatory insurance contributions for state social insurance or for health insurance in particularly large amounts" are those exceeding twelve thousand leva.

30. (new - SG, issue 74 of 2015) "Pornographic performance" is a live or real-time presentation before another of a lascivious display of the sexual organs of a person who has not reached 18 years of age, or of the participation of such person in real or simulated lewd act, copulation, sexual intercourse, including sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism.

31. (new - SG, issue 16 of 2019, amend. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) A crime is committed "in conditions of domestic violence" if it is committed through the exercise of physical, sexual or psychological violence, placing in economic dependence, forced restriction of personal life, personal freedom and personal rights and is perpetrated against an ascendant, descendant, spouse or former spouse, person with whom there is a child, person with whom one is or has been in actual marital cohabitation, or person with whom one lives or has lived in one household.

32. (new - SG, issue 84 of 2023) "Financial or other means for financing terrorism" are any assets, financial, economic resources, tangible or intangible benefit, movable or immovable property, regardless of the manner of acquisition, documents or instruments, including in electronic or digital form, proving right of ownership or interest, funds, bank credits, traveler's checks, bank checks, money transfers, shares, securities, bonds, bills of exchange or letters of credit, interest, dividends or other income or value accrued or generated from such funds that can potentially be used to obtain funds, goods or services.

Special provision

#### Art. 94.

The provisions of the general part of this code shall also apply to crimes provided for in other laws.

SPECIAL PART

Chapter One.

CRIMES AGAINST THE REPUBLIC

### Section I.

Treason

#### Art. 95.

(Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) Whoever with the purpose of overthrowing, undermining or weakening the power in the republic participates in carrying out an attempt at a coup for the violent seizure of power in the center or in localities, or in a mutiny, or in an armed uprising, shall be punished by imprisonment from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Art. 96.

(Amended - SG, No. 41 of 1985) (1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) Whoever with the purpose of undermining or weakening the power in the republic or creating difficulties for it deprives of life a state or public figure shall be punished by imprisonment of twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.

(2) Whoever with the same purpose causes grievous bodily harm to such a person shall be punished by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(3) (Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) Whoever with the purpose under para. 1 through arson, explosion, flooding or other generally dangerous act causes the death of one or more persons shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, by life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Art. 97.

(Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) Whoever with the purpose specified in the preceding article commits a generally dangerous crime under Art. 349 or 350 shall be punished by imprisonment from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.

Art. 97a. (New - SG, No. 41 of 1985) (1) Whoever with the purpose under Art. 96 holds someone as a hostage, whose release is made conditional upon the fulfillment of a specified condition by the state, by a state or public organization or by a third party, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) When in the cases under the preceding paragraph the perpetrator threatens that if the condition set by him is not fulfilled, he will cause death or grievous or moderate bodily harm to the detainee, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

### Section II.

Betrayal and Espionage

#### Art. 98.

(1) Whoever incites a foreign state or public group abroad to war or other hostile action against the Republic shall be punished by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on one who commits an act with the purpose of provoking war or other hostile action against the Republic.

#### Art. 99.

(1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) Whoever deprives of life a representative of a foreign state with the purpose of provoking war or international complications against the republic shall be punished by imprisonment from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.

(2) For grievous bodily harm to such a person with the same purpose, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

#### Art. 100.

(1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) A Bulgarian citizen who, during a declared or commenced war, voluntarily enlists in the ranks of an enemy army or armed group or participates in a hostile action against the Republic, or in any form goes over to the side of the enemy, shall be punished by imprisonment from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.

(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on a Bulgarian citizen who in any manner assists a foreign state or public group abroad in conducting military or other hostile actions against the Republic.

#### Art. 101.

(1) A Bulgarian citizen who leaves the country or refuses to return to the country with the purpose of placing himself in the service of a foreign state or foreign organization, in order to serve it to the detriment of the republic, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) If the act is committed by a serviceman, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

#### Art. 102.

(1) Whoever with the purpose of reducing the defensive capability of the republic provokes mutiny or insubordination in the Bulgarian army or flight from it, or with the same purpose disrupts its preparation or supply, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) (Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) If grave consequences have resulted from the act or if it has been committed during wartime, the punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Art. 103.

(Supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever, performing state service or a mission before a foreign government or international organization, deliberately conducts them to the detriment of the republic, shall be punished by imprisonment from ten to fifteen years, as well as by deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 9.

#### Art. 104.

(1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Whoever discloses or collects with the purpose of disclosing to a foreign state or foreign organization information constituting a state secret shall be punished for espionage by imprisonment from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.

(2) If the perpetrator voluntarily discloses to the authorities the committed crime, he shall be punished under mitigating circumstances.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 99 of 1989, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Information constituting a state secret shall be determined by law.

#### Art. 105.

(1) Whoever places himself in the service of a foreign state or foreign organization, in order to serve it as a spy, if he has not committed an act under the preceding article, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) The perpetrator shall not be punished if he voluntarily reveals himself to the authorities.

### Section III.

Sabotage and Wrecking

#### Art. 106.

(Supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) Whoever with the purpose of weakening the power or creating difficulties for it destroys or damages public buildings, structures, installations, facilities, means of transport or communication or other significant public property, shall be punished for sabotage by imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and in particularly grave cases - by imprisonment of twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Art. 107.

Whoever with the purpose of weakening the power or creating difficulties for it disrupts or undermines industry, transport, agriculture, the monetary and credit system, other economic sectors or individual economic enterprises, by using state institutions, economic enterprises or public organizations, by obstructing their activity, or by failing to fulfill important economic tasks entrusted to him, shall be punished for wrecking by imprisonment from three to ten years, and in particularly grave cases - by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

### Section IV.

Other Crimes

#### Art. 108.

(Amended - SG, issue 41 of 1985, amended - SG, issue 99 of 1989) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 38 of 2007) Whoever preaches fascist or other anti-democratic ideology or violent change of the social and state order established by the Constitution of the Republic of Bulgaria, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or by a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 38 of 2007, supplemented - SG, issue 95 of 2016) Whoever in any manner desecrates the coat of arms, flag or anthem of the Republic of Bulgaria, or the flag or anthem of the European Union, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or by a fine of up to three thousand leva.

Art. 108a. (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, supplemented - SG, issue 74 of 2015, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017, supplemented - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever with the purpose to create turmoil and fear in the population or to threaten or coerce an organ of authority, a public representative or a representative of a foreign state or international organization to do or omit something or to cause serious destabilization or destruction of fundamental political, constitutional, economic or social structures of the state or international organization, commits a crime under Art. 115, Art. 116, para. 1, item 1, Art. 128, Art. 142, Art. 142a, para. 3, item 2, Art. 143, Art. 143a, Art. 144, para. 2, Art. 170, para. 3, Art. 216, para. 1 and 5, Art. 216a, para. 2 or 3, Art. 319b - 319g, Art. 326, Art. 330, Art. 333, Art. 334, Art. 336a, Art. 337, Art. 339, Art. 340, Art. 341a, Art. 341b, Art. 341v, Art. 344, Art. 347, para. 1, Art. 348, Art. 349, Art. 350, Art. 352, para. 1, 2 and 3, Art. 354, Art. 356e, Art. 356z, Art. 356k, Art. 356l and Art. 356m, shall be punished for terrorism by imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and when death is caused - by imprisonment from fifteen to thirty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever in any manner, directly or indirectly collects or provides financial or other means, knowing or supposing that they will be used wholly or partially:

- 1. (supplemented - SG, issue 101 of 2017, amended - SG, issue 84 of 2023) for the commission or preparation for the commission of crimes under para. 1, 3, 4 and para. 6 - 8 or Art. 111;
- 2. by an organization or group that has set as its goal the commission of an act under para. 1 or 3;
- 3. (supplemented - SG, issue 101 of 2017, amended - SG, issue 84 of 2023) by a person who has participated in the commission or preparation of crimes under para. 1, 3, 4 and para. 6 - 8 or Art. 111, (supplemented - SG, issue 84 of 2023) shall be punished by imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from five thousand to thirty thousand leva.
  - (3) (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amended - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever recruits or trains individuals or groups of persons with the purpose to participate in the commission or preparation for a crime under para. 1 or under Art. 109, or in any manner to assist another person or group of persons to commit such acts, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to ten years.
  - (4) (New - SG, issue 74 of 2015, amended - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever receives training with the purpose under para. 3, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to eight years.
  - (5) (New - SG, issue 74 of 2015) The perpetrator under para. 4 shall not be punished if he voluntarily discloses himself to the authorities before a crime under para. 1 has been committed.
  - (6) (New - SG, issue 74 of 2015, amended - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever crosses the border of the country with the purpose to participate in the commission or preparation for a crime under para. 1 - 4, under Art. 109, Art. 111, para. 1, or in any manner to assist another person or group of persons to commit such acts, including against another state, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to ten years.
  - (7) (New - SG, issue 74 of 2015, amended - SG, issue 84 of 2023) The punishment under para. 6 shall also be imposed on a foreigner who illegally resides in the country with the purpose under para. 6.
  - (8) (New - SG, issue 84 of 2023) The punishment under para. 6 shall also be imposed on the one who organizes or facilitates travel for the purpose of terrorism of the persons under para. 6 or 7.
  - (9) (Former para. 3 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, former para. 4 - SG, issue 74 of 2015, former para. 8 - SG, issue 84 of 2023) The object of the crime under para. 2 shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

#### Art. 109.

(1) (Amended - SG, issue 99 of 1989, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever forms or leads an organization or group that has set as its goal to commit crimes under this chapter, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to twelve years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever is a member of such organization or group, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to ten years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.

(3) (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) When the organization or group has set as its goal to commit a crime under Art. 108a, the punishment is:

- 1. under para. 1 - imprisonment from ten to twenty years;
- 2. under para. 2 - imprisonment from two to ten years.
  - (4) (Amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, former para. 3 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) A participant in the organization or group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities, discloses everything that is known to him about the organization or group and thereby substantially facilitates the discovery and proving of crimes committed by it under this chapter, shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55.
  - (5) (Amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, former para. 4 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) A participant in the organization or group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities and discloses the organization or group before another crime under this chapter has been committed by it or by him, shall not be punished.

#### Art. 110.

(1) (Amended - SG, issue 99 of 1989, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, former text of Art. 110 - SG, issue 74 of 2015, supplemented - SG, issue 101 of 2017, amended - SG, issue 84 of 2023) For preparation for a crime under Art. 95, 96, 99, 106, 107, Art. 108a, para. 1, 6 - 8 and Art. 111, para. 1, the punishment is imprisonment of up to six years.

(2) (New - SG, issue 74 of 2015) A foreigner who on the territory of the country prepares to commit abroad a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years, but not more than the punishment provided in Art. 108a, para. 1.

#### Art. 111.

(Repealed - SG, issue 99 of 1989, new - SG, issue 84 of 2023) (1) Whoever threatens to commit a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1 - 4 and 6 - 8 and this threat could cause reasonable apprehension for its realization, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to four years.

(2) If the act under para. 1 is committed by a leader of an organization or group under Art. 109, para. 1, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) Whoever publicly incites to commit a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1 - 4 and 6 - 8 through preaching before a multitude of people, through dissemination of printed works or in another manner, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to ten years, but with no heavier punishment than that provided for the crime itself.

#### Art. 112.

(Repealed - SG, issue 99 of 1989)

Art. 112a. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985, repealed - SG, issue 99 of 1989)

#### Art. 113.

(Repealed - SG, issue 99 of 1989)

Supplementary Provisions


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations | > Section IV.

#### Art. 114.

(1) (Amd. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amd. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) For crimes under this chapter, the court may impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 - 10.

(2) (Suppl. - SG, issue 92 of 2002) For crimes under Art. 95 - 107, Art. 108a and 109, the court may order confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.

Chapter Two.

CRIMES AGAINST THE PERSON

### Section I.

Murder

#### Art. 115.

Whoever intentionally kills another shall be punished for murder by imprisonment from ten to twenty years.

#### Art. 116.

(Amd. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, suppl. - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) For murder:

- 1. of an official, of a representative of the public, as well as of a military person, including from an allied or friendly state or armed forces, during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function, or of a person enjoying international protection;
- 2. (amd. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) by an official, as well as by a representative of the public, by a police officer during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function;
- 3. of a father or mother, as well as of a biological son or biological daughter;
- 4. (suppl. - SG, issue 62 of 1997) of a pregnant woman, of a minor or of more than one person;
- 5. of a person who is in a helpless state;
- 6. by means or methods dangerous to the life of many, in a particularly torturous manner for the murdered person or with particular cruelty;

6a. (new - SG, issue 16 of 2019) committed in conditions of domestic violence;

7. with mercenary purpose;

8. with the purpose of facilitating or concealing another crime;

8a. (new - SG, issue 84 of 2013) with the purpose of removing a bodily organ, tissue, cell or bodily fluid from the victim;

9. committed with premeditation;

10. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002) committed by a person acting on order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;

11. (previous item 10 - SG, issue 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, amd. and suppl. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) committed on hooligan, racist, xenophobic or sexual orientation-related motives and

12. (amd. - SG, issue 153 of 1998, previous item 11, amd. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004) constituting dangerous recidivism or committed by a person who has committed another intentional murder under the previous or present article, for which no sentence has been imposed, (suppl. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amd. - SG, issue 153 of 1998, amd. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amd. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) the punishment is imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

(2) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amd. - SG, issue 153 of 1998, amd. and suppl. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, suppl. - SG, issue 43 of 2005, effective from 01.09.2005, amd. - SG, issue 64 of 2007, amd. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amd. - SG, issue 80 of 2009, amd. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, issue 61 of 2013) For murder of a judge, prosecutor, investigator, police officer, investigating police officer, state bailiff, private bailiff and assistant private bailiff, as well as of a customs officer, a revenue officer, an employee of the Executive Forest Agency or an employee of the Ministry of Environment and Water exercising control activity, or of a medical specialist, a teacher (educator) during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function, the punishment is imprisonment from twenty to thirty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Art. 117.

(1) (Amd. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Preparation for murder shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on one who incites another to murder.

#### Art. 118.

(Amd. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) For murder committed in a state of strong irritation, which was provoked by the victim by violence, serious insult or slander, or by another unlawful act, from which serious consequences for the perpetrator or his relatives have occurred or could have occurred, the punishment is: in the cases of Art. 115 - imprisonment from one to eight years, and in the cases of Art. 116, para. 1, items 1 - 6 - imprisonment from three to ten years.

#### Art. 119.

For murder committed while exceeding the limits of necessary defense, the punishment is imprisonment of up to five years.

#### Art. 120.

For murder committed by a mother upon her newborn during childbirth or immediately thereafter, the punishment is imprisonment of up to three years.

#### Art. 121.

(Amd. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) For murder of a just-born newborn with a monstrous appearance, the guilty parent shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 122.

(1) (Amd. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever causes death to another by negligence shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.

(2) (Amd. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) If death is caused by firearm or by a strongly acting poisonous substance, or if death is caused to two or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years.

#### Art. 123.

(1) (Amd. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever causes death to another due to ignorance or negligent performance of an occupation or other legally regulated activity constituting a source of increased danger shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) (Amd. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever by negligence causes death to another through actions that fall within an occupation or activity under the previous paragraph, which he has no right to practice, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) (Amd. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) If in the cases under the previous paragraphs the perpetrator was in a drunken state or if death has been caused to more than one person, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years, and in particularly serious cases - imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(4) If the perpetrator after the act has done everything in his power to save the victim, the punishment is: under para. 1 and 2 - imprisonment of up to three years; under para. 3 - imprisonment of up to five years, and in particularly serious cases - imprisonment from three to ten years.

#### Art. 124.

(1) Whoever causes the death of another by negligence as a result of intentionally inflicted bodily injury shall be punished by imprisonment from three to twelve years for severe bodily injury, from two to eight years for medium bodily injury and up to five years for minor bodily injury.

(2) (New - SG No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, amended - SG No. 89 of 1986) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed in a state of strong irritation, which is provoked by the injured person through violence, serious insult or slander, or through another unlawful act from which serious consequences have occurred or could have occurred for the perpetrator or his relatives, the punishment is: for severe bodily injury - imprisonment up to five years; for medium bodily injury - imprisonment up to three years; for minor bodily injury - imprisonment up to two years.

(3) (New para. 3 - SG No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) If the bodily injury from which death resulted is a dangerous recidivism, the punishment is: for severe bodily injury - imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and for medium bodily injury - imprisonment from three to ten years.

(4) (New - SG No. 89 of 1986) When the act under para. 1 or 3 is committed when exceeding the limits of necessary defense, the punishment is: for severe bodily injury - imprisonment up to five years, for medium bodily injury - imprisonment up to four years, and for minor bodily injury - imprisonment up to two years.

#### Art. 125.

A mother who by negligence causes the death of her unborn or newly born child shall not be punished.

#### Art. 126.

(1) (Amended - SG No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever with the consent of a pregnant woman kills the fetus outside an accredited medical establishment or in violation of the approved medical standards and rules of good medical practice, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years.

(2) (Amended - SG No. 62 of 1997) If the perpetrator does not have a higher medical education or has killed the fetus of two or more women, the punishment is imprisonment up to eight years.

(3) (Amended - SG No. 62 of 1997) If the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.

(4) The pregnant woman does not bear criminal liability under the preceding paragraphs, including for instigation and aiding.

(5) (Amended - SG No. 62 of 1997) If the killing of the fetus is committed without the consent of the pregnant woman, the punishment is imprisonment from three to eight years.

(6) (Amended - SG No. 62 of 1997) If in the latter case the death of the pregnant woman has resulted, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twelve years.

#### Art. 127.

(1) (Amended - SG No. 26 of 2010) Whoever in any manner aids or incites another to suicide and such or only an attempt follows, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) For the same crime committed against a minor or against a person whom the perpetrator knows to be incapable of directing his actions or not understanding the nature or significance of what is done, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.

(3) Whoever through cruel treatment or systematic humiliation of the dignity of a person in material or other dependence on him brings him to suicide or to an attempt at suicide, having allowed this, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(4) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed by negligence, the punishment is imprisonment up to three years.

### Section II.

Bodily injury

#### Art. 128.

(1) Whoever causes another severe bodily injury shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) Bodily injury is severe if it has caused: prolonged disturbance of consciousness; permanent blindness in one or both eyes; permanent deafness; loss of speech; reproductive incapacity; disfigurement causing permanent disturbance of speech or a sensory organ; loss of one kidney, the spleen or a wing of the lung; loss or mutilation of a leg or arm; permanent general disturbance of health dangerous to life.

#### Art. 129.

(1) (Amended - SG No. 26 of 2010) Whoever causes another medium bodily injury shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years.

(2) Bodily injury is medium if it has caused: permanent weakening of vision or hearing; permanent impediment to speech, to movement of the limbs, the neck or the waist, to the functions of the sexual organs without causing reproductive incapacity; fracture of the jaw or knocking out of teeth without which chewing or speaking is impeded; disfigurement of the face or other parts of the body; permanent disturbance of health not dangerous to life, or disturbance of health temporarily dangerous to life; injuries which penetrate the cranial, thoracic and abdominal cavity.

#### Art. 130.

(1) (Amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever causes another disturbance of health, outside the cases of Art. 128 and 129, shall be punished for minor bodily injury by imprisonment up to two years or by probation.

(2) (Amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, corrected - SG No. 31 of 1982, amended - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) For minor bodily injury consisting in causing pain or suffering without disturbance of health, the punishment is imprisonment up to six months or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(3) If in the cases of the preceding paragraphs the injured person immediately returned to the perpetrator with the same bodily injury, the court may release both from punishment.

#### Art. 131. (1) For causing bodily injury:

1. (supplemented - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997) to an official, to a representative of the public, as well as to a military person, including from an allied or friendly state or army, in connection with or on the occasion of the performance of their service or function; or to a person enjoying international protection;

2. (amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) by an official, by a representative of the public, by a police authority in connection with or on the occasion of the performance of their service or function;

3. to a mother or to a father;

4. (supplemented - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997) to a pregnant woman, to a minor, or to more than one person;

5. (amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975) in a manner particularly tormenting to the victim;

5a. (new - SG, No. 16 of 2019) in conditions of domestic violence;

6. (new - SG, No. 95 of 1975) by a person who has intentionally committed another serious or moderate bodily injury under Art. 128 and 129 or under this article, for which no sentence has been imposed;

7. (new - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) repeatedly, if the bodily injury is serious or moderate;

8. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) by a person acting on order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group; 8a. (new - SG, No. 84 of 2013) with the purpose of removing a bodily organ, tissue, cell or bodily fluid from the victim;

9. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) in a manner or by means dangerous to the life of many or with particular cruelty;

10. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) with a selfish purpose;

11. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) with the purpose of facilitating or concealing another crime;

12. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 67 of 2023) on hooligan, racist, xenophobic or sexual orientation-related motives.

(New formulation - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years for serious bodily injury; from two to ten years for moderate bodily injury; up to three years for minor bodily injury under Art. 130, para. 1 and up to one year or probation under Art. 130, para. 2;

(2) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, supplemented - SG, No. 43 of 2005, effective from 01.09.2005, amended - SG, No. 64 of 2007, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 80 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, supplemented - SG, No. 61 of 2013) For causing bodily injury to a judge, prosecutor, investigator, police authority, investigating policeman, state bailiff, private bailiff and assistant private bailiff, as well as to a customs officer, to a revenue authority, to an employee of the Executive Forest Agency or to an employee of the Ministry of Environment and Water performing control activity or to a medical specialist, to a teacher (educator) in connection with or on the occasion of the performance of their service or function, the punishment is imprisonment:

- 1. from five to fifteen years for serious bodily injury;
- 2. from three to ten years for moderate bodily injury;
- 3. from one to five years for minor bodily injury under Art. 130, para. 1;
- 4. up to three years for minor bodily injury under Art. 130, para. 2.

Art. 131a. (Previous para. 2 of Art. 131 - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) In cases of dangerous recidivism, the punishment is: for serious bodily injury imprisonment from eight to fifteen years, and for moderate bodily injury imprisonment from five to twelve years.

#### Art. 132.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986) For bodily injury, except in the cases of Art. 131a, caused to another in a state of strong irritation provoked by the victim with violence, with grave insult, with slander or with another unlawful act, from which serious consequences have occurred or could have occurred for the perpetrator or their relatives, the punishment is:

- 1. imprisonment up to three years for serious bodily injury;
- 2. imprisonment up to one year for moderate bodily injury;
- 3. (amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) imprisonment three months or probation up to six months for minor bodily injury under Art. 130, para. 1;
- 4. (amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) probation up to six months or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva under Art. 130, para. 2.
  - (2) The punishments under the preceding paragraph are also imposed in cases of causing bodily injury when exceeding the limits of necessary defense.

#### Art. 133.

(Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever causes another person serious or moderate bodily injury by negligence shall be punished with imprisonment up to one year or with probation.

#### Art. 134.

(1) Whoever causes another person serious or moderate bodily injury due to ignorance or due to negligent performance of an occupation or other legally regulated activity that constitutes a source of increased danger shall be punished:

- 1. with imprisonment up to three years for serious bodily injury and
- 2. (amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) with imprisonment up to two years or with probation for moderate bodily injury.
  - (2) (Amended and supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever by negligence causes another person serious or moderate bodily injury through actions that fall within an occupation or activity under the preceding paragraph, which they have no right to exercise, shall be punished for serious bodily injury with imprisonment up to five years, and for moderate bodily injury - with imprisonment up to three years.
  - (3) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) If in the cases of the preceding paragraphs the perpetrator was in a state of intoxication or if injury has been caused to more than one person, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years for serious bodily injury and imprisonment up to five years for moderate bodily injury.
  - (4) If the perpetrator after the act has done everything in their power to provide assistance to the victim, this is taken into account as a mitigating circumstance in determining the punishment.

#### Art. 135.

(1) (Amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 62 of 1997) Whoever, knowing that they suffer from a venereal disease, infects another with the same disease, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to two hundred leva.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 62 of 1997) If in the case of the preceding paragraph the infected persons are minors under 16 years of age or more than two persons have been infected, the punishment is imprisonment of up to five years or a fine of up to five hundred leva.

(3) (Amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 62 of 1997) Whoever, knowing that they suffer from a venereal disease, infects another with the same disease by negligence, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year or a fine of up to two hundred leva.

(4) (Amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 62 of 1997) Whoever, through sexual intercourse or otherwise, places another person in danger of being infected with a venereal disease, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six months or a fine of up to two hundred leva.

(5) (Amd. - SG No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 62 of 1997) A person suffering from a venereal disease who refuses to be treated or evades regular mandatory treatment shall be punished with a fine of up to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.

(6) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment of up to six months.

### Section III.

Endangerment

#### Art. 136.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 136 - SG No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever violates rules established for the protection of labor safety and thereby endangers the life or health of workers shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or probation, as well as public reprimand.

(2) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) When by an act under the preceding paragraph the life or health of workers is endangered by negligence, the punishment is imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 137.

Whoever exposes a person deprived of the ability to protect themselves due to minority, old age, illness, or generally due to their helplessness, in such a way that their life may be in danger, and being aware of this, does not come to their aid, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years.

#### Art. 138.

(Amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever knowingly does not render assistance to a person for whom they are obliged to care and who is in danger for their life and has no ability to protect themselves due to minority, old age, illness, or generally due to their helplessness, in cases where they could have rendered assistance, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 139.

(Amd. - SG No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever, in case of immediate danger to the life of another, does not come to the aid which they could have given without danger to themselves or to another, shall be punished with probation of up to six months or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Art. 140.

(Amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) A driver of a vehicle who, after a traffic accident in which they are involved, does not render the necessary assistance to the injured person, which they could have given without danger to themselves or to another, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 141.

(Amd. - SG No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) A person who practices a medical profession, if, after being invited, does not come to the aid of a sick person or a woman in childbirth without a valid reason, shall be punished with probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) If the offender knew that the sick person or woman in childbirth were in a dangerous situation, the punishment is imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

(3) (Amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever, being obliged to render assistance to a sick person, does not render such assistance without valid reasons, shall be punished with probation of up to six months or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

### Section IV.

Abduction and unlawful deprivation of liberty (Title amd. - SG No. 50 of 1995)

#### Art. 142.

(New - SG, No. 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended and supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever abducts another person shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) The punishment is imprisonment from seven to fifteen years when:

- 1. the perpetrator was armed;
- 2. the act was committed by two or more persons;
- 3. (amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997) the abducted person is a pregnant woman or has not reached 18 years of age;
- 4. the abducted person enjoys international protection;
- 5. the act was committed with respect to two or more persons;

5a. (new - SG, No. 16 of 2019) the act was committed under conditions of domestic violence;

6. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) the act was committed by a person engaged in security activity, by an employee in an organization that performs security or insurance activity, by a person acting on behalf of such organization or representing that they act on such behalf, by a person from the staff of the Ministry of Internal Affairs or a person representing themselves as such;

7. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002) the abduction is for mercenary purpose or with the purpose of the person being taken out of the country's borders;

8. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) the act was committed by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organization or group under Art. 321a or an organized criminal group;

9. (new - SG, No. 67 of 2023) the act was committed on racist, xenophobic or related to sexual orientation motives.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) The punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment, as well as confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person, when:

- 1. the act was committed repeatedly or constitutes dangerous recidivism;
- 2. significant harmful consequences have resulted from the act;
- 3. special cruelty was shown towards the abducted person;
- 4. the act was committed in a particularly painful or dangerous to the health of the abducted manner;
- 5. the release of the abducted person was made conditional upon the fulfillment of a certain condition by a third party.
  - (4) (Repealed - SG, No. 26 of 2010)
  - (5) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2010) For preparation, incitement or conspiracy for the purpose of committing a crime under this article, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years.
  - (6) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2010) In the cases under para. 1 - 5, the perpetrator shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55 if they voluntarily surrender to the authorities, disclose everything known to them about the committed crimes, and thereby substantially facilitate the detection and proof of the crimes.

Art. 142a. (Previous Art. 142 - SG, No. 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully deprives someone of freedom shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) If the act was committed by an official or by a representative of the public in violation of their office or function, or by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 and 2 was committed:

- 1. with respect to a pregnant woman, minor or juvenile;
- 2. with respect to a person enjoying international protection;
- 3. on racist, xenophobic or related to sexual orientation motives, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
  - (4) (New - SG, No. 16 of 2019) If the act under para. 1 was committed under conditions of domestic violence, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
  - (5) (Previous para. 3, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 16 of 2019) If the act under the preceding paragraphs was committed in a manner painful or dangerous to the health of the victim, or if the deprivation of freedom lasted more than two days, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years.
  - (6) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 50 of 1995, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 5 - SG, No. 16 of 2019) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever knowingly places or detains a healthy person in a health facility for the mentally ill.
  - (7) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 50 of 1995, previous para. 6 - SG, No. 16 of 2019)

### Section V.

Coercion

#### Art. 143.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 50 of 1995, previous text of Art. 143 - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever coerces another to commit, omit or endure something against their will by using force, threat or abuse of power shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years.

(2) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) If the act was committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.

(3) (New - SG, No. 16 of 2019, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 was committed:

- 1. under conditions of domestic violence;
- 2. on racist or xenophobic motives, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
  - (4) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, supplemented - SG, No. 43 of 2005, in force from 01.09.2005, amended - SG, No. 64 of 2007, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 80 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, previous para. 3 - SG, No. 16 of 2019) When coercion is exercised in relation to a judge, prosecutor, investigator, police officer, investigating policeman, state bailiff, private bailiff and assistant private bailiff, as well as a customs officer, a revenue authority, an employee of the Executive Forest Agency or an employee of the Ministry of Environment and Water exercising control activity during or on the occasion of the performance of their office or function, the punishment is:
- 1. in the cases under para. 1 - imprisonment from two to eight years;
- 2. in the cases under para. 2 - imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

Art. 143a. (New - SG, No. 41 of 1985) (1) Whoever detains someone as a hostage, whose release they make conditional upon the fulfillment of a certain condition by the state, a state or public organization or a third party, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to eight years.

(2) When in the cases under the preceding paragraph the perpetrator threatens that if the condition set by them is not fulfilled, they will cause death or serious or medium bodily injury to the detainee, the punishment is imprisonment from two to ten years.

(3) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) If the act under the preceding paragraphs was committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8, the punishment is:

- 1. under para. 1 - imprisonment from two to ten years;
- 2. under para. 2 - imprisonment from five to twelve years.

#### Art. 144.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever threatens another with a crime against their person or property or against the person or property of their relatives and this threat could arouse reasonable fear of its realization, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years.

(2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) For a threat against an official or public representative during or in connection with the performance of their service or function, or against a person enjoying international protection, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to five years.

(3) (Am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, suppl. - SG, No. 16 of 2019, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) The punishment shall be imprisonment of up to six years when:

- 1. the perpetrator has threatened with murder;
- 2. the act was committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, items 6 and 8;
- 3. the act was committed in conditions of domestic violence;
- 4. the act was committed with racist or xenophobic motives.

Art. 144a. (New - SG, No. 16 of 2019) (1) Whoever systematically stalks another and this could arouse reasonable fear for their life or health, or for the life or health of their relatives, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

(2) Stalking under para. 1 is any behavior with a threatening character against a specific person, which may be expressed in pursuing the other person, showing the other person that they are being watched, entering into unwanted communication with them through all possible means of communication.

(3) (Suppl. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) If the act was committed in conditions of domestic violence or with racist or xenophobic motives, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to five years.

Art. 144b. (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023) (1) Whoever through force, threat or otherwise tortures another, to make them or another person give information or confession, to be punished for an act which they or another has committed or is suspected of having committed, shall be punished for torture by imprisonment from one to six years, if not subject to more severe punishment.

(2) When the act was committed by an official during or in connection with the performance of their service, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years.

### Section VI.

Disclosure of another's secret

#### Art. 145.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 145, am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever unlawfully discloses another's secret, dangerous to someone's reputation, which has been entrusted to them or has become known to them in connection with their occupation, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever discloses the secret of adoption with the intention to cause harmful consequences to the adopted person, the adopter or their family, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six months or probation, and when serious consequences have resulted from the act - by imprisonment of up to one year.

Art. 145a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) Whoever uses information gathered through the use of special intelligence means, outside its intended purpose for protecting national security or for the purposes of criminal proceedings, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to five hundred leva.

(2) When the act was committed by an official who acquired the information or it became known to them within the scope of their service, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(3) In the cases under the previous paragraph, the court may order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

### Section VII.

Insult and slander

#### Art. 146.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 21 of 2000) Whoever says or does something humiliating to the honor or dignity of another in their presence, shall be punished for insult with a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva. In this case, the court may also impose a punishment of public reprimand.

(2) If the insulted person has responded immediately with an insult, the court may release both from punishment.

#### Art. 147.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 21 of 2000) Whoever spreads a shameful circumstance about another or attributes a crime to them, shall be punished for slander with a fine from three thousand to seven thousand leva and public reprimand.

(2) The perpetrator shall not be punished if the truth of the spread circumstances or of the attributed crimes is proven.

#### Art. 148.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) For insult:

- 1. inflicted publicly;
- 2. (am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) disseminated through mass media or otherwise;
- 3. (rep. - SG, No. 67 of 2023)
- 4. (am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) by an official or public representative during or in connection with the performance of their service or function;
- 5. (new - SG, No. 67 of 2023) inflicted with racist or xenophobic motives, (am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) the punishment shall be a fine from five hundred to ten thousand leva and public reprimand.
  - (2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 21 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) For slander committed under the conditions of the previous paragraph, as well as for slander from which serious consequences have resulted, the punishment shall be a fine from one thousand leva to fifteen thousand leva and public reprimand.
  - (3) In the cases of para. 1, point 1, para. 2 of Art. 146 may find application.

Art. 148a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 21 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever discloses orally, through mass media or otherwise data, circumstances or claims about another, based on unlawfully acquired information from the archive of the Ministry of Internal Affairs, shall be punished with a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.

### Section VIII.

Debauchery

#### Art. 149.

(Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 107 of 1996, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever commits an act with the purpose of arousing or satisfying sexual desire without intercourse with respect to a person who has not attained 14 years of age shall be punished for debauchery by imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 107 of 1996, suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) For debauchery the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years when committed:

- 1. by use of force or threat;
- 2. by bringing the victim into a helpless state;
- 3. by taking advantage of the helpless state of the victim;
- 4. by taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision;
- 5. with respect to a person engaged in prostitution.
  - (3) (Amend. - SG, No. 107 of 1996, amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) When the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
  - (4) (New - SG, No. 107 of 1996) For debauchery the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years:
- 1. if committed by two or more persons;
- 2. (revoked - SG, No. 62 of 1997, new - SG, No. 74 of 2015) if committed with respect to a person who does not understand the nature or significance of what has been done.
- 3. (revoked - SG, No. 62 of 1997)
- 4. (revoked - SG, No. 62 of 1997)
  - (5) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) For debauchery the punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years:
- 1. if debauchery is committed with two or more minors;
- 2. if serious bodily injury is caused or an attempted suicide has followed;
- 3. if it constitutes dangerous recidivism;
- 4. (new - SG, No. 38 of 2007) if it constitutes a particularly serious case.

#### Art. 150.

(1) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 107 of 1996, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, previous text of Art. 150, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever commits an act with the purpose of arousing or satisfying sexual desire without intercourse with respect to a person who has attained 14 years of age, by use of force or threat, by taking advantage of his or her helpless state or by bringing him or her into such a state, or by taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever commits the act under para. 1 with respect to a minor engaged in prostitution.

(3) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 2, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 is committed with respect to a person who does not understand the nature or significance of what has been done, or the act constitutes a particularly serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.

#### Art. 151.

(1) (Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever has intercourse with a person who has not attained 14 years of age, insofar as the act does not constitute an offense under Art. 152, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to six years.

(2) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 is committed:

- 1. by taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision;
- 2. with respect to a person who has not attained 14 years of age and is engaged in prostitution;
- 3. by two or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.
  - (3) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 2 - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 is committed with a minor by taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years.
  - (4) (Previous para. 2 - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 3 - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever has intercourse with a person who has attained 14 years of age and does not understand the nature or significance of what has been done shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years.

#### Art. 152. (1) Whoever has intercourse with a female person:

1. deprived of the possibility of self-defense, and without her consent;

2. by compelling her thereto by force or threat;

3. by bringing her into a helpless state, shall be punished for rape by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) For rape the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years:

- 1. (amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if the raped person has not attained eighteen years of age;
- 2. if she is a descendant relative;
- 3. (new - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) if committed repeatedly.
  - (3) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) For rape the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years:
- 1. if committed by two or more persons;
- 2. if medium bodily injury is caused;
- 3. if an attempted suicide has followed;
- 4. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if committed with the purpose of involving in subsequent depraved acts or prostitution;
- 5. (previous item 4 - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if it constitutes dangerous recidivism.
  - (4) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) For rape the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years:
- 1. if the raped person has not attained fourteen years of age;
- 2. if serious bodily injury is caused;
- 3. if suicide has followed;
- 4. if it constitutes a particularly serious case.

#### Art. 153.

(Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever has intercourse with another person by compelling him or her thereto by taking advantage of his or her official or material dependence on him shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.

#### Art. 154.

Intercourse between ascendants and descendants, between brothers and sisters and between adopters and adoptees shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.

Art. 154a. (1) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous text of Art. 154a, amend. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever commits depraved acts or intercourse with a minor engaged in prostitution shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 is committed repeatedly or by two or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years.


This excerpt sits under: | Конституция | Правилници | > Раздел VIII.

#### Art. 155.

(Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever inclines another person to prostitution or procures them for lewd acts or sexual intercourse shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.

(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever systematically provides premises to various persons for sexual intercourse or for lewd acts shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.

(3) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) When the acts under para. 1 and 2 are committed for profit, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva.

(4) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever inclines or coerces another person to use narcotic substances or their analogues for the purpose of prostitution, sexual intercourse, lewd acts or performing sexual intercourse or acts of sexual gratification with a person of the same sex shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.

(5) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) When the act under para. 1 - 4 is committed:

- 1. by a person acting on commission or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 2. with respect to a person who has not reached 18 years of age, or is insane;
- 3. with respect to two or more persons;
- 4. repeatedly;
- 5. under the conditions of dangerous recidivism, the punishment in the cases under para. 1 and 2 is imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva, in the cases under para. 3 - imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty-five thousand leva, and in the cases under para. 4 - imprisonment from ten to twenty years and a fine from one hundred thousand to three hundred thousand leva.
  - (6) (Previous para. 3 - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, revoked - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006)
  - (7) (Previous para. 4 - SG, No. 62 of 1997, previous para. 5 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, revoked - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005)

Art. 155a. (New - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever through information or communication technology or otherwise provides or collects information about a person who has not reached 18 years of age in order to establish contact with them for the purpose of performing a lewd act, sexual intercourse, sexual relations, prostitution, for creating pornographic material or for participation in a pornographic performance shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever through information or communication technology or otherwise establishes contact with a person who has not reached 14 years of age for the purpose of performing a lewd act, sexual intercourse, sexual relations, for creating pornographic material or for participation in a pornographic performance.

Art. 155b. (1) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous text of Art. 155b, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022, amend. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever inclines a person who has not reached 14 years of age to observe real, virtual or simulated sexual intercourse between persons of the same or different sex or lascivious display of human genitals, sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) (New - SG, No. 53 of 2022, amend. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever inclines a person who has not reached 14 years of age to participate in real, virtual or simulated sexual intercourse between persons of the same or different sex or lascivious display of human genitals, sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism shall be punished with imprisonment from five to ten years.

(3) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, previous para. 2 - SG, No. 53 of 2022) When the act under para. 1 is committed:

- 1. through the use of force or threat;
- 2. by using a position of dependency or supervision;
- 3. by two or more persons who conspired in advance;
- 4. (amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022, amend. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

Art. 155c. (1) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, previous text of Art. 155c, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022, amend. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever through the use of force or threat, or by using a position of dependency or supervision inclines a minor to observe real, virtual or simulated lewd acts, sexual intercourse, sexual relations, including sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism, as well as lascivious display of human genitals, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to seven years.

(2) (New - SG, No. 53 of 2022, amend. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever through the use of force or threat, or by using a position of dependency or supervision inclines a minor to participate in real, virtual or simulated lewd acts, sexual intercourse, sexual relations, including sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism, as well as for lascivious display of human genitals, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.

#### Art. 156.

(Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever abducts another person with the purpose of being provided for depraved acts shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva.

(2) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) The punishment is imprisonment from five to twelve years when:

- 1. the abducted person has not reached 18 years of age;
- 2. the abducted person is provided for depraved acts, or
- 3. the abduction is with the purpose that the person be provided for depraved acts outside the borders of the country.
  - (3) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) The punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva when:
- 1. the act is committed by a person acting on commission or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 2. the abducted person is provided for depraved acts outside the borders of the country;
- 3. the act constitutes dangerous recidivism.

#### Чл. 157.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) (1) Whoever commits sexual intercourse or acts of sexual gratification with a person of the same sex, using force or threat for this purpose or exploiting a position of dependence or supervision, as well as with a person deprived of the ability to defend themselves, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) When the act under para. 1 is committed against a minor engaged in prostitution, the punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) When the act under para. 1 is committed against a person who has not reached 14 years of age, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twelve years.

(4) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever commits sexual intercourse or acts of sexual gratification with a person of the same sex who has not reached 14 years of age shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(5) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) When the act under para. 4 is committed against a person who has not reached 14 years of age and is engaged in prostitution, the punishment is from three to ten years.

(6) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever commits sexual intercourse or acts of sexual gratification with a person of the same sex who has reached 14 years of age but does not understand the nature or significance of what is being done shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(7) When the act under para. 1 - 6 constitutes a particularly serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years.

#### Чл. 158.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.)

Чл. 158а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever in any way recruits, assists or uses a person who has not reached 18 years of age, or a group of such persons, to participate in a pornographic performance shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever coerces a person who has not reached 18 years of age, or a group of such persons, to participate in a pornographic performance shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) When the act under para. 1 or 2 is committed against a person who has not reached 14 years of age, the punishment is imprisonment from three to eight years.

(4) When a property benefit is obtained from the act, the punishment is:

- 1. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) in cases under para. 1 or 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva;
- 2. in cases under para. 3 - imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva.
  - (5) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever watches a pornographic performance in which a person who has not reached 18 years of age participates shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years.

Чл. 158б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) For a crime under чл. 149 - 157 or чл. 158а, the court may also impose deprivation of a right under чл. 37, ал. 1, т. 6 or 7.

#### Чл. 159.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г.) Whoever creates, exhibits, presents, broadcasts, offers, sells, rents or otherwise distributes pornographic material shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.

(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) Whoever distributes pornographic material through information or communication technology or in another similar manner shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.

(3) (Предишна ал. 2, изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 53 от 2022 г.) Whoever exhibits, presents, offers, sells, rents or otherwise distributes pornographic material to a person who has not reached 16 years of age shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years and a fine up to five thousand leva.

(4) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., предишна ал. 3, изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) For the act under para. 1 - 3, the punishment is imprisonment from three to six years and a fine up to ten thousand leva when:

- 1. a person who has not reached 18 years of age, or a person who appears as such, was used to create the pornographic material;
- 2. a person who does not understand the nature or significance of what was done was used to create the pornographic material;
