- 3. it was committed by two or more persons;
- 4. it was committed repeatedly.
  - (5) (Предишна ал. 4, изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) When the act under para. 1 - 4 is committed on commission or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine up to twenty thousand leva, and the court may also order confiscation of part or all of the perpetrator's property.
  - (6) (Предишна ал. 5, изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever possesses or obtains for themselves or for another, through information or communication technology or by other means, pornographic material for the creation of which a person who has not reached 18 years of age was used, or a person who appears as such, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years and a fine up to ten thousand leva.
  - (7) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) The punishment under para. 6 shall also be imposed on whoever, through information or communication technology, knowingly accesses pornographic material for the creation of which a person who has not reached 18 years of age was used, or a person who appears as such.
  - (8) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) In cases under para. 1 - 7, the court may also impose deprivation of a right under чл. 37, ал. 1, т. 6 or 7.
  - (9) (Предишна ал. 6 - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., предишна ал. 7 - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

### Section IX.

Trafficking in Human Beings (New - SG No. 92 of 2002)

Art. 159a. (New - SG No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amended - SG No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG No. 84 of 2013) Whoever recruits, transports, conceals or receives individuals or groups of people for the purpose of being used for depraved acts, for forced labour or for begging, for removal of a bodily organ, tissue, cell or bodily fluid or to be kept in forced subjugation regardless of their consent, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from three thousand to twelve thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG No. 27 of 2009) When the act under para. 1 has been committed:

- 1. against a person who has not attained eighteen years of age;
- 2. by using coercion or by misleading the person;
- 3. by abduction or unlawful deprivation of liberty;
- 4. by using a state of dependence;
- 5. by abuse of power;
- 6. by promising, giving or receiving benefits;
- 7. (new - SG No. 84 of 2013) by an official during or on the occasion of performing his or her duties, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
  - (3) (New - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG No. 27 of 2009) When the act under para. 1 has been committed against a pregnant woman for the purpose of selling the child, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to fifteen years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva.

Art. 159b. (New - SG No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amended - SG No. 27 of 2009) Whoever recruits, transports, conceals or receives individuals or groups of people and transfers them across the border of the country with the purpose under Art. 159a, para. 1, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(2) (Supplemented - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG No. 27 of 2009) If the act under para. 1 has been committed under the conditions of Art. 159a, para. 2 and 3, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twelve years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva.

Art. 159c. (New - SG No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG No. 84 of 2013) Whoever uses a person who has suffered from trafficking in human beings for depraved acts, for forced labour or for begging, for removal of a bodily organ, tissue, cell or bodily fluid or to be kept in forced subjugation regardless of his or her consent, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.

Art. 159d. (New - SG No. 92 of 2002, previous Art. 159c, amended - SG No. 27 of 2009) When the act under Art. 159a - 159c constitutes dangerous recidivism or has been committed by order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva, and the court may also rule confiscation of part or all of the perpetrator's property.

Supplementary Provision

#### Art. 160.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 160 - SG No. 54 of 1978, amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG No. 26 of 2010) For the crimes under Art. 116, para. 1, item 2, 123, 126, Art. 131, para. 1, item 2, 134, Art. 142, para. 2 and 3, the court may rule deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6 or 7.

(2) (New - SG No. 54 of 1978, repealed - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982)

Special Provision

#### Art. 161.

(1) (Amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG No. 21 of 2000, previous text of Art. 161 - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG No. 16 of 2019) For light bodily injury under Art. 130 and Art. 131, para. 1, items 3 - 5, for light and medium bodily injury under Art. 132, for the crimes under Art. 144, para. 1, Art. 145, 146 - 148a, as well as for bodily injury under Art. 132, 133 and 134, inflicted on an ascendant, descendant, spouse, brother or sister, criminal prosecution shall be initiated upon complaint of the injured person.

(2) (New - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 16 of 2019) For bodily injury under Art. 129, inflicted on an ascendant, descendant, spouse, brother or sister, as well as for crimes under Art. 133, Art. 135, para. 1, 3 and 4, Art. 139 - 141 and Art. 144a, para. 1, criminal prosecution of general nature shall be initiated upon complaint of the injured person to the prosecutor's office and may not be discontinued at his or her request.

Chapter Three.

CRIMES AGAINST THE RIGHTS OF CITIZENS

### Section I.

Crimes Against the Equality of Citizens (Title amended - SG No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011)

#### Art. 162.

(1) (Amended - SG No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, supplemented - SG No. 67 of 2023) Whoever by word, print or other means of mass information, through electronic information systems or in any other way preaches or incites discrimination, violence or hatred based on race, colour, origin, nationality or ethnic affiliation or sexual orientation, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to four years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, as well as public reprimand.

(2) (Amended - SG No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, amended and supplemented - SG No. 67 of 2023) Whoever uses violence against another or damages his or her property because of his or her race, colour, origin, nationality, ethnic affiliation, religion, political beliefs or sexual orientation, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to four years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, as well as public reprimand.

(3) (Amended - SG No. 27 of 2009) Whoever forms or leads an organization or group which has as its goal the commission of acts under para. 1 and 2 or systematically permits the commission of such acts, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva, as well as public reprimand.

(4) Whoever is a member of such an organization or group, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years and public reprimand.

(5) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, revoked - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005)

#### Art. 163.

(1) (Supplemented - SG No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG No. 67 of 2023) Persons who participate in a crowd assembled for an attack on groups of the population, individual citizens or their property in connection with their race, colour, origin, nationality, ethnic affiliation or sexual orientation, shall be punished:

- 1. the instigators and ringleaders - by imprisonment of up to five years;
- 2. (amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) all others - by imprisonment of up to one year or probation.
  - (2) If the crowd or some of the participants are armed, the punishment shall be:
- 1. for the instigators and ringleaders - imprisonment from one to six years;
- 2. for all others - imprisonment of up to three years.
  - (3) If an attack has been committed and it has resulted in serious bodily injury or death, the instigators and ringleaders shall be punished by imprisonment from three to fifteen years, and all others - by imprisonment of up to five years, when they are not subject to a more severe punishment.

### Section II.

Crimes Against Religions

#### Art. 164.

(Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) (1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever preaches or incites discrimination, violence or hatred on religious grounds through word, print or other mass media, through electronic information systems or in any other way, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to four years or by probation, as well as by a fine of five thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) (Supplemented - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever desecrates, destroys or damages a religious temple, house of prayer, sanctuary or building adjacent to them, their symbols, graves or tombstones, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or by probation, as well as by a fine of three thousand to ten thousand leva.

(3) (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023) The punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to five years or probation, as well as a fine of five thousand to ten thousand leva, when the act under para. 2 is committed with racist or xenophobic motives.

#### Art. 165.

(1) Whoever by force or threat prevents citizens from freely professing their faith or performing their religious rites and services, which do not violate the laws of the country, public order and good morals, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on anyone who in the same manner compels another to participate in religious rites and services.

(3) For the acts under Art. 163, committed against groups of the population, individual citizens or their property in connection with their religious affiliation, the penalties provided therein shall apply.

#### Art. 166.

(Supplemented - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding the entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) Whoever forms a political organization on a religious basis or whoever through word, print, action or in any other way uses the church or religion for propaganda against the state authority or its measures, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years, unless subject to a more severe punishment.

### Section III.

Crimes against the political rights of citizens (Title amended - SG, No. 1 of 1991)

#### Art. 167.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 1 of 1991, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, previous text of Art. 167 - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever by violence, fraud, threat or other unlawful means prevents someone from exercising their right to vote or from being elected or from voting in a referendum or from participating in a petition for holding a referendum, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to three years and by a fine of one thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever offers or gives to another a property benefit in order to induce them to exercise their right to vote in favor of a particular candidate, political party or coalition or to vote in a referendum in a particular way shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and by a fine of ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(3) (New - SG, No. 85 of 2007, effective from 23.10.2007, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 17 of 2013, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever organizes the offering or giving to another of a property benefit in order to induce them to exercise their right to vote in favor of a particular candidate, political party or coalition or to vote in a referendum in a particular way shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to seven years and by a fine of ten thousand to twenty-five thousand leva.

(4) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed on a person who provides the property benefit to the persons under para. 2 and 3, in order for it to be offered or given to another with the purpose of inducing them to exercise their right to vote in favor of a particular candidate, political party or coalition or to vote in a referendum in a particular way.

(5) (New - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 - 4 is committed by an official in or on the occasion of the performance of their official duties, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from two to seven years and a fine of ten thousand to thirty thousand leva.

(6) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous para. 5, amended - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014) In the cases under para. 2, 3, 4 and 5, the court shall also impose punishment of deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

(7) (New - SG, No. 19 of 2008, previous para. 4, supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous para. 6 - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014) The perpetrator under para. 2 shall not be punished if they voluntarily report to the appropriate authority a committed crime under para. 3 and 4.

Art. 167a. (1) (New - SG, No. 85 of 2007, effective from 23.10.2007, previous text of Art. 167a - SG, No. 19 of 2008, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014, supplemented - SG, No. 88 of 2020) Whoever in order to exercise their right to vote in favor of a particular candidate, political party or coalition or to vote in a referendum in a particular way requests or receives a property benefit, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years and by a fine of one thousand to five thousand leva.

(2) (New - SG, No. 19 of 2008, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) The perpetrator shall not be punished if they voluntarily report to the appropriate authority a committed crime under Art. 167, para. 2, 3 or 4.

#### Art. 168.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, previous text of Art. 168 - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014) Whoever exercises the right to vote or votes in a referendum without having such a right shall be punished by probation for up to six months or by a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014) Whoever exercises their right to vote two or more times in one election, or votes two or more times in one referendum shall be punished by probation and by a fine of five hundred to two thousand leva.

Art. 168a. (New - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective from 05.03.2014) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever in violation of established rules prints ballots for voting shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and by a fine of one thousand to three thousand leva.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on anyone who unlawfully holds or distributes ballots for voting.

#### Чл. 169.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 19 от 2014 г., в сила от 05.03.2014 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) An official, as well as a member of an electoral commission, who violates the secrecy of voting or in any way falsifies a result from elections or a referendum shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years.

Чл. 169а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 1 от 1991 г., изм. бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever by violence, threat or other unlawful means compels someone contrary to their convictions or will to join or leave a political party, organization, movement or coalition with a political purpose shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to three years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

Чл. 169б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 1 от 1991 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever by violence, threat or other unlawful means obstructs someone from exercising their constitutional political rights shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to three years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

Чл. 169в. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 1 от 1991 г.) When the act under Art. 169a and 169b is committed by an official in connection with or on the occasion of performing their official duties, the punishment is deprivation of liberty of up to five years.

Чл. 169г. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) (1) Whoever forms or leads a group which sets itself the goal of committing crimes under this section shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.

(2) Whoever is a member of such a group shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to six years.

(3) A participant in the group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities, discloses everything known to them about the group, and thereby substantially facilitates the detection and proof of crimes committed by it shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55.

(4) A participant in the group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities and discloses the group before another crime under this section has been committed by it or by them shall not be punished.

### Раздел IV.

Нарушаване неприкосновеността на жилище, помещение или превозно средство (Загл. изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.)

#### Чл. 170.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever enters another's dwelling by using force, threat, deceit, dexterity, abuse of power or special technical means shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to three years or probation of up to six months.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed at night or by an armed person, or by two or more persons, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to five years.

(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) If the acts under the preceding paragraphs are directed against a dwelling, vehicle or official premises of a person enjoying international protection, the punishment is: under para. 1 - deprivation of liberty from one to five years, and under para. 2 - deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.

(4) (Предишна ал. 3, изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) Whoever unlawfully remains in another's dwelling despite an express request to leave shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to one year.

### Раздел V.

Нарушаване неприкосновеността на кореспонденцията

#### Чл. 171.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г.) Whoever unlawfully:

- 1. opens, forges, conceals or destroys another's letter, telegram, sealed papers, packet or other similar items;
- 2. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) takes another's letter or telegram, even if open, with the purpose of learning their content or with the same purpose delivers another's letter or telegram to someone else;
- 3. (нова - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) learns a communication not addressed to them, sent electronically, or diverts such communication from its addressee, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to one year or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva
  - (2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) If the act is committed by an official who has taken advantage of their official position, the punishment is deprivation of liberty of up to two years, and the court may also impose deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.
  - (3) (Доп. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 101 от 2017 г.) Whoever by using special technical means unlawfully gains access to or learns a communication not addressed to them, transmitted by telephone, telegraph, through a computer network or by other telecommunications means, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to two years.
  - (4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 101 от 2017 г.) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed when the subject of the act is computer data transmitted within one or between multiple information systems, including electromagnetic emissions from an information system.
  - (5) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., предишна ал. 4, доп. - ДВ, бр. 101 от 2017 г.) When the act under para. 3 and 4 is committed with a mercenary purpose or significant damage has been caused, the punishment is deprivation of liberty of up to three years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

Чл. 171а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) (1) (Изм. и доп. - ДВ, бр. 24 от 2015 г., в сила от 31.03.2015 г.) Whoever unlawfully acquires, stores, discloses or disseminates data such as those collected, processed, stored or used pursuant to the Electronic Communications Act shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to three years or probation.

(2) When the act under para. 1 is committed with a mercenary purpose, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to six years.

### Раздел VI.

Престъпления против трудовите права на гражданите

#### Чл. 172.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 1 от 1991 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. и доп. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever knowingly obstructs someone from taking up employment or compels them to leave employment due to their nationality or ethnic origin, race, skin color, religion, social origin, sexual orientation, membership or non-membership in a trade union or other organization, political party, organization, movement or coalition with a political purpose, or due to their political or other convictions or those of their relatives, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to three years or a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(2) An official who fails to execute an order or an enforceable decision to reinstate an improperly dismissed worker or employee shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to three years.


This excerpt sits under: | Конституция | Правилници | > Раздел III.

### Section VII.

Crimes against intellectual property (Title amended - SG, No. 50 of 1995)

Art. 172a. (New - SG, No. 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever records, reproduces, distributes, broadcasts or transmits, or otherwise uses another's object of copyright or related right, or copies thereof, without the consent required by law of the holder of the respective right, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine up to ten thousand levs.

(2) (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever builds or maintains an information system or provides an information society service with the purpose of committing crimes under para. 1 and obtaining material benefit.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 53 of 2022, previous para. 2, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever, without the consent required by law, possesses material carriers containing another's object of copyright or related right, where the monetary equivalent of one or more of the objects exceeds 70 minimum wages and the objects exceed fifty in number, or possesses a master for reproducing such carriers, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to six years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand levs.

(4) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 53 of 2022, previous para. 3, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 - 3 is committed repeatedly or has caused significant harmful consequences, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from three thousand to fifteen thousand levs.

(5) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, previous para. 4, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 3 is of a particularly large scale, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand levs.

(6) (Previous para. 4 - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, previous para. 5 - SG, No. 67 of 2023) For minor cases, the perpetrator shall be punished by administrative procedure under the Copyright and Related Rights Act.

(7) (Previous para. 5, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, previous para. 6, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) When the object of the crime belongs to the perpetrator, it shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

Art. 172b. (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) (1) (Amended and supplemented - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever, without the consent of the holder of the exclusive right, uses in commercial activity a trademark, invention, utility model, industrial design, plant variety or animal breed, being an object of that exclusive right, or uses a geographical indication or its imitation without legal basis, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine up to ten thousand levs.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed repeatedly or has caused significant harmful consequences, the punishment is imprisonment from five to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifteen thousand levs.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) When the object of the crime belongs to the perpetrator, it shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

#### Art. 173.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever publishes or uses under their own name or under a pseudonym another's work of science, literature or art or a substantial part of such work, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred levs, as well as public censure.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 81 of 1999, effective 14.12.1999) The same punishment shall also be imposed on whoever submits for registration or registers under their own name another's invention, utility model or industrial design.

(3) (New - SG, No. 53 of 2022) When the act under para. 1 is committed on the internet or has caused significant harmful consequences, the punishment is imprisonment up to six years and a fine up to ten thousand levs.

#### Art. 174.

(Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 81 of 1999, effective 14.12.1999, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever, by abusing their official position, joins as a co-author of an invention, utility model or industrial design or of a work of science, literature or art, without having participated in the creative work of its creation, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred levs, as well as public censure.

### Section VIII.

Crimes against the freedom of assemblies, rallies and demonstrations

Art. 174a. (New - SG, No. 10 of 1990) (1) Whoever, by violence, fraud, threat or other unlawful means, disperses or prevents the holding of an assembly, rally or demonstration permissible under the Assemblies, Rallies and Demonstrations Act, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years.

(2) An organizer who, in violation of Art. 12, para. 3, and 13, para. 1 of the Assemblies, Rallies and Demonstrations Act, holds prohibited or continues to hold terminated assembly, rally or demonstration, shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year.

### Section IX.

Crimes against the right of workers and employees to associate in trade union organizations and unions (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023)

### Section IX.

Art. 174b. (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023) (1) Whoever, through violence, threat or other unlawful means, prevents someone from exercising their right to trade union association by forcing them, contrary to their convictions or will, to become a member or to renounce membership in a trade union organization, or prevents them from establishing such, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years or a fine up to five thousand levs.

(2) Whoever, through violence, threat or other illegal means, prevents someone from exercising rights arising from their exercised right to trade union association, shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year or a fine up to three thousand levs.

(3) If the act under para. 1 or 2 is committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years or a fine up to ten thousand levs.

Special provision

#### Art. 175.

(Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 53 of 2022, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 67 of 2023) For crimes under Art. 170, para. 1 and 4, Art. 171, para. 1, Art. 172, para. 2, Art. 173, para. 1 and 2 and Art. 174b, criminal prosecution is initiated by complaint of the victim.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 19 of 2008) For crimes under Art. 172, para. 1 and Art. 174, general criminal prosecution is initiated by complaint of the victim to the prosecutor's office and may not be terminated at their request.

Chapter Four.

CRIMES AGAINST MARRIAGE, FAMILY AND YOUTH

### Section I.

Crimes against marriage and family

#### Art. 176.

(1) Whoever, when entering into marriage, deliberately conceals from the civil status official a legal impediment to the marriage, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) A civil status official who performs a marriage knowing that there is a legal impediment to the conclusion of the marriage shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.

(3) (Repealed - SG, No. 51 of 2000)

#### Art. 177.

(Amended - State Gazette, No. 16 of 2019) (1) Whoever forces another to enter into marriage by using force, threat or abuse of power shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who abducts another with the purpose to force them to enter into marriage.

(3) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who, with the purpose to force another to enter into marriage, induces them by deception to cross into the territory of another state.

(4) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who in any manner induces a person who was not able to understand the nature and significance of the act to enter into marriage or to cross into the territory of another state with the purpose to force them to enter into marriage.

(5) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to eight years when the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed:

- 1. by a parent, other relative or guardian;
- 2. against a minor;
- 3. against two or more persons;
- 4. for mercenary purpose.

#### Art. 178.

(Amended - State Gazette, No. 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 10 of 1993, amended - State Gazette, No. 92 of 2002) A parent or other relative who receives ransom to allow his daughter or relative to enter into marriage shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred levs, as well as by public reprimand.

(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on the one who gives or who mediates in the giving or receiving of such ransom.

#### Art. 179.

(1) Whoever concludes another marriage while having a lawful marriage shall be punished for bigamy by imprisonment of up to three years.

(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on the one who concludes a marriage with a person whom they know is in a lawful marriage.

(3) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 103 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2005) If the first marriage is declared invalid or is terminated on other grounds, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 180.

(Repealed - State Gazette, No. 1 of 1991)

#### Art. 181.

(Amended - State Gazette, No. 103 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2005) Whoever violates their obligation to a spouse, ascendant or descendant unable to care for themselves and thereby places them in a position of serious difficulty, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by probation, as well as by public reprimand.

#### Art. 182.

(1) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 103 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2005, amended - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2010) A parent or guardian who leaves a person under parental care or guardianship without supervision and sufficient care and thereby creates a danger to their physical, mental or moral development shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years, as well as by public reprimand.

(2) (New - State Gazette, No. 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982, amended - State Gazette, No. 10 of 1993, amended - State Gazette, No. 92 of 2002, amended - State Gazette, No. 103 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2005, amended - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2010, amended - State Gazette, No. 16 of 2019) A parent or other relative who fails to comply with or in any manner obstructs the execution of a court decision regarding the exercise of parental rights or regarding personal contacts with a child shall be punished by probation and a fine from two thousand to five thousand levs, and in particularly serious cases - by imprisonment of up to six months and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand levs.

(3) (New - State Gazette, No. 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982) The perpetrator shall not be punished if, after a warning from the competent authority, they comply with the decision or remove the obstacles to its execution. This provision shall not be applied repeatedly.

Art. 182a. (New - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever, for the purpose of property gain, induces a parent through gift, promise, threat or abuse of official position to abandon their child or to give consent to their adoption shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to two thousand levs.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on the one who induces a minor to give consent to their adoption when the law requires it.

(3) Whoever, for the purpose of unlawful property gain, mediates between a person or family wishing to adopt a child and a parent wishing to abandon their child, or a woman agreeing to carry a child in her womb with the purpose to hand it over for adoption, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years and a fine of up to three thousand levs.

(4) If the act under para. 3 is committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to four thousand levs.

Art. 182b. (New - State Gazette, No. 75 of 2006, effective as of 13.10.2006) (1) A female person who gives consent to the sale of her child domestically or abroad shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand levs.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a pregnant woman who gives consent to the sale of her child before its birth.

#### Art. 183.

(Amended - State Gazette, No. 95 of 1975, amended - State Gazette, No. 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 92 of 2002, effective as of 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2004, amended - State Gazette, No. 103 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2005, supplemented - State Gazette, No. 47 of 2009, effective as of 01.10.2009) Whoever, having been ordered to support their spouse, ascendant, descendant, brother or sister, knowingly fails to fulfill their obligation in the amount of two or more monthly installments shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or by probation.

(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on the one who deliberately places themselves in impossibility to provide support, either by transferring their property, or by not exercising their rights, or in another manner.

(3) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982) The perpetrator shall not be punished if, before the sentence is issued by the first instance, they fulfill their obligation and no other harmful consequences have occurred for the victim. This provision shall not be applied repeatedly.

(4) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 92 of 2002, effective as of 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2004) If the act under para. 1 and 2 is committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to two years or probation, as well as public reprimand.

#### Art. 184.

(1) Whoever intentionally replaces, conceals or substitutes a small child or in another manner conceals or replaces someone's civil status shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years.

(2) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982, amended - State Gazette, No. 10 of 1993, amended - State Gazette, No. 92 of 2002) If this is done for mercenary purpose, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred levs.

#### Art. 185.

(1) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever arbitrarily takes in or detains with themselves another's child under fourteen years of age and does not immediately notify the authorities or return them to the parents or their guardian shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years.

(2) (Amended - State Gazette, No. 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982, amended - State Gazette, No. 10 of 1993, amended - State Gazette, No. 92 of 2002, amended - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2010) If this is done with violence, threat or fraud or with the intention to use the child for mercenary or immoral purposes, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of up to five thousand levs.

#### Art. 186.

(Amended - State Gazette, No. 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982, amended - State Gazette, No. 10 of 1993, amended - State Gazette, No. 92 of 2002, amended - State Gazette, No. 103 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2005, amended - State Gazette, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever takes in an abandoned or lost child under seven years of age and does not immediately report this to the authorities, to the parents or their guardian shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or by probation.

### Section II.

Crimes against Youth

#### Art. 187.

(Amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amend. - SG No. 26 of 2010) Whoever tortures a young child or a juvenile in his care or whose upbringing has been entrusted to him, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation, as well as public censure.

#### Art. 188.

(Amend. - SG No. 74 of 2015) (1) Whoever induces a person under 18 years of age to commit a crime through coercion or through use of a position of dependency or supervision, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.

(2) The penalty under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever induces a person under 18 years of age to prostitution through coercion or through use of a position of dependency or supervision.

(3) Where the act under para. 1 or 2 has resulted in harmful consequences for the physical, mental or moral development of the victim, when the act does not constitute a more serious crime, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to six years and public censure.

(4) Where the act under para. 2 has been committed against a person under 14 years of age, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to ten years.

(5) In the cases under para. 1 - 4, the court may also impose deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 or 7.

#### Art. 189.

(1) (Amend. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG No. 27 of 2009) Whoever systematically uses a person in his care for begging shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.

(2) (Amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) If the perpetrator is a parent or guardian of the victim, the penalty shall be imprisonment of up to two years or probation, as well as public censure.

#### Art. 190.

(Amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amend. - SG No. 16 of 2019) (1) Whoever forces another to cohabit as spouses with someone, by using force, threat or abuse of power, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) The penalty under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who abducts another with the purpose of forcing him to cohabit as spouses with someone.

(3) The penalty under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who, with the purpose of forcing another to cohabit as spouses with him or with another person, induces him through deception to cross into the territory of another state.

(4) The penalty under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who in any manner induces a person who was unable to understand the nature and significance of the act to cohabit as spouses with another.

(5) The penalty shall be imprisonment from three to eight years, where the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed:

- 1. by a parent, other relative or guardian;
- 2. against a juvenile;
- 3. against two or more persons;
- 4. with mercenary intent.

#### Art. 191.

(Amend. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) An adult who, without having entered into marriage, cohabits as spouses with a female person under 16 years of age, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or probation, as well as public censure.

(2) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) An adult who induces or facilitates a juvenile male and a female person under 16 years of age to cohabit as spouses without having entered into marriage, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or probation.

(3) (Former para. 2 - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amend. - SG No. 89 of 1986) If the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed against a person under 14 years of age, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two to five years.

(4) (Former para. 3, amend. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, repealed - SG No. 16 of 2019)

#### Art. 192.

(Amend. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG No. 26 of 2010) A parent or other relative who receives ransom to allow his daughter or female relative under 16 years of age to cohabit as spouses with another, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or a fine up to three thousand leva.

(2) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) The same penalty shall also be imposed on whoever gives or mediates in the giving of such ransom.

Art. 192a. (New - SG No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Amend. - SG No. 27 of 2009) Whoever without proper authorization employs a person under 18 years of age shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six months and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.

(2) (Amend. - SG No. 27 of 2009) If the act under para. 1 has been committed against a person under 16 years of age, the penalty shall be imprisonment of up to one year and a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva.

(3) (Amend. - SG No. 27 of 2009) If the act under para. 1 has been committed repeatedly, the penalty shall be imprisonment of up to one year and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva, and under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from three thousand to eight thousand leva.

#### Art. 193.

(Amend. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amend. - SG No. 89 of 1986) (1) (Amend. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever intoxicates with alcoholic beverages a person under 18 years of age or a mentally incompetent person shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six months or a fine up to five hundred leva.

(2) (Amend. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amend. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amend. - SG No. 27 of 2009) Whoever sells an alcoholic beverage to a person under 18 years of age or to a mentally incompetent person for personal consumption shall be punished by a fine up to one thousand leva and probation, and if he does this systematically - by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine up to three thousand leva.

Special provision

Art. 193a. (New - SG No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG No. 26 of 2004, repealed - SG No. 26 of 2010, new - SG No. 16 of 2019) For a crime under Art. 182, para. 2, criminal prosecution shall be initiated upon complaint of the victim.

Chapter Five.

CRIMES AGAINST PROPERTY (Title amend. - SG No. 10 of 1993)

### Section I.

Theft

#### Art. 194.

(1) (Amend. - SG No. 10 of 1993) Whoever takes another's movable property from the possession of another without his consent with the intention of unlawfully appropriating it shall be punished for theft by imprisonment of up to eight years.

(2) Theft also exists when part of the property belongs to the perpetrator.

(3) (Amend. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amend. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) In minor cases, the penalty shall be imprisonment of up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Art. 195.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993) For theft, the punishment is imprisonment from one to ten years:

- 1. if committed during a fire, flood, shipwreck, disaster, war or other public calamity;
- 2. (amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) if the stolen item is not under constant supervision;
- 3. if committed through destruction, damage or undermining of barriers solidly made for the protection of persons or property;
- 4. (amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) if a motor vehicle, technical means or special method was used to commit the theft;
- 5. if committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission, when it does not constitute a minor case;
- 6. if the theft was committed by an official who took advantage of his official position;
- 7. in non-minor cases, if committed repeatedly;
- 8. (new - SG, No. 10 of 1993) if the theft was committed from a deceased person's grave;
- 9. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) if the theft was committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, items 6 and 8;
- 10. (new - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) if the subject of the theft is explosives, pyrotechnic devices, weapons or ammunition for firearms;
- 11. (new - SG, No. 101 of 2017) if the theft is for the purpose of obtaining funds for committing a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1, 2, 6 or 7;
- 12. (new - SG, No. 7 of 2019) if the subject of the theft is a motor vehicle.
  - (2) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) For theft on a large scale, the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years, and the court may order confiscation of up to one-half of the property of the guilty person.
  - (3) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 102 of 2006, amended - SG, No. 102 of 2008, amended - SG, No. 93 of 2009, effective from 25.12.2009, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, amended - SG, No. 79 of 2015, effective from 01.11.2015) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed for theft of explosives, firearms or ammunition for firearms from the structural units of the Ministry of Internal Affairs, from the Ministry of Defense, from the Bulgarian Army and from the structures subordinate to the Minister of Defense, from the State Agency "State Reserve and Wartime Stocks", from the State Agency "National Security", from the Main Directorate "Protection" and Main Directorate "Execution of Punishments" at the Ministry of Justice, from the State Agency "Intelligence" and the National Protection Service.
  - (4) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, previous para. 3 - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) In minor cases under items 2 and 6 of para. 1, the punishment is imprisonment up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
  - (5) (Previous para. 4, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 7 of 2019) For preparation for theft under para. 1, items 3, 4 and 12, the punishment is imprisonment up to three years or probation.

#### Art. 196.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993) For theft constituting dangerous recidivism, the punishment is:

- 1. in the cases of Art. 194, para. 1 - imprisonment from two to ten years;
- 2. (supplemented - SG, No. 26 of 2004) in the cases of Art. 195, para. 1 and 2 - imprisonment from three to fifteen years, and in the cases under Art. 195, para. 3 - imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
  - (2) (Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) The court may order confiscation of up to one-half of the property of the guilty person.

Art. 196a. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) For theft on an especially large scale, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years and confiscation of all or part of the property of the guilty person.

#### Art. 197. If until the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the stolen item is returned or replaced, the punishment is:

1. in the cases of Art. 194, para. 1 - imprisonment up to five years;

2. (amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) in the cases of Art. 194, para. 3 and 195, para. 4 - probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva;

3. (amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986) in the cases of Art. 195, para. 1, items 2-6 - imprisonment up to eight years;

4. (amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986) in the cases of Art. 195, para. 2 in connection with Art. 194 or with Art. 195, para. 1, items 2 - 6 - imprisonment up to eight years;

5. (new - SG, No. 89 of 1986) in the cases of Art. 196a - imprisonment from eight to twenty years. Art. 197a. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993)

### Section II.

Robbery

#### Art. 198.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993) Whoever takes another's movable property from the possession of another with the intention to unlawfully appropriate it, using force or threat for this purpose, shall be punished for robbery with imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) Threat means threatening with such an immediate act which exposes to serious danger the life, health, honor or property of the threatened person or of any other person present.

(3) Robbery is also any theft in which the person caught at the scene of the crime uses force or threat to retain possession of the stolen item.

(4) Robbery also exists when, in order to take the item, the person has been rendered unconscious or placed in a defenseless state.

#### Art. 199.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993) For robbery of items:

- 1. on a large scale;
- 2. committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance to commit thefts or robberies;
- 3. accompanied by serious or medium bodily injury;
- 4. (amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) constituting dangerous recidivism;
- 5. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) committed by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, (amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and the court may also order confiscation of up to one-half of the property of the guilty person.
  - (2) (Amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995) For robbery of a movable item:
- 1. accompanied by serious or medium bodily injury, which resulted in death;
- 2. accompanied by murder or attempted murder;
- 3. (Amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) on an especially large scale, if the perpetrator was armed, the punishment is imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation. The court may also order confiscation of all or part of the property of the guilty person.

#### Art. 200.

For preparation for robbery under Art. 198, the punishment is imprisonment up to two years, and under Art. 199 - imprisonment up to three years.

### Section III.

Embezzlement

#### Art. 201.

(1) (Am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 10 of 1993, suppl. - SG No. 50 of 1995, the suppl. of SG No. 50 of 1995 was declared unconstitutional by Decision No. 19 of 1995 of the Constitutional Court - SG No. 97 of 1995, am. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, previous text of Art. 201 - SG No. 83 of 2019) An official who misappropriates someone else's money, items or other valuables handed over in that capacity or entrusted to keep or manage them shall be punished for official misappropriation with imprisonment of up to eight years, and the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person and deprive them of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

(2) (New - SG No. 83 of 2019) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed when the act is committed by a foreign official.

#### Art. 202. (1) For official misappropriation the punishment is imprisonment from one to ten years:

1. if another crime was also committed to facilitate it, for which the law does not provide for a heavier punishment;

2. (am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) if committed by two or more persons who conspired in advance.

(2) For official misappropriation the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years:

- 1. (am. - SG No. 92 of 2002) if in large amounts,
- 2. (am. - SG No. 92 of 2002) if it constitutes dangerous recidivism or
- 3. (new - SG No. 92 of 2002) if the misappropriated funds are from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by the European Union to the Bulgarian state.
  - (3) (Am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) In the cases of the preceding paragraphs the court deprives the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. The court may also order confiscation under para. 1 of up to one half, and under para. 2 - of part or all of the property of the guilty person.

#### Art. 203.

(1) (Am. - SG No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) For official misappropriation in particularly large amounts, constituting a particularly serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years.

(2) (Am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004) The court orders confiscation of all or part of the property of the guilty person and deprives them of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

#### Art. 204. In minor cases of official misappropriation the punishment is:

a) (am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) under Art. 201 - imprisonment of up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva. b) (am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) under Art. 202, para. 1 - imprisonment of up to two years or probation.

#### Art. 205. (1) If the misappropriated money, items or valuables are deposited or replaced before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court, the punishment is:

1. (am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) in the cases under Art. 201 - imprisonment of up to five years;

2. (am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) in the cases under Art. 202, para. 1 - imprisonment from one to seven years;

3. in the cases under Art. 202, para. 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years;

4. (am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 89 of 1986) in the cases under Art. 203 - imprisonment from eight to twenty years;

5. (am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) in the cases under Art. 204, letter "a" - probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva;

6. (am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) in the cases under Art. 204, letter "b" - imprisonment of up to six months or probation.

(2) (Am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) In the cases of points 2, 3 and 4 of the preceding paragraph the court also orders deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, and in the cases under point 3 may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person, and in the cases under point 4 orders confiscation of part or all of the property.

#### Art. 206.

(Am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG No. 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully misappropriates someone else's movable property which they possess or keep shall be punished for embezzlement with imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) (Suppl. - SG No. 92 of 2002) Embezzlement also exists when part of the item belongs to the perpetrator, as well as when the item belongs to the perpetrator but is encumbered with a pledge and the perpetrator disposes of it unlawfully without preserving the rights of the pledgee, or when the perpetrator pledges someone else's property, thereby making it difficult for the creditor to obtain satisfaction.

(3) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) If the embezzlement is in large amounts or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years, and the court deprives the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7 and may order confiscation of part or all of their property.

(4) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) For embezzlement in particularly large amounts, constituting a particularly serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and the court also orders deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7 and confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.

(5) (Previous para. 3, am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) In minor cases the punishment is imprisonment of up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(6) (Previous para. 4, am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) If the misappropriated property is deposited or replaced before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court, the punishment is:

- 1. under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to three years;
- 2. under para. 3 - imprisonment from two to eight years;
- 3. under para. 4 - imprisonment from three to twelve years;
- 4. (am. - SG No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) under para. 5 - probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
  - (7) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) In the cases of point 2 of the preceding paragraph the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person and deprive them of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, and in the cases of point 3 orders confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person and deprives them of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

#### Art. 207.

(Am. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, corr. - SG No. 31 of 1982, am. - SG No. 10 of 1993) (1) (Am. - SG No. 92 of 2002) Whoever finds someone else's movable property and does not report it within one week to the owner, to the authorities or to the one who lost it shall be punished with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on whoever unlawfully misappropriates someone else's property which came into their possession accidentally or by mistake.

#### Art. 208.

(1) (Amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 26 of 2004, amended and supplemented - SG No. 27 of 2009, effective 10.04.2009) Whoever discovers a treasure and within seven days does not report to the authorities shall be punished with probation or a fine from five hundred to one thousand leva.

(2) (New - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG No. 27 of 2009, effective 10.04.2009) Whoever, while searching, discovers a treasure and within two weeks does not report to the authorities shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.

(3) (Previous para. 2, amended - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 26 of 2004) If the treasure is of particularly large size, the punishment shall be: under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to two years or a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, and under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to five years or a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.

(4) (New - SG No. 27 of 2009, effective 10.04.2009) When the treasure contains a cultural value or the act under para. 1 - 3 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be: under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to two years and a fine of up to five thousand leva, under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, and under para. 3 - imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(5) (New - SG No. 27 of 2009, effective 10.04.2009) When the act under para. 2 - 4 has been committed on commission or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva.

(6) (New - SG No. 26 of 2004, previous para. 4 - SG No. 27 of 2009, effective 10.04.2009) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

### Section IV.

Fraud

#### Art. 209.

(Amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG No. 26 of 2010) Whoever, with the purpose of obtaining for himself or for another a property benefit, induces or maintains someone in error and thereby causes property damage to him or to another, shall be punished for fraud with imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) (Amended - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG No. 26 of 2010) Whoever with the same purpose exploits the error, inexperience or lack of awareness of someone and thereby causes property damage to him or to another shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years.

(3) (Amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) In minor cases under the preceding paragraphs, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 210. (1) For fraud the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to eight years:

1. if the perpetrator has presented himself as an official or as a person acting on commission of the authorities;

2. (amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) if it has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission;

3. (supplemented - SG No. 26 of 2004) if the fraud has been committed by an official or by an agent within the scope of his office or agency or by a person who directly conducts by occupation transactions in cash currency;

4. if it has been committed repeatedly in non-minor cases;

5. (new - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) if the damage caused is of large size.

(2) (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) In the cases of points 4 and 5, the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.

#### Art. 211.

(Amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) When the fraud under Art. 209, para. 1 and 2 and under 210 is of particularly large size, constituting a particularly serious case or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years. The court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.

#### Art. 212.

(Amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended and supplemented - SG No. 26 of 2010) Whoever, through the use of a document with false content or of an inauthentic or falsified document, obtains without legal basis another's movable or immovable property with the intention of misappropriating it shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) (Amended and supplemented - SG No. 27 of 2009) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on the one who, through the compilation of a document with false content or of an inauthentic or falsified document, knowingly gave the opportunity to another natural or legal person to obtain without legal basis such property.

(3) (New - SG No. 92 of 2002) When the property is from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by them to the Bulgarian state, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years.

(4) (Previous para. 3 - SG No. 92 of 2002) If the property under the preceding paragraphs is of large size or the act constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to fifteen years.

(5) (Previous para. 4 - SG No. 92 of 2002) For documentary fraud of particularly large size, constituting a particularly serious case, the punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years.

(6) (Previous para. 5 - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) In minor cases under paragraphs 1 and 2, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to two years or probation.

(7) (Previous para. 6 - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, amended - SG No. 27 of 2009) In the cases of para. 1, the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, and in the cases of para. 3, 4 and 5 shall order confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

Art. 212a. (New - SG No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amended - SG No. 38 of 2007) Whoever, with the purpose of obtaining a benefit for himself or for another, induces or maintains someone in error by entering, modifying, deleting or erasing computer data or using another's electronic signature and thereby causes damage to him or to another shall be punished for computer fraud with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of up to six thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG No. 38 of 2007) The same punishment shall be imposed on the one who, without having the right, enters, modifies, deletes or erases computer data in order to obtain something that is not due to him.

Art. 212b. (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, previous Art. 212a - SG No. 92 of 2002) (1) If the obtained property under Art. 212 is returned or replaced before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first-instance court, the punishment shall be:

- 1. under para. 1 and 2 - imprisonment of up to five years;
- 2. under para. 3 - imprisonment from two to eight years;
- 3. (supplemented - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) under para. 4 and 5 - imprisonment from five to fifteen years;
- 4. (amended - SG No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) under para. 6 - probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
  - (2) (Amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) In the cases under point 2 of the preceding paragraph, the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, and under point 3 shall order confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

#### Art. 213.

(Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever destroys, damages or annihilates his own insured property with fraudulent intent shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred levs.

### Section V.

Extortion

Art. 213a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) Whoever, with the purpose of compelling another to dispose of a thing or of his right or to assume a proprietary obligation, threatens him with violence, disclosure of disgraceful circumstances, damage to property or other unlawful act with serious consequences for him or his relatives, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of one thousand to three thousand levs.

(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine of three thousand to five thousand levs when the act:

- 1. is accompanied by a threat of murder or grievous bodily harm;
- 2. is accompanied by causing light bodily injury;
- 3. is accompanied by seizure, destruction or damage to property;
- 4. has been committed by two or more persons;
- 5. has been committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, items 6 and 8;
- 6. has been committed by an armed person;
- 7. has been committed repeatedly in non-negligible cases.
  - (3) The punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine of five thousand to ten thousand levs, and the court may order confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property, when:
- 1. medium or grievous bodily harm has been caused, if a more severe punishment is not provided for the committed crime;
- 2. substantial proprietary damages have been caused;
- 3. the act has been committed by an organization or group or on commission from a person, organization or group;
- 4. the act is accompanied by an explosion or arson;
- 5. the act has been committed by or with the participation of an official;
- 6. the act has been committed against an official in connection with his service;
- 7. the act constitutes dangerous recidivism.
  - (4) (Am. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) The punishment shall be imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years or life imprisonment, or life imprisonment without commutation, and the court may order confiscation of part or all of the perpetrator's property, when the act is:
- 1. accompanied by grievous or medium bodily harm, from which death has resulted;
- 2. accompanied by murder or attempted murder.

#### Art. 214.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 50 of 1995, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever, with the purpose of obtaining for himself or for another a proprietary benefit, compels someone by force or threat to perform, to omit or to suffer something contrary to his will and thereby causes him or another proprietary damage, shall be punished for extortion by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of one thousand to three thousand levs, and the court may impose confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property.

(2) (Am. - SG, No. 50 of 1995, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) For extortion under the conditions of Art. 213a, paras. 2, 3 and 4, the punishment shall be:

- 1. under para. 2 - imprisonment from two to ten years and a fine of four thousand to six thousand levs, and the court may order confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property;
- 2. under para. 3 - imprisonment from five to fifteen years, a fine of five thousand to ten thousand levs and confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property;
- 3. (Am. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) under para. 4 - imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation and confiscation of not less than 1/2 of the perpetrator's property.
  - (3) (New - SG, No. 50 of 1995) For extortion the punishment shall be from five to fifteen years imprisonment and a fine of up to five hundred levs, and the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the perpetrator's property, if:
- 1. it is accompanied by grievous or medium bodily harm;
- 2. the act constitutes dangerous recidivism.

Art. 214a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) For preparation for a crime under Arts. 213a and 214, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to three years.

### Section VI.

Receiving Stolen Property

#### Art. 215.

(Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, suppl. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever, with the purpose of obtaining for himself or for another a proprietary benefit, conceals, acquires or assists in alienating another's movable things, which he knows or supposes to have been acquired by another through a crime or through another socially dangerous act, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years, but with a punishment not more severe than that provided for the crime itself.

(2) (Am. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine of five thousand to ten thousand levs, if the receiving:

- 1. is on a large scale;
- 2. involves things placed under a special regime;
- 3. is carried out as a trade;
- 4. has been committed repeatedly or constitutes dangerous recidivism.

### Section VII.

Destruction and Damage

#### Art. 216.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) Whoever unlawfully destroys or damages another's movable or immovable thing shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.

(2) (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever destroys, demolishes or damages his own mortgaged or pledged property shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of up to two thousand levs.

(3) (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017) Whoever, by effecting unauthorized access to an information system of significance to an enterprise, institution, legal or natural person, thereby destroys or damages another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of up to ten thousand levs.

(4) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, previous para. 2, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) In minor cases the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to six months or a fine of one hundred to three hundred levs.

(5) (Am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, previous para. 3 - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) The punishment shall be imprisonment of up to ten years, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7, when:

- 1. the act is connected with the destruction or damage of elements of the telecommunications network;
- 2. substantial damages or other serious consequences have resulted from the act;
- 3. the act has been committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, items 6 and 8;
- 4. the act has been committed on racist, xenophobic or sexual orientation-related motives.
  - (6) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, previous para. 4, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) If the act under paras. 1, 2, 3 and 5 has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to two years or a fine of one hundred to three hundred levs.

Art. 216a. (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever, by himself or through another, unlawfully violates the integrity of facilities or objects of an electricity transmission or electricity distribution network, of a gas transmission or heat transmission system, of a system for transmission of liquid fuels, or of a water supply or sewerage system, thereby creating conditions for diversion of electrical energy, natural gas, liquid fuels, thermal energy or water, or discharge of waste water, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of up to twenty thousand levs.

(2) (New - SG, No. 84 of 2023) The same punishment shall also be imposed on one who, by himself or through another, causes disruptions or interruption of the supply of water, electricity or other basic natural resources, from which danger to human life may arise.
