(3) (Am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, previous para. 2, suppl. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 or 2 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to ten years and a fine of up to thirty thousand levs.

### Раздел VIII.

Abuse of trust

#### Чл. 217.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever knowingly damages another's property entrusted to him to manage or safeguard shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or by a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on a representative or attorney-in-fact who knowingly acts against the lawful interests of the person represented.

(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on anyone who disposes of an attached or pledged thing left with him for safekeeping.

(4) (Предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) If the above acts result in substantial or irreparable damages, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

Special provision

#### Чл. 218.

(Отм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г.)

Supplementary provisions

(Загл. изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.)

Чл. 218а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1979 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., отм. - бр. 10 от 1993 г.)

Чл. 218б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 21 от 2000 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) For acts under Art. 194, para. 3, Art. 195, para. 4, Art. 204, letter "a", Art. 206, para. 1 and 5 and Art. 207 and for property concealment in connection therewith, where the value of the object is up to the amount of two minimum wages for the country established as of the date of commission of the act, the punishment shall be a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva imposed by administrative procedure, if the object of the crime is returned or replaced.

(2) The provision of the preceding paragraph shall not apply where:

- 1. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 21 от 2000 г.) the perpetrator has committed two or more acts within one year, the total value of the object of which exceeds one hundred and fifty leva;
- 2. the perpetrator has been convicted of such a crime, or if an administrative penalty has been imposed on him for such an act and one year has not elapsed since its commission;
- 3. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 33 от 2011 г., в сила от 27.05.2011 г.) the object of the act is weapons, ammunition for firearms, explosive, poisonous or narcotic substances, pyrotechnic articles, combat and other special equipment.

Чл. 218в. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) Criminal prosecution shall be initiated on complaint by the injured party in the following cases:

- 1. for crimes under Art. 216, para. 4 and 6 and Art. 217, para. 1 and 2, where the object of the crime is private property;
- 2. for theft, appropriation, fraud and extortion, where the object of the crime is private property, if the injured party is the spouse, ascendant, descendant or collateral relative to the second degree of the perpetrator or lives with him in one household, or is the guardian or trustee of the perpetrator.

Глава шеста.

CRIMES AGAINST THE ECONOMY

### Раздел I.

General economic crimes

#### Чл. 219.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) An official who does not exercise sufficient care for the management, administration, operation or preservation of the property entrusted to him or for the work assigned to him, and from this results substantial damage, destruction or waste of the property or other substantial harm to the enterprise or to the economy, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., изм.- ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) Whoever, despite his obligations, has not exercised sufficient control over the work of persons to whom the management, disposal or accounting of public property has been entrusted, and from this has resulted substantial harm to the enterprise or to the economy, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) If the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed intentionally and does not contain the elements of a more serious crime, the punishment shall be imprisonment of two to eight years, and the court may order deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

(4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) For a crime under the preceding paragraphs in particularly large amounts, constituting a particularly serious case, the punishment shall be under para. 1 and 2 - imprisonment of three to ten years, and under para. 3 - imprisonment of three to twelve years, and in these cases the court shall also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

#### Чл. 220.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) An official who knowingly concludes a disadvantageous transaction and from this results substantial harm to the economy or to the institution, enterprise or organization which he represents, shall be punished by imprisonment of one to six years, and the court may order deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) In particularly serious cases under the preceding paragraph, the punishment shall be three to ten years' imprisonment, and the court shall order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 101 от 2001 г.)

#### Чл. 221.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1988 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 1 от 1991 г.)

Чл. 221а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 27 от 1973 г., попр. бр. 31 от 1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 86 от 1991 г., попр., бр. 90 от 1991 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever orders or, in violation of his duties, permits agricultural land or pastures to be taken and used for construction and other non-agricultural purposes that have not been alienated or transferred according to the established procedure, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever continues, orders or, in violation of his duties, permits the continuation of construction or other non-agricultural use on land under the preceding paragraph, after the construction and other use have been stopped by the competent authorities according to the established procedure, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

Чл. 221б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 44 от 1984 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 1 от 1991 г.)

#### Чл. 222.

(Отм. - ДВ, бр. 1 от 1991 г.)

#### Чл. 223.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever provides false data concerning the quantity, quality or type of production or of work performed with the purpose of obtaining a pecuniary benefit that is not due, if this does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or by probation.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever receives remuneration for his production or work performed that is not due to him, knowing that it was determined on the basis of such false data, if this does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by probation or by a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(3) What is acquired by the perpetrator as a result of the crimes under the preceding paragraphs shall be returned to the respective organization.


This excerpt sits under: | Конституция | Правилници |

#### Чл. 224.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever receives a gift or other property benefit, in order to give or because they have given to a foreign state, foreign organization or company, or to a foreign citizen information from which significant harm to the economy has resulted or may result, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on the one who has given the gift or property benefit.

(3) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

#### Чл. 225.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 1973 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 81 от 1990 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever sells goods at a price above the determined one or before it is determined or approved according to the established procedure or receives remuneration for a service greater than legally permissible, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to two years or a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed repeatedly and is a non-trivial case or if the excess amount received is significant, the punishment is imprisonment from six months to three years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 26 от 1973 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 81 от 1990 г., нова - ДВ, бр. 81 от 1990 г.) Whoever, after being punished for a violation under Art. 3 of the Law on Combating Speculation, commits the same violation before one year has elapsed from the entry into force of the penal decree, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years.

(4) (Отм., предишна ал. 5 - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever deliberately compiles false information or presents false data for determining the price of goods or services and from this damage has resulted or could have resulted to a state institution or enterprise, to a public organization or to citizens, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

(5) (Отм., предишна ал. 7 - ДВ, бр. 81 от 1990 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) In cases where under the preceding paragraphs it imposes a sentence of imprisonment, the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 or 7.

(6) (Отм., предишна ал. 5 - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 81 от 1990 г., нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 2020 г., в сила от 24.03.2020 г.) When the act under para. 1 is committed during a declared state of emergency under Art. 84, item 12 of the Constitution of the Republic of Bulgaria, the punishment is imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of five thousand to ten thousand leva.

Чл. 225а. (Отм. - ДВ, бр. 1 от 1991 г.)

Чл. 225б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever for work performed or service rendered receives a property benefit which is not due to them, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to two years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed repeatedly or the benefit is in large amounts, the punishment is imprisonment of up to three years.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) In trivial cases under para. 1, the punishment is a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.

(4) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

Чл. 225в. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) (1) Whoever, while performing work for a legal entity or sole trader, requests or accepts a gift or any benefit which is not due to them, or accepts an offer or promise of a gift or benefit, in order to perform or not perform an act in violation of their duties in carrying out commercial activity, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years or a fine of up to twenty thousand leva.

(2) Whoever in carrying out commercial activity offers, promises or gives a gift or any benefit to a person who performs work in a legal entity or for a sole trader, in order to perform or not perform an act in violation of their duties, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or a fine of up to fifteen thousand leva.

(3) The punishments under the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed also when with the consent of the person under para. 1 the gift or benefit has been offered, promised or given to another.

(4) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) Whoever mediates the commission of any of the acts under the preceding paragraphs, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year or a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(5) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

#### Чл. 226.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever, by using a state, cooperative or other public organization, carries out private economic activity in violation of established regulations and thereby obtains significant unlawful income, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever carries out private economic activity by concealing it in the form of a state, cooperative or other public organization, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(3) (Предишна ал. 2, изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) An official from the respective state, cooperative or other public organization who allows the crime under the preceding paragraphs to be committed, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or probation.

(4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 26 от 1973 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Upon repeated commission of the crime under the preceding paragraphs or when the unlawful income is in large amounts, the punishment is imprisonment from one to eight years.

(5) (Предишна ал. 4 изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 1973 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) If the unlawful income under the preceding paragraphs is in especially large amounts and the case is especially serious, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years.

#### Art. 227.

(Amd. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amd. - SG, No. 81 of 1999, in force as of 14.12.1999, repealed - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force as of 13.10.2006, new - SG, No. 19 of 2012) (1) Whoever simultaneously employs five or more illegally residing foreigners on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to four years and a fine of two thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who employs an illegally residing foreigner on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria whom the perpetrator knows to be a victim of human trafficking.

(3) Whoever employs an illegally residing foreigner on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria who has not reached 18 years of age shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine of three thousand to thirty thousand leva.

(4) Whoever systematically employs one or more illegally residing foreigners on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years and a fine of five thousand to fifty thousand leva.

(5) The punishment under para. 4 shall also be imposed on a person who employs an illegally residing foreigner on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria under working conditions that substantially differ from the working conditions of legally employed persons and violate human dignity.

(6) In minor cases under para. 2 - 4, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to two years or probation, as well as a fine of one thousand to ten thousand leva.

Art. 227a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force as of 01.07.1982, amd. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, repealed - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force as of 13.10.2006)

### Section I "a".

Crimes against creditors (New - SG, No. 107 of 1996)

Art. 227b. (New - SG, No. 107 of 1996) (1) (Amd. - SG, No. 85 of 1998, amd. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force as of 13.10.2006) A trader who becomes insolvent and does not declare this before the court within 30 days from the cessation of payments shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(2) (Amd. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) The punishment under para. 1 shall also apply to persons who manage and represent a commercial company or cooperative if within 30 days from the cessation of payments they have not requested the court to initiate insolvency proceedings.

(3) The punishment under para. 1 shall also apply to a procurator who has not fulfilled his obligation under Art. 626, para. 3 of the Commercial Act.

(4) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amd. - SG, No. 59 of 2006, in force as of 01.01.2007, suppl. - SG, No. 102 of 2015, in force as of 01.01.2016) The punishment under para. 1 shall also apply to persons who, having been obliged to notify the Bulgarian National Bank of a bank's insolvency under the Credit Institutions Act, as well as persons who, having been obliged to notify the Financial Supervision Commission of an insurer's or reinsurer's insolvency under the Insurance Code, have not done so.

Art. 227c. (New - SG, No. 107 of 1996) (1) A trader who, after the initiation of insolvency proceedings:

- 1. conceals, destroys, damages or alienates gratuitously money, things, securities or other valuables that could serve to satisfy his creditors;
- 2. alienates money, things, securities or other valuables that could serve to satisfy his creditors, when what is given significantly exceeds what is received and is done in contradiction to the normal conduct of business activity;
- 3. forgives or conceals his own claim;
- 4. acknowledges or assumes in any manner or satisfies a non-existent obligation;
- 5. takes a loan knowing that he cannot repay it;
- 6. extends credit for goods, money, things, securities or other valuables belonging to him in a manner contrary to the normal conduct of business activity;
- 7. unlawfully satisfies only one or several creditors or secures them to the detriment of other creditors;
- 8. destroys, conceals or falsifies his commercial books or documents or keeps them in violation of law in a manner that hinders the establishment of the assets and liabilities of his enterprise or activity, if the enumerated acts have caused significant damages, shall be punished for intentional bankruptcy by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
  - (2) When an act under para. 1 has caused damages in particularly large amounts, constituting a particularly serious case, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years. The court shall also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

Art. 227d. (New - SG, No. 107 of 1996) The punishments under Art. 227c shall also apply to persons who manage and represent a commercial company or cooperative if they commit or allow the commission of the acts specified in the same article, and in cases under para. 1 the court may also impose a fine of up to 500 leva, and under para. 2 - confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.

Art. 227e. (New - SG, No. 107 of 1996) (1) A trader who:

- 1. has not conducted his commercial affairs with the care of a good trader or has engaged in obviously risky transactions that do not fall within the scope of his ordinary activity;
- 2. has made personal, family or other expenses obviously inappropriate and unrelated to the activity and inconsistent with his financial status;
- 3. has failed to prepare or has prepared an incorrect annual accounting report and balance sheet when obliged to do so, as a result of which he has been declared insolvent and damages to creditors have ensued, shall be punished for negligent bankruptcy by deprivation of liberty for up to two years, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
  - (2) The punishments under para. 1 shall also apply to a trader who has been declared insolvent without having fulfilled his obligations under a preceding recovery plan.
  - (3) The punishments under para. 1 shall also apply to persons who manage and represent a commercial company or cooperative if they commit or allow the commission of the acts specified in the same paragraph.
  - (4) Persons under para. 1-3 shall not be punished if before the pronouncement of the sentence by the first instance they satisfy their creditors. This provision shall not apply repeatedly.

Art. 227f. (New - SG, No. 107 of 1996) (1) A trader who has obligations to another trader for whom insolvency proceedings have been initiated, knowing this and not fulfilling his obligation within the agreed or customary term, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or a fine of up to 200 leva.

(2) A trader who, with the approval, knowledge or in the interest of his creditor, conceals in whole or in part an obligation to him and thereby causes damage to his creditor, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years and a fine of up to 300 leva.

(3) Whoever, knowing that insolvency proceedings have been initiated for a trader, conceals or destroys with his consent his things that belong or would belong to the insolvency estate, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years and a fine of up to 300 leva.

### Section II.

Crimes in specific economic sectors

#### Чл. 228.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) (1) Whoever as a manager or control body orders or allows the production of poor quality, non-standard or incomplete industrial products or products that do not meet the established requirements for quality, type or marks, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to three years or with probation.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever in violation of their official duties marks as standard or does not mark goods that do not meet the respective requirements, when this is mandatory, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to one year or with probation.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) When the products or goods under the previous paragraphs are not in significant quantities or with significant value, the punishment under para. 1 is a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and under para. 2 - a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.

#### Чл. 229.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever when receiving agricultural products on behalf of a procurement or trading organization deceives the supplier regarding the quality or quantity of these products, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to three years or with probation, as well as with public censure.

#### Чл. 230.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever violates a regulation issued against the spread or occurrence of an infectious disease among domestic animals shall be punished with probation up to six months or with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) If infection has resulted from this, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to one year or probation.

(3) If the infectious disease is widely spread, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to three years.

(4) In accordance with the differences in the previous paragraphs, also punished is one who violates a regulation issued for combating diseases and pests affecting plants.

#### Чл. 231.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) Whoever puts on sale industrial or agricultural goods in significant quantities or with significant value that do not meet the requirements specified in para. 1 of Art. 228, without having explicitly declared these deficiencies of theirs, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to two years, with a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva and with deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) In minor cases under the previous paragraph, the punishment is a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.

#### Чл. 232.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) A seller who deceives a buyer in weighing or measuring goods or who uses false measures and weights shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to two years or with probation.

(2) With the same punishment shall be punished: a) whoever deceives a buyer by mixing foreign substances or deteriorating the quality of the goods in another way; b) whoever deceives a client regarding the quality of goods, materials or services.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) When damage up to one hundred leva has been caused by one or more acts under the previous paragraphs, the punishment is a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.

(4) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) If the act under the previous paragraph has been committed after an administrative punishment has been imposed on the person under the same paragraph with a decision that has entered into force, and one year has not elapsed from the commission of the first violation, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(5) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) In cases when under the previous paragraphs the court determines a punishment of deprivation of liberty, the court may also rule deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6 or 7.

#### Чл. 233.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 81 от 1990 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., нов - ДВ, бр. 102 от 1995 г.) (1) (Изм. и доп. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г.) Whoever without appropriate license, registration or permission exports, imports, transfers, transports, transits, carries out brokerage activity with weapons or with products or technologies with dual use, as well as when such activities are carried out in violation of prohibitions, restrictions or sanctions imposed by the Security Council of the United Nations, by the Organization for Security and Cooperation in Europe or by the European Union, indicated in an act of the Council of Ministers or arising from an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to six years and with a fine up to two hundred thousand leva.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) For particularly serious cases under para. 1, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from 3 to 8 years and a fine up to five hundred thousand leva.

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) In minor cases under para. 1, the punishment is a fine up to twenty thousand leva.

(4) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г.) The weapons or products or technologies with dual use, subject of the crime, shall be confiscated in favor of the state regardless of whose property they are, and if they are missing or have been alienated, their equivalent shall be awarded, determined according to the foreign trade contract.

#### Art. 234.

(Repealed - SG, No. 1 of 1991, new - SG, No. 107 of 1996) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010, amended - SG, No. 7 of 2019) Whoever distributes or possesses excise goods without a tax stamp, when such is required by law, in non-minor cases, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine up to 10 times the market price of the distributed goods, as well as with deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

(2) (New - SG, No. 7 of 2019) Whoever produces or possesses for the purpose of distribution alcohol, alcoholic beverages or tobacco products without a proper permit, in non-minor cases, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva, as well as with deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010, former para. 2 - SG, No. 7 of 2019) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7, when the act:

- 1. was committed repeatedly;
- 2. was committed by two or more persons who conspired in advance;
- 3. if the object of the crime is in large amounts.
  - (4) (New - SG, No. 7 of 2019) Whoever, in violation of the established order, manufactures, possesses or conceals objects, materials or tools, as well as technical equipment, which he knows or assumes are intended for or have served to construct facilities for the production of alcohol, alcoholic beverages or tobacco products under para. 2, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
  - (5) (Former para. 3 - SG, No. 7 of 2019) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

Art. 234a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever carries out foreign trade activity without a permit, which is required by law or by a decree of the Council of Ministers, or in violation of such a permit issued, shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years, a fine from five to ten thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

Art. 234b. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010, amended - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) Whoever purchases or carries out commercial activity with ferrous or non-ferrous metal waste without a license, which is required by a normative act, or in violation thereof, shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years and a fine from two thousand to fifty thousand leva, as well as with deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

(2) The waste, object of the act under para. 1, shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if missing or alienated, their equivalent value shall be awarded.

Art. 234c. (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) Whoever, by himself or through another, unlawfully connects to an electric transmission or electric distribution network, or to a gas transmission, heat transmission, water supply or sewerage system, liquid fuel transmission system, or unlawfully interferes with commercial metering devices for electricity, natural gas, liquid fuel, thermal energy or water, or discharged wastewater, thereby creating conditions for incomplete accounting of consumed electricity, natural gas, liquid fuel, thermal energy or water, or discharged wastewater, shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years and a fine up to fifteen thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) If the act under para. 1 was committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from one to eight years and a fine up to twenty thousand leva.

Art. 234d. (New - SG, No. 108 of 2020) (1) Whoever, without a permit, registration or license, which are required by a normative act or an act of the European Union, carries out by occupation public passenger transport, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years.

(2) When the act under para. 1 was committed by two or more persons who conspired in advance for its commission, or through the use of a false or altered document or a document with false content, the punishment is imprisonment from two to five years and a fine from two to ten thousand leva.

(3) (Declared unconstitutional in the part "regardless of whose property it is" by Constitutional Court Decision No. 12 of 2021 - SG, No. 84 of 2021) In the cases under para. 1 and 2, the motor vehicle used for committing the act shall be confiscated in favor of the state, regardless of whose property it is, and when missing or alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

#### Art. 235.

(Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever, without a proper written permit or with a proper permit but outside the places, terms, quantities and trees indicated therein, cuts, collects, extracts, takes or transports from the forest fund any trees or parts thereof, including cut or fallen ones, shall be punished with imprisonment up to six years and a fine from one thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) With the punishment under para. 1 shall also be punished the one who conceals, loads, transports, unloads, stores or processes illegally obtained timber from another.

(3) For a crime under para. 1 and 2, the punishment is imprisonment from one to eight years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva, if:

- 1. was committed by two or more persons who conspired in advance for its commission;
- 2. was committed in complicity with a forest official who took advantage of his official position;
- 3. was committed through the use of a false or altered document or a document with false content;
- 4. was committed repeatedly;
- 5. the object of the crime is in large amounts.
  - (4) When the crimes under para. 1 - 3 were committed by a person acting on order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group or constitute dangerous recidivism, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to one hundred thousand leva.
  - (5) When the object of the crime is in particularly large amounts and the case is particularly serious, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from fifty thousand to five hundred thousand leva.
  - (6) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In minor cases, the punishment is imprisonment up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
  - (7) (Former para. 6 - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if missing or alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

#### Art. 236.

(Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 86 of 1991, amended - SG, No. 85 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever destroys or damages in any way forest trees, young growth, undergrowth, forest plantation or forest nursery, shall be punished with imprisonment up to two years or probation, as well as with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and in particularly serious cases - with imprisonment up to five years.

#### Art. 237.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amended - SG, issue 86 of 1991.) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 85 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever kills or captures large game without proper permission shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year or a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva, as well as deprivation of the right under Art. 37, Para. 1, Item 7.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amended - SG, issue 86 of 1991, amended - SG, issue 85 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever, without possessing a hunting license, kills or captures small game specifically indicated in the Hunting Act, as well as one who, although possessing a hunting license, kills or captures such game during a prohibited time, in a prohibited place or with prohibited means, shall be punished with probation of up to six months or a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva, as well as deprivation of the right under Art. 37, Para. 1, Item 7.

(3) The killed or captured game shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if missing or alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

#### Art. 238.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amended - SG, issue 86 of 1991, amended - SG, issue 55 of 2018) (1) Whoever catches, captures fish and/or other aquatic organisms through the use of explosive, poisonous or stunning substances shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year and a fine of ten thousand to fifteen thousand leva or with probation.

(2) Whoever catches, captures fish and/or other aquatic organisms:

- 1. with technical devices and facilities for catching with electric current without a valid permit;
- 2. with fishing nets in sites other than the Black Sea, the Danube River and the sites for which the person has registration under Art. 25 of the Fisheries and Aquaculture Act and has two or more penalty decrees for administrative violations under the Fisheries and Aquaculture Act that have entered into force;
- 3. in prohibited places or during low water;
- 4. in non-industrial waters during the spawning period of fish;
- 5. of species prohibited for catching, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six months and a fine of five thousand to ten thousand leva or with probation.
  - (3) For a crime under Para. 1 and 2, the punishment is imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of fifteen thousand to twenty thousand leva, when:
- 1. it has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission;
- 2. it has been committed in complicity with an employee of the Executive Agency for Fisheries and Aquaculture who has taken advantage of his official position;
- 3. it has been committed repeatedly;
- 4. the subject of the crime is in large amounts.
  - (4) The caught fish, the other caught aquatic organisms, as well as the tools and means with which the crime was committed, shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

#### Art. 239.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 86 of 1991) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 85 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) A manager of an enterprise, company, institution, organization or other official who violates or allows a violation of the rules established by a special law for the preservation of fish and other aquatic organisms and for the proper development of fish breeding in the country shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or with probation, as well as a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) With the same punishment shall be punished any person who releases or discharges into fishery waters contaminated waters or substances which, by their quantity or properties, may harm fish and other aquatic organisms.

#### Art. 240.

(1) (Amended and supplemented - SG, issue 27 of 2009) A foreign national who enters with a vessel into the territorial waters of the Republic of Bulgaria in the Black Sea and carries out commercial fishing without permission from the respective border authorities, if not subject to a more severe punishment under another law, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years, regardless of the liability under the Fisheries and Aquaculture Act.

(2) If this is committed by a group of foreign nationals who are armed, the punishment is imprisonment of up to five years.

(3) (Amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) The caught fish, the other caught aquatic organisms, as well as the tools and means with which the crime was committed, shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

Art. 240a. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever carries out extraction of underground resources without a granted concession or when the effect of the granted concession for extraction has been suspended under the procedure of Art. 68 of the Underground Resources Act or on the basis of the concluded contract shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of one thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(2) With the punishment under Para. 1 shall also be punished one who holds, stores, transports, processes or alienates illegally extracted underground resources.

(3) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine of five thousand to fifty thousand leva, when:

- 1. the subject of the crime is in large amounts;
- 2. the act has been committed in a protected territory or protected zone within the meaning of the Biological Diversity Act;
- 3. for the commission of the act, a technical device or road vehicle has been used;
- 4. the act has been committed through the use of a document with false content, a false or forged document;
- 5. the act has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance;
- 6. the act has been committed with the participation of an official who has taken advantage of his official position;
- 7. the act has been committed by a person who systematically engages in such activity;
- 8. the act has been committed repeatedly.
  - (4) The punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine of ten thousand to one hundred thousand leva, when:
- 1. the subject of the crime is in particularly large amounts and the case is particularly serious;
- 2. the act has been committed by a person who acts on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 3. the act has been committed under the conditions of dangerous recidivism.
  - (5) In minor cases under Para. 1 and 2, the punishment is imprisonment of up to one year or probation, or a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.
  - (6) The subject of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if missing or alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
  - (7) (Declared unconstitutional in the part "and when it is not the property of the perpetrator" by Constitutional Case No. 1 of 2023 - SG, issue 10 of 2023) The vehicle or portable means that served for the transportation or carrying of the subject of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state even when it is not the property of the perpetrator, unless its value clearly does not correspond to the gravity of the crime.
  - (8) Whoever prepares, acquires, keeps or conceals items, materials or instruments, which he knows are intended or have served for the extraction of underground resources without a concession, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years.

### Section III.

Crimes against the customs regime (Title amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995)

#### Art. 241.

(Repealed - SG, issue 50 of 1995)

#### Art. 242.

(Amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Whoever transports across the border of the country goods without the knowledge and permission of the customs authorities, when this is committed: a) by persons who systematically engage in such activity; b) (amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) through the use of a document with false content, of another's, inauthentic or falsified document; c) by an official who is in direct connection with the customs service; d) (amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 26 of 2004, supplemented - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) when potent or toxic substances, explosive substances, weapons or ammunition for firearms, pyrotechnic products, nuclear material, nuclear facilities or other sources of ionizing radiation or components, or precursors therefor, determined by law or by an act of the Council of Ministers, are transported; e) (amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975) goods and items for commercial or production purposes in large amounts; f) (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) by two or more persons who have conspired in advance; g) (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group; h) (new - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) through the transport of a specimen of a protected species from wild flora or fauna or a part thereof, or product. (amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) shall be punished for aggravated smuggling with imprisonment from three to ten years and with a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva;

(2) (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000) Whoever transports across the border of the country narcotic substances and/or their analogues without proper authorization, shall be punished for high-risk narcotic substances with imprisonment from ten to fifteen years and a fine from one hundred thousand to two hundred thousand leva and for risk narcotic substances - with imprisonment from three to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to one hundred thousand leva.

(3) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000) Whoever transports across the border of the country precursors or equipment and materials for the production of narcotic substances without proper authorization, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to ten years and a fine from fifty thousand to one hundred thousand leva.

(4) (New para. 4 - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, para. 3 repealed, former para. 4 - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, former para. 3, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000) When the subject of the smuggling under the preceding paragraphs is in especially large amounts and the case is especially serious or when any of the persons under letter "f" of para. 1 is a customs officer, the punishment is: in the cases under para. 1 - imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from fifty thousand to two hundred thousand leva, and in the cases under para. 2 and 3 - imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years and a fine from two hundred thousand to three hundred thousand leva.

(5) (Former para. 2, amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, former para. 4, amended - No. 89 of 1986, former para. 5, amended - SG, No. 50 of 1995, former para. 4, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) In the cases under para. 1, letters "a", "d" and "e", as well as under para. 2, 3 and 4, the court may instead of a fine impose confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty party.

(6) (Former para. 3, amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, former para. 5, amended - No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, former para. 6, No. 50 of 1995, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, former para. 5, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000) In minor cases under para. 1, 2 and 3, the punishment is a fine up to one thousand leva, imposed by administrative procedure.

(7) (Former para. 4, amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, former para. 6, amended - No. 89 of 1986, former para. 7, No. 50 of 1995, former para. 6 - SG, No. 21 of 2000) The subject of the smuggling shall be forfeited to the state, regardless of whose property it is, and if it is missing or alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded at the respective state retail prices.

(8) (Former para. 5, amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, former para. 7, amended - No. 89 of 1986, former para. 8, No. 50 of 1995, former para. 7 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, declared unconstitutional in the part "and when it is not the property of the perpetrator" by Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 12 of 2021 - SG, No. 84 of 2021) The means of transport or conveyance used for the transportation or carrying of the goods, subject of the smuggling, shall be forfeited to the state even when it is not the property of the perpetrator, unless its value manifestly does not correspond to the gravity of the crime.

(9) (New - SG, No. 41 of 1985, former para. 8, amended - No. 89 of 1986, former para. 9, amended, No. 50 of 1995, former para. 8, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000) For preparation under para. 2, 3 and 4, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years. In these cases, para. 7 shall apply.

Art. 242a. (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, supplemented - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Whoever transports across the state border goods with documents for transit transport and in violation of the established procedure unloads the goods on the territory of the country, shall be punished with imprisonment up to six years and a fine from fifty thousand to five hundred thousand leva. The goods and the means of transport used for the transport shall be forfeited to the state regardless of whose property they are.

### Section IV.

Crimes against the monetary and credit system

#### Art. 243.

(1) Whoever produces inauthentic or falsifies authentic monetary signs with circulation in the country or abroad, shall be punished for counterfeiting of monetary signs with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on the person who counterfeits:

- 1. tax or postage stamps and
- 2. bonds issued by the state, or other state securities.
- 3. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) payment instruments.

#### Art. 244.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 24 of 2005, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 101 of 2017, supplemented - SG, No. 84 of 2023) Whoever puts into circulation counterfeit monetary or other signs or payment instruments under Art. 243, para. 2, accepts, acquires, transfers or uses such, knowing that they are counterfeit, or transports them across the border of the country or conveys them, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009) The punishment under para. 1 shall be imposed also on a person who holds such signs or payment instruments under Art. 243, para. 2 in large quantities.

Art. 244a. (New - SG, No. 24 of 2005) (1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 101 of 2017) Whoever in violation of the established procedure produces, accepts, acquires, transports across the border of the country or conveys monetary signs with circulation in the country or abroad or monetary signs that have not yet been issued but are intended for release into circulation as legal tender, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) Whoever knowingly puts such monetary signs into circulation, shall be punished with imprisonment up to eight years.

#### Art. 245.

(Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever accepts a counterfeit monetary or other sign under Art. 243 and after learning that it is such, knowingly puts it into circulation as authentic, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years and with a fine up to one thousand leva.

#### Art. 246.

(1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) Preparation for committing a crime under Art. 243 or association for such purpose or for dissemination of counterfeited monetary or other signs or payment instruments under Art. 243, para. 2 is punishable by imprisonment for up to six years.

(2) (Supplemented - SG, issue 24 of 2005) Shall not be punished that accomplice in the association who, before the counterfeiting is completed (if the association was formed for such purpose) or before the dissemination of the counterfeited signs begins (if the association was formed for this) or before completing the preparation of the means under para. 3, withdraws from the act and reports to the authorities.

(3) (Amended and supplemented - SG, issue 24 of 2005, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017, supplemented - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever prepares, receives, acquires, keeps or conceals, transfers across the border of the country, transfers, transports, disseminates or provides objects, materials or instruments, computer programs and data, as well as any other means or elements for protection of monetary signs, knowing that they are intended or that they have been used for producing false monetary signs or for counterfeiting monetary or other signs or payment instruments under Art. 243, para. 2, is punishable by imprisonment for up to six years.

#### Art. 247.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982, corrected issue 31 of 1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2005, repealed - SG, issue 26 of 2010)

#### Art. 248.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective as of 01.01.2005) Whoever knows that a crime under Art. 243 and 244 is being committed and does not report this to the authorities is punishable by probation or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred levs.

(2) The preceding paragraph does not apply to the spouse, descendants, ascendants, brothers and sisters of the perpetrator and their spouses.

Art. 248a. (New - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective as of 13.10.2006) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever, in order to obtain a loan, submits false information is punishable by imprisonment for up to three years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand levs.

(2) (Amended and supplemented - SG, issue 101 of 2017) The same punishment is imposed also on whoever submits false information or conceals information in violation of an obligation to provide such, in order to obtain funds from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by the European Union to the Bulgarian state, as well as funds belonging to the Bulgarian state with which projects financed with funds from these funds are co-financed.

(3) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 101 of 2017) If the act under para. 1 and 2 is committed by a person who manages or represents a legal entity or civil partnership, or by a merchant, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand levs.

(4) With the punishment under para. 3 is also punished the official who authorized the loan or released the funds under para. 2, if they knew that the information submitted was false.

(5) (New - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017) If as a result of the act under para. 2 funds were obtained from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by the European Union to the Bulgarian state, as well as funds belonging to the Bulgarian state with which projects financed with funds from these funds are co-financed, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.

#### Art. 249.

(Amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective as of 13.10.2006) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) Whoever uses a payment instrument or data from a payment instrument without the consent of the holder, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, is punishable by imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine up to twice the amount obtained.

(2) Whoever uses a payment instrument issued in the Republic of Bulgaria or abroad without having coverage for the amount for which the instrument was used is punishable by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine up to twice the amount obtained.

(3) Whoever prepares, installs or uses a technical device to obtain information about the content of a payment instrument is punishable by imprisonment from one to eight years and a fine up to twice the amount obtained.

(4) The same punishment is imposed also on a person who stores or provides to another the information under para. 3.

#### Art. 250.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective as of 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 89 of 1986, repealed, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Previous text of Art. 250 - SG, issue 21 of 2000) Whoever transfers amounts outside the country by bank transfer using a false, altered document or a document with false content is punishable by imprisonment from one to ten years and a fine up to twice the amount of the transfer.

(2) (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective as of 13.10.2006)

(Chapter Seven "Crimes against personal property" with Art. 251 - 268 repealed - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

#### Art. 251.

(Repealed - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017, amended - SG, issue 82 of 2023) Whoever fails to fulfill the obligation to declare monetary funds carried across the border of the country, and the value of the subject of the crime is particularly large, is punishable by imprisonment for up to five years and a fine in the amount of one fifth of the value of the subject of the crime.

(2) (Repealed - SG, issue 83 of 2019, new - SG, issue 82 of 2023) Whoever fails to fulfill the obligation to declare monetary funds carried across the border of the country and their lawful origin is not established is punishable by imprisonment from one to six years, and the funds are confiscated in favor of the state.

#### Art. 252.

(Repealed - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, supplemented - SG, issue 23 of 2009, effective as of 01.11.2009, amended - SG, issue 13 of 2020, effective as of 14.02.2020) Whoever without appropriate authorization engages as an occupation in banking, insurance or other financial transactions, provides payment services or issues electronic money for which such authorization is required is punishable by imprisonment from three to five years and confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997) When the activity under para. 1 has caused significant damages to another or significant unlawful income has been obtained, the punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand levs, and the court may also order confiscation of part or all of the perpetrator's property.

(3) The punishment under para. 2 is imposed also on whoever, when conducting banking activity with authorization, uses funds acquired in violation of established provisions.

Chapter Seven.

CRIMES AGAINST THE FINANCIAL, TAX AND INSURANCE SYSTEMS (TITLE AMENDED - SG, ISSUE 62 OF 1997, AMENDED - SG, ISSUE 51 OF 2000)


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Rules | > Section IV.

#### Art. 253.

(Repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993, new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 85 of 1998, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004, supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever performs a financial operation or transaction with property, or conceals the origin, location, movement or actual rights over property, which he knows or suspects to have been acquired through a crime or other socially dangerous act, shall be punished for money laundering with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva.

(2) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004, supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) The penalty under para. 1 shall also be imposed on anyone who acquires, receives, holds, uses, converts or assists in any way in the conversion of property, which he knows or suspects at the time of receiving it to have been acquired through a crime or other socially dangerous act.

(3) (Previous para. 2, supplemented - SG, No. 26 of 2004) The penalty is imprisonment from one to eight years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva, when the act under para. 1 and 2 is committed:

- 1. (amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) by two or more persons who have conspired in advance, or by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 2. two or more times;
- 3. by an official within the scope of his duties;
- 4. (new - SG, No. 26 of 2004) by opening or maintaining an account in a financial institution under a fictitious name or under the name of a person who has not given his consent for this;
- 5. (new - SG, No. 103 of 2020) by an obliged person under Art. 4 of the Law on Measures Against Money Laundering or his employee or worker in or on the occasion of fulfilling obligations under the same law.
  - (4) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, previous para. 3, supplemented - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) The penalty is imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from twenty thousand to two hundred thousand leva, when the act under para. 1 and 2 is committed with funds or property, which the perpetrator knew or suspected to have been acquired through a serious intentional crime.
  - (5) (New - SG, No. 85 of 1998, previous para. 3 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, previous para. 4, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) When the funds or property are in particularly large amounts and the case is particularly serious, the penalty is imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva, and the court deprives the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
  - (6) (New - SG, No. 85 of 1998, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, previous para. 5, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) The object of the crime or the property into which it has been transformed shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
  - (7) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) The provisions of para. 1 - 6 shall also apply when the crime through which the property was acquired does not fall under the criminal jurisdiction of the Republic of Bulgaria.

Art. 253a. (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) (1) Preparation for money laundering or association for such purpose shall be punished with imprisonment up to two years or with a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) The same penalty shall also be imposed on anyone who incites another to money laundering.

(3) Property intended for money laundering shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

(4) A participant in the association under para. 1 who, before the money laundering is completed, terminates his participation in the association and reports it to the authorities shall not be punished.

Art. 253b. (New - SG, No. 85 of 1998, previous text of Art. 253a, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004, supplemented - SG, No. 84 of 2023) An official who violates or fails to comply with the provisions of the Law on Measures Against Money Laundering or the Law on Measures Against the Financing of Terrorism, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished in non-negligible cases with imprisonment up to three years and with a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.

#### Art. 254.

(Repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993, new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, repealed - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006)

Art. 254a. (New - SG, No. 51 of 2000) (1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An official who, in violation of a budget law or a subordinate act implementing it, disposes of budgetary funds or funds with a designated purpose not according to their intended purpose, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years or with probation, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.

(2) If harmful consequences for the state or municipality have resulted from the act under para. 1, the penalty is imprisonment up to five years and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.

(3) (Repealed - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006)

(4) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) In the cases under para. 1, the perpetrator shall not be punished if, before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court, the unlawful administrative act is revoked and the unlawfully expended funds are fully reimbursed. This provision shall not be applied again.

Art. 254b. (New - SG, No. 24 of 2005) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever uses received financial funds from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by the European Union to the Bulgarian state not according to their intended purpose, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) If an official orders the act under the previous paragraph to be committed, the penalty is imprisonment from two to eight years, and the court may deprive the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

#### Art. 255.

(Repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993, new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, in force from 06.11.1997, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) (1) Whoever avoids the determination or payment of tax obligations in large amounts by:

- 1. failing to submit a declaration;
- 2. affirming an untruth or concealing the truth in a submitted declaration;
- 3. failing to issue an invoice or other accounting document;
- 4. destroying, concealing or failing to preserve within the legally established terms accounting documents or accounting registers;
- 5. maintaining or permitting the maintenance of accounting in violation of the requirements of the accounting legislation;
- 6. preparing or using a document with false content, a false or forged document in conducting business activity, in maintaining accounting or in presenting information before the revenue authorities or public enforcement agents;
- 7. deducting an undue tax credit, (amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine up to two thousand leva.
  - (2) (Amended - SG, No. 67 of 2008, amended - SG, No. 12 of 2009, in force from 01.01.2010, amendment regarding entry into force - SG, No. 32 of 2009) When the act under para. 1 is committed with the participation of an official from the border police, customs administration, the National Revenue Agency, or a registered auditor, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to six years and a fine up to five thousand leva, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
  - (3) When the tax obligations are in especially large amounts, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to eight years and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator.
  - (4) If before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the undeclared or unpaid tax obligation is paid into the budget together with the interest, the punishment under para. 1 and 2 shall be deprivation of liberty up to two years and a fine up to five hundred leva, and under para. 3 - deprivation of liberty up to three years and a fine up to one thousand leva.

Art. 255a. (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) (1) Whoever avoids the determination or payment of tax obligations in large amounts through the transformation of a commercial company or other legal entity, through conducting a transaction with a commercial enterprise or through conducting a transaction with related parties within the meaning of the Tax and Social Security Procedure Code, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine up to ten thousand leva.

(2) When the tax obligations are in especially large amounts, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to eight years and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator.

(3) If before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the undeclared or unpaid tax obligation is paid into the budget together with the interest, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty up to three years and a fine up to one thousand leva.

Art. 255b. (New - SG, No. 107 of 2014, in force from 01.01.2015) (1) Whoever conceals mandatory insurance contributions for state social insurance or for health insurance in large amounts by:

- 1. declaring insurable income in a smaller amount than the actual for the insured person;
- 2. failing to submit a declaration;
- 3. affirming an untruth or concealing the truth in a submitted declaration;
- 4. preparing or using a document with false content, a false or forged document in conducting business activity, in maintaining accounting or in presenting information before the revenue authorities;
- 5. destroying or concealing within the legally established terms accounting documents, accounting registers or payroll statements; shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to five years and a fine up to two thousand leva.
  - (2) When the act under para. 1 is committed with the participation of a revenue authority or a registered auditor, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine up to five thousand leva, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
  - (3) When the obligations for mandatory insurance contributions for state social insurance or for health insurance are in especially large amounts, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to eight years and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator.
  - (4) If before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the mandatory insurance contributions for state social insurance or for health insurance are paid into the budget together with the interest, the punishment under para. 1 and 2 shall be deprivation of liberty up to two years and a fine up to five hundred leva, and under para. 3 - deprivation of liberty up to three years and a fine up to one thousand leva.
  - (5) The worker or employee subject to mandatory insurance does not bear criminal liability under para. 1 - 4, including for incitement and aiding.

#### Art. 256.

(Repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993, new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) (1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 103 of 2020, in force from 01.01.2021) Whoever through the use of a document with false content or of a false or forged document obtains from the state budget, from the budget of the state social insurance or from the budget of the National Health Insurance Fund an undue monetary amount in large amounts or enables another person to obtain such amount, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from two to eight years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.

(2) When the act under para. 1 is committed with the participation of a person under Art. 255, para. 2 or by a person acting on the orders of or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, or if the obtained monetary amount is in especially large amounts, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2020, in force from 01.01.2021) If before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the obtained amount is paid into the state budget, into the budget of the state social insurance or into the budget of the National Health Insurance Fund together with the interest, the punishment under para. 1 shall be deprivation of liberty up to three years and a fine up to one thousand leva, and under para. 2 - deprivation of liberty up to five years and a fine up to three thousand leva.

#### Art. 257.

(Repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993, new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, in force from 06.11.1997, repealed - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)

#### Art. 258.

(Repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993, new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) Whoever unlawfully obstructs a revenue authority from fulfilling its legal obligation shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to three years and a fine from one thousand to two thousand leva.

(2) If the act under para. 1 is committed through force or threat, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva.

#### Art. 259.

(Repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993, new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever creates a legal entity with an ideal purpose or establishes a foundation that does not carry out or carries out fictitiously the activity and purpose declared upon registration, in order to obtain under its cover credits, to be exempted from taxes, to receive tax relief or to obtain other property benefit, as well as to conduct prohibited activity, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to three years, a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

Art. 259a. (New - SG, No. 51 of 2000, declared unconstitutional by Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 14 of 2000 - SG, No. 98 of 2000) (1) An official who authorizes the payment of remuneration without all due mandatory insurance contributions having been paid, if the unpaid amount is in large amounts, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to three years.

(2) In the cases under para. 1 the perpetrator shall not be punished if before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court he fulfills his obligation in full together with the due interest.

#### Чл. 260.

(Revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997) (1) A licensed appraiser who gives a false appraisal or opinion on the value of property being appraised and from this results damage in non-trivial cases, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to three years and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 67 of 2008) A registered auditor who certifies a false annual financial statement of a trader, knowing this, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to one year and a fine up to five hundred leva, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

Чл. 260а. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever acquires or disposes for their own account or for the account of a third party, directly or indirectly, of financial instruments, by unlawfully using inside information relating to them, and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred, when committed by a person who possesses inside information because:

- 1. they are a member of the administrative, management or supervisory body of the issuer or participant in the emissions allowances market, or
- 2. they have share participation in the capital of an issuer or participant in the emissions allowances market, or
- 3. they have access to the information through the performance of an activity, profession or official duties, shall be punished for insider dealing with deprivation of liberty up to four years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva, and the court may order deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
  - (2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever acquires or disposes for their own account or for the account of a third party, directly or indirectly, of financial instruments, by unlawfully using inside information relating to them, acquired outside the cases under para. 1, which they know to be inside information, and from this act significant harmful consequences have occurred.
  - (3) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever, after having submitted an order for a financial instrument, receives inside information relating to the financial instrument, and on the basis of it cancels or modifies it and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred.
  - (4) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever, after having submitted an order or bid for their own account or for the account of a third party, in auction sales of emissions allowances or other auction products based on them, which are conducted pursuant to Commission Regulation (EU) 1031/2010 of 12 November 2010 on the timing, administration and other aspects of auctioning of greenhouse gas emission allowances pursuant to Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council establishing a scheme for greenhouse gas emission allowance trading within the Community (OJ, L 302/1 of 18 November 2010), receives inside information relating to them, and on the basis of it modifies or cancels the order, or modifies or cancels the bid and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred.
