  - (5) Whoever on the basis of inside information induces another person to acquire or dispose of financial instruments, to modify or cancel an order for a financial instrument to which the inside information relates, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to four years.
  - (6) The punishment under para. 5 shall also be imposed on whoever knows that they have been induced on the basis of inside information and submits, modifies or cancels an order for a financial instrument to which the information relates, and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred.
  - (7) If the act under para. 1 - 6 is committed:
- 1. by two or more persons who have conspired in advance;
- 2. by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 3. repeatedly, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to five years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
  - (8) In the cases under para. 1 - 7 the object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and when it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

Чл. 260б. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever unlawfully discloses to another person inside information which they possess regarding a financial instrument, except when the disclosure is made in the ordinary performance of an activity, profession or official duties, or constitutes market research, and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred, shall be punished for unlawful disclosure of inside information with deprivation of liberty up to two years.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever discloses an inducement when they know that the inducement is based on inside information, and from this disclosure significant harmful consequences have occurred.

Чл. 260в. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 83 of 2019) Whoever enters into transactions or places orders to trade or performs another type of activity or action, by giving false or misleading signals regarding the supply, demand or price of a financial instrument or related spot contract for commodities or sets this price at an abnormal or artificial level, and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred, shall be punished for market manipulation with deprivation of liberty up to four years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.

(2) Whoever through the use of an artificial device or through misleading enters into transactions or places orders to trade, or performs another type of activity or action, which affects the price of one or more financial instruments or related spot contract for commodities, and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from two to four years.

(3) Whoever with the aim of securing an advantage or obtaining a benefit for themselves or for another disseminates information through media or in another way, which gives false or misleading signals regarding the supply, demand or price of a financial instrument or related spot contract for commodities, or sets for one or more financial instruments or related spot contract for commodities a price at an abnormal or artificial level and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to four years and a fine from three to five thousand leva.

(4) Whoever provides false or misleading information or input data or in any other way manipulates the calculation of a benchmark and from this significant harmful consequences have occurred, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from one to four years.

(5) In the cases under para. 1 - 4 the object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and when it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

#### Чл. 261.

(Revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

#### Чл. 262.

(Revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

#### Чл. 263.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

#### Чл. 264.

(Revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

#### Чл. 265.

(Revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

#### Чл. 266.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

#### Чл. 267.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

#### Чл. 268.

(Revoked - SG, issue 10 of 1993)

Глава осма.

CRIMES AGAINST THE ACTIVITY OF STATE BODIES, PUBLIC ORGANIZATIONS AND PERSONS PERFORMING PUBLIC FUNCTIONS (TITLE AMENDED - SG, ISSUE 62 OF 1997, AMENDED - SG, ISSUE 43 OF 2005, IN FORCE FROM 01.09.2005)

### Раздел I.

Crimes against the order of government

#### Чл. 269.

(Amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever uses force or threat with the aim of compelling an authority, a public representative, a private bailiff or assistant private bailiff to perform or omit something in office or related to their function, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to six years.

(2) When the crime under para. 1 is committed by participants in a crowd, the instigators and ringleaders shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.

#### Art. 270.

(Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993) (1) (Previous text of Art. 270 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 43 of 2005, effective 01.09.2005, amended - SG, No. 64 of 2007, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully obstructs an authority, private bailiff or assistant private bailiff from performing their duties shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or a fine of five hundred to two thousand leva.

(2) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) When the duties of the authority are related to control over trafficking of narcotic substances, analogues or precursors, the punishment is imprisonment for up to five years and a fine of ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.

Art. 270а. (New - SG, No. 26 of 1973, repealed - SG, No. 89 of 1986)

#### Art. 271.

(Repealed - SG, No. 99 of 1989)

#### Art. 272.

(Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004) Whoever arbitrarily leaves a populated place despite a prohibition established by proper administrative procedure shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months or probation.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004) Whoever, after appropriate warning, continues systematically to violate the legally established measures of administrative supervision taken against him shall be punished by probation for a term of one year.

#### Art. 273.

(New - SG, No. 102 of 2006) Whoever discloses information establishing affiliation with State Security or the intelligence services of the Bulgarian People's Army, in violation of the Law on Access to and Disclosure of Documents and on Announcing Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens with State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to six years and a fine of fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva.

#### Art. 274.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever arbitrarily performs an act that falls within the scope of the office of an official which he does not hold or from which he has been deprived shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or probation.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on one who arbitrarily performs an act that falls within the scope of the function of a public representative with which he has not been entrusted or from which he has been deprived, and thereby unlawfully affects public or private interests.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever, without having the right, wears uniform clothing or an official badge shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or probation, as well as by public reprimand.

Art. 274а. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amended and supplemented - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever possesses or wears uniform clothing, official badges or inscriptions in violation of the Law on the Ministry of Internal Affairs, the Law on Defense and the Armed Forces of the Republic of Bulgaria, the Law on Execution of Punishments and Detention, or the Law on the Judicial System shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or probation.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) If the act under para. 1 is committed with the purpose of committing another crime, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years.

(3) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever in any way stains, unlawfully destroys or damages uniform clothing, official badges or inscriptions worn or placed in accordance with the Law on the Ministry of Internal Affairs, the Law on Defense and the Armed Forces of the Republic of Bulgaria, the Law on Execution of Punishments and Detention, or the Law on the Judicial System shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

#### Art. 275.

(Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, supplemented - SG, No. 43 of 2005, effective 01.09.2005, amended - SG, No. 64 of 2007) Whoever, being obliged by law to give assistance to an authority, private bailiff or assistant private bailiff, does not do so after being duly summoned shall be punished by probation or a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever, being summoned by a competent official in a case dangerous to the life, health or property of someone, refuses to come to the assistance which he can give without danger to himself or to another shall be punished by probation or a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Art. 276.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever counterfeits or puts into circulation counterfeit official authenticating marks, such as seals, marks for the quality of precious metals, entrance tickets, transport tickets and other such, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or probation.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever knowingly uses such a counterfeit mark shall be punished by a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever unlawfully takes away, destroys or conceals official authenticating marks intended for establishing, paying or accounting for values, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or probation.

(4) (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever without proper authorization makes a seal of a state or public organization shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or probation.

(5) The same punishment shall be imposed on one who takes away a seal of a state or public organization with the purpose of using it unlawfully.

#### Art. 277.

(Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 43 of 2005, effective from 01.09.2005, amended - SG, No. 64 of 2007) Whoever knowingly removes or damages a seal placed lawfully by an authority, a private bailiff or an assistant private bailiff on movable or immovable property as a sign that access to it or disposition of it is restricted, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever disposes of property placed under seizure and left in his custody.

Art. 277а. (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, effective from 10.04.2009) (1) Whoever without appropriate authorization searches for archaeological objects shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.

(2) Whoever without appropriate authorization conducts or orders to be conducted field archaeological excavations, geophysical or underwater surveys, or not in accordance with the legally established procedure conducts excavation works on the territory of an immovable cultural property or in its protection zone, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years and by a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(3) If in committing the acts under para. 1 and 2 technical means or motor vehicles are used, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from five thousand to fifty thousand leva.

(4) Whoever orders or allows the carrying out of unlawful activity in a protected area for the preservation of cultural heritage shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and by a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva.

(5) Whoever continues, orders or allows to be continued the activity under para. 4 after it has been stopped by the competent authorities shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and by a fine from three thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(6) With the punishments under para. 4 and 5 shall also be punished whoever organizes or manages the activity, if he knew or assumed that it is carried out in violation of the Cultural Heritage Act.

(7) Whoever unlawfully produces, possesses or conceals objects, materials, tools or computer programs which he knows or assumes are intended or have been used for searching, storage, modification or transfer of archaeological objects shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

(8) In the cases under para. 1 - 6 the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person, and under para. 7 - of part or all of the property of the guilty person.

#### Art. 278.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) (1) Whoever discovers a cultural value and within seven days does not notify the authorities shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or a fine of five hundred to three thousand leva.

(2) When the cultural value has particularly high scientific or artistic value, the punishment is imprisonment of up to four years or a fine of one thousand to five thousand leva.

(3) If destruction or damage to the cultural value has ensued, when the perpetrator did not intend or allow this, the punishment is: in cases under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to four years and a fine of one thousand to five thousand leva, and in cases under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of two thousand to ten thousand leva.

(4) The perpetrator shall not be punished when he reports the discovered cultural value even after the period under para. 1, if the consequences under para. 3 have not occurred.

(5) An official who does not request identification and registration of a cultural value shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

(6) Whoever possesses an archaeological object that has not been identified and registered according to the appropriate procedure shall be punished by imprisonment of up to four years and a fine of two thousand to ten thousand leva, and when the object of the crime represents national wealth, as well as when more than three archaeological objects are held - by imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of three thousand to fifteen thousand leva. The court may also impose confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

(7) The object of the crime shall be seized in favor of the state.

Art. 278а. (New - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) (1) Whoever offers for alienation or alienates a cultural value that has not been identified and registered shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of one thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever acquires such a cultural value.

(3) When the acts under para. 1 and 2 are committed repeatedly or constitute dangerous recidivism, or are committed by order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, as well as when they are committed with the aim that the object of the crime be taken across the borders of the country, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine of five thousand to fifty thousand leva.

(4) The same punishment shall also be imposed on whoever, without appropriate permission, takes a cultural value outside the borders of the country.

(5) The punishment under para. 1 - 4 shall also be imposed when the object of the crime is a document from the National Archival Fund.

(6) The object of the crime shall be seized in favor of the state, and when it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

Art. 278б. (New - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) (1) Whoever unlawfully destroys or damages his own cultural value or document from the National Archival Fund shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or a fine of five hundred to two thousand leva, as well as public censure.

(2) An official who unlawfully gives permission for destruction, demolition, damage, modification or export of a cultural value or a document from the National Archival Fund shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years or a fine of one thousand to five thousand leva, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

(3) If from the act under para. 2 destruction, demolition, damage, modification or export of the cultural value has ensued, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years, a fine of one thousand to five thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

Art. 278б.SUP>1. (New - SG, issue 1 of 2019) (1) Whoever, with the aim of obtaining for himself or another a property benefit, falsifies a work of painting, sculpture, graphics or archaeological objects or items, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year and a fine of two thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who offers for sale or puts into circulation as authentic works of painting, sculpture, graphics or archaeological objects.

(3) A person who gives a false conclusion certifying the authenticity of works of painting, sculpture, graphics or archaeological objects or items shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year and a fine of three thousand to thirty thousand leva.

(4) If the act is committed by a person in the exercise of commercial activity, the punishment is imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of ten thousand to fifty thousand leva, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

(5) The object of the crime shall be seized in favor of the state.

Art. 278в. (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 86 of 1991, former Art. 278а - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever unlawfully destroys or damages a protected territory or habitat - subject of protection in a protected zone, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation, as well as a fine of two thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) Whoever destroys or damages exceptionally valuable unique and irreplaceable earth and rock formations and caves declared protected shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of two thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(3) When the act under para. 1 or 2 is committed by negligence, the punishment is probation and a fine of one thousand to five thousand leva.
Art. 278г. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever unlawfully destroys, damages, acquires, possesses or alienates a specimen of a protected species of wild flora or fauna, when it does not constitute a minor case, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation, as well as a fine of two thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) Whoever trades in specimens of protected species of wild flora or fauna or their parts or products, when it does not constitute a minor case, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of two thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(3) When the act under para. 1 or 2 is committed by negligence, the punishment is probation and a fine of one thousand to five thousand leva.
Art. 278д. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) Whoever unlawfully destroys, damages, possesses, acquires or alienates a specimen of European or globally endangered wild vertebrate animals or a specimen of a species under Annex No. 3 to the Biological Diversity Act, marked with the sign (*), shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years, as well as a fine of five thousand to twenty thousand leva.

#### Art. 279.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever enters or exits through the border of the country without permission from the competent authorities or even with permission, but not through the designated places, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to six years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.

(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) If the act under para. 1 has been committed repeatedly or constitutes a dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to eight years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.

(3) (Предишна ал. 2, изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) In the cases under the preceding paragraphs, the court may instead of a fine impose confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty party.

(4) (Предишна ал. 3, изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Preparation for a crime under para. 1 and 2 shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years or probation.

(5) (Предишна ал. 4 - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) Shall not be punished the one who enters the country to avail himself of the right to asylum in accordance with the Constitution.

#### Art. 280.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., отм. бр. 37 от 1989 г., нов - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever transports across the border of the country individual persons or groups of people without permission from the competent authorities or even with permission, but not through the designated places, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to eight years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years, a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator, if:

- 1. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) a minor or underage person has been transported across the border of the country;
- 2. the transport was done without the knowledge of the person;
- 3. the person transported across the border is not a Bulgarian citizen;
- 4. a motor, aircraft or other means of transport has been used;
- 5. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) the transport is organized by a group or organization or is carried out by order and/or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 6. (нова - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) the transport was carried out in a manner dangerous to the life of the transported persons;
- 7. (нова - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) the act is accompanied by resistance and/or disobedience toward an authority;
- 8. (нова - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) the act is committed by destroying, undermining or damaging a border protection facility.
  - (3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years, a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator, when the act is committed with the participation of an official who has taken advantage of his official position.
  - (4) (Предишна ал. 3, доп. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., обявена за противоконституционна в частта "или му е предоставено доброволно" с РКС № 12 от 2021 г. - ДВ, бр. 84 от 2021 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) If the act under para. 1 and 2 has been committed repeatedly or constitutes a dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years, a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator.
  - (5) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) In the cases under para. 2, item 5, the vehicle shall be forfeited in favor of the state if it is the property of the perpetrator, or a fine shall be imposed in the amount of the market value of the vehicle in leva when it is not his property.

#### Art. 281.

(1) (Отм. - ДВ, бр. 37 от 1989 г., нов - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г., предишен текст на чл. 281, изм. и доп. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) Whoever in any way unlawfully assists a foreigner to reside or pass through the country in violation of the law shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from three thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to ten years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva when the act is:

- 1. committed by using a motor, aircraft or other means of transport;
- 2. (доп. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) organized by a group or organization or by order and/or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 3. committed in a manner dangerous to the life of the person;
- 4. (изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) committed in relation to a minor or underage person;
- 5. committed in relation to more than one person;
- 6. (нова - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) committed with the purpose of the perpetrator obtaining for himself or for another a material benefit;
- 7. (нова - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) accompanied by resistance and/or disobedience toward an authority.
  - (3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г., обявена за противоконституционна в частта "или му е предоставено доброволно" с РКС № 12 от 2021 г. - ДВ, бр. 84 от 2021 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) If the act under para. 1 and 2 has been committed repeatedly or constitutes a dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva.
  - (4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) In the cases under para. 2, item 1, the vehicle shall be forfeited in favor of the state if it is the property of the perpetrator, or a fine shall be imposed in the amount of the market value of the vehicle in leva when it is not his property.

### Section II.

Crimes in office

#### Art. 282.

(Amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) An official who violates or fails to perform their official duties, or exceeds their authority or rights with the purpose of obtaining for themselves or for another a benefit or causing harm to another, and from which significant harmful consequences may occur, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years, and the court may also impose deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6, or probation.

(2) (Amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) If the act has resulted in substantial harmful consequences or has been committed by a person holding a responsible official position, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years, and the court may also impose deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

(3) (New - SG No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) For particularly serious cases under the preceding paragraph, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years, and the court shall also impose deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

(4) (New - SG No. 62 of 1997) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed on an official who has committed the crime with the participation of a person referred to in Art. 142, para. 2, items 6 and 8.

(5) (New - SG No. 21 of 2000) If the act under the preceding paragraphs is related to the exercise of control over the production, processing, storage, trade in the country, import, export, transit and accounting of narcotic substances and precursors, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to ten years under para. 1 and from three to fifteen years under para. 2.

Art. 282a. (New - SG No. 62 of 1997) An official who, in the presence of the conditions provided for in a normative act necessary for the issuance of a special permit for the performance of a specific activity, refuses or delays beyond the time limits provided by law its issuance, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years, a fine of up to five hundred leva and deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

#### Art. 283.

(Amended - SG No. 26 of 1973, amended - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) An official who uses their official position to obtain for themselves or for another an unlawful benefit shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.

Art. 283a. (New - SG No. 62 of 1997) If the crimes under Art. 282 and 283 are related to privatization, sale, leasing or tenancy, as well as contribution to commercial companies of state, municipal and cooperative property, as well as property of legal entities, the punishment shall be:

- 1. under Art. 282 - deprivation of liberty from three to ten years, a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7;
- 2. under Art. 283 - deprivation of liberty from one to three years, a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

Art. 283b. (New - SG No. 62 of 1997, supplemented - SG No. 92 of 2002) An official who hinders or obstructs the exercise by the owners of their rights restored under the Law on Restoration of Ownership of Nationalized Immovable Property, under the Law on Restoration of Ownership of Certain Expropriated Properties under the Law on Territorial and Settlement Planning, the Law on Planned Construction of Populated Places, the Law on Development of Populated Places, the Law on State Property and the Law on Ownership and under the Law on Ownership and Use of Agricultural Land and the Law on Compensation of Owners of Nationalized Property, the Law on Privatization and Post-Privatization Control or through final judicial acts under another law, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from two to six years.

#### Art. 284.

(Amended - SG No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) An official who, to the detriment of the state, an enterprise, an organization or a private person, discloses to another or publishes information that has been entrusted to them or is accessible to them by virtue of their office and which they know constitutes an official secret, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years or probation.

(2) The punishment for an act under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a non-official person working in a state institution, enterprise or public organization, to whom information constituting an official secret has become known in connection with their work.

(3) (Amended - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amended - SG No. 9 of 2021, effective 06.02.2021) If the act under para. 1 has been committed by an expert, interpreter or Bulgarian sign language interpreter with respect to information that has become known to them in connection with the task assigned to them, which they are obliged to keep secret, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to two years or probation.

Art. 284a. (New - SG No. 102 of 2006) An official from the composition of the Commission for Disclosure of Documents and Announcing Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens to State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army or from its administration, who discloses information or disseminates a document in violation of the Law on Access to and Disclosure of the Documents and on Announcing Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens to State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army, which have become known to them in that capacity, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to six years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva.

Art. 284b. (New - SG No. 102 of 2006) An official who fails to remove from record an undercover agent or employee holding a public office or performing a public activity within the meaning of the Law on Access to and Disclosure of the Documents and on Announcing Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens to State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to six years and a fine of fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva.

Art. 284v. (New - SG No. 27 of 2009) An official who unlawfully authorizes or orders the use of special intelligence means, or applies them, or stores information obtained through them, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

#### Art. 285.

An official who knowingly allows a person subordinate to them to commit a crime related to their service or work shall be punished with the punishment provided for the committed crime.

### Section III.

Crimes against justice

#### Art. 286.

(1) (Amended - SG No. 62 of 1997) Whoever falsely accuses someone of a crime before a competent authority, knowing that they are innocent, or presents false evidence against them, shall be punished for false accusation by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and public reprimand.

(2) (Previous para. 3 - SG No. 62 of 1997) (Amended - SG No. 62 of 1997) If the falsely accused person is brought to criminal responsibility, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to ten years.

#### Art. 287.

(Amend. - SG No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An official who, during or on the occasion of performing their service, alone or through another, commits unlawful coercive actions against an accused, witness or expert witness, in order to extract a confession, testimony, conclusion or information from them, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years and by deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

Art. 287a. (New - SG No. 62 of 1997) Whoever, with the purpose of misleading an organ of the judiciary:

- 1. prepares false technical records or alters genuine ones;
- 2. destroys a record or part of it, collects or arranges data from the record and thereby creates a false impression of the relevant circumstance;
- 3. uses forged technical records;
- 4. unlawfully uses information acquired by special intelligence means, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand leva.

#### Art. 288.

(Amend. - SG No. 50 of 1995, amend. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An organ of authority who fails to timely perform the duties imposed upon them by their service regarding criminal prosecution, or otherwise obstructs such prosecution with the purpose of saving another from the punishment due to them by law, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and by deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

#### Art. 289.

(Amend. - SG No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG No. 26 of 2010) Whoever induces an official from the investigative organs or from the prosecutorial or judicial organs to violate their official duty in connection with the administration of justice, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years.

#### Art. 290.

(1) Whoever before a court or before another competent organ of authority, as a witness, orally or in writing, knowingly confirms an untruth or conceals the truth, shall be punished for perjury by deprivation of liberty up to five years.

(2) (Amend. - SG No. 9 of 2021, effective from 06.02.2021) The same punishment shall be imposed on an interpreter or interpreter of Bulgarian sign language who, before a court or before another competent organ of authority, in writing or orally, knowingly gave a false translation.

Art. 290a. (New - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) Whoever confirms an untruth or conceals the truth in a written declaration submitted before a court, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to three years.

#### Art. 291.

(1) (Amend. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever, as an expert witness, before a court or before another competent organ of authority, orally or in writing, knowingly gave a false conclusion, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years and by deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

(2) (Amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed by negligence, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to one year or probation. The court may also order deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

#### Art. 292.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 292 - SG No. 89 of 1986) For a crime under Art. 290 and 291, punishability ceases:

- 1. when the person, if they tell the truth, would accuse themselves of a crime and
- 2. (amend. - SG No. 9 of 2021, effective from 06.02.2021) when the person retracts before the competent organ their false testimony, translation or conclusion before the sentence or decision enters into force and before criminal prosecution has been initiated against them for this.
  - (2) (New - SG No. 89 of 1986) The provision of item 2 of the preceding paragraph shall also apply in the cases under Art. 290a, when the person withdraws their declaration before the decision on the case for which it was submitted has been issued.

#### Art. 293.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 293, suppl. - SG No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever incites another to commit a crime under Art. 290, 290a and 291, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to one year or by probation.

(2) (New - SG No. 89 of 1986) If two or more persons are incited and the case is particularly serious, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to three years.

Art. 293a. (New - SG No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever, after being sentenced to fulfill a monetary obligation by a judicial act that has entered into force and within one year from its entry into force, does not fulfill their obligation to the creditor, despite having monetary means or property for this, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to one year or by probation.

#### Art. 294.

(1) (Amend. - SG No. 62 of 1997) Whoever assists a person who has committed a crime to escape or to have criminal prosecution against them obstructed or to remain unpunished, without having agreed with that person before they committed the crime itself, shall be punished for personal harboring by deprivation of liberty up to five years, but not with a heavier punishment than that provided for the harbored person.

(2) If this is committed with the purpose of material gain, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to five years, but not heavier than that provided for the harbored person.

(3) The above provisions do not apply to the spouses, descendants, ascendants, brothers and sisters of the harbored person and their spouses.

(4) (New - SG No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG No. 27 of 2009) If the perpetrator is a judge, prosecutor, investigator or police organ, investigating police officer, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.

#### Art. 295.

(Amend. - SG No. 50 of 1995, amend. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An organ of authority who, with the purpose of saving another from punishment or delaying the execution of the punishment, fails to execute a sentence that has entered into force, if by their service they were obliged to do what is necessary for bringing the sentence into execution, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to six years, and the court may deprive them of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6, or by probation.

#### Art. 296.

(Amend. - SG No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Suppl. - SG No. 27 of 2009, amend. - SG No. 102 of 2009, effective from 22.12.2009, suppl. - SG No. 41 of 2015, effective from 06.07.2015) Whoever obstructs or prevents in any way the execution of a court decision or does not comply with a protection order for domestic violence or a European Protection Order, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to three years or a fine up to five thousand leva.

(2) Whoever, with the purpose of obstructing or preventing the execution of a court decision, destroys, damages, conceals or alienates a thing to which this decision relates, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to three years or a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime.

(3) (New - SG No. 60 of 2011) Whoever violates a ban on attending sports events in the country or abroad, within the period of the ban imposed under the procedure of the Law on the Protection of Public Order during the Conduct of Sports Events, after having been punished for the same act by administrative procedure, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to three years or by probation.

(4) (New - SG No. 16 of 2019) If the act under para. 1 is committed repeatedly, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to five years.

#### Art. 297.

(1) A prisoner who escapes shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to three years.

(2) If for the escape the prisoner made use of digging under, demolishing, destroying walls, doors, windows and others, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to five years.

(3) A prisoner is anyone detained in custody according to the procedure established by law.

#### Art. 298.

(1) Prisoners who have conspired to escape through joint efforts shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to two years.

(2) If the prisoners proceed to the execution of the intended escape, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to five years.

#### Art. 299.

(Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) An official who arbitrarily releases or allows a prisoner to escape shall be punished with imprisonment for up to five years, and the court may deprive the guilty party of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

#### Art. 300.

(Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, repealed - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005)

### Section IV.

Bribery

#### Art. 301.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) An official who requests or accepts a gift or any benefit to which they are not entitled, or accepts an offer or promise of a gift or benefit, in order to perform or not perform an official act or because they have performed or not performed such an act, shall be punished for bribery with imprisonment for up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) If the official has committed any of the acts under para. 1 in order to violate or because they have violated their duties, when such violation does not constitute a crime, the punishment is imprisonment for up to eight years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) If the official has committed any of the acts under para. 1 in order to commit or because they have committed another crime in connection with their duties, the punishment is imprisonment for up to ten years and a fine of up to fifteen thousand leva.

(4) (Amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) In the cases under the preceding paragraphs, the court shall also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

(5) (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a foreign official who requests or accepts a bribe or accepts an offer or promise of a bribe.

#### Art. 302. For bribery committed:

1. (supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 26 of 2010) by a person holding a responsible official position, including a judge, lay judge, prosecutor or investigator, or by a police authority, or by an investigating police officer;

2. through extortion by means of abuse of official position;

3. (amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) repeatedly and

4. in large amounts, the punishment is: a) (supplemented - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 51 of 2000, supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) in the cases of Art. 301, para. 1 and 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years, a fine of up to twenty thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7; b) (amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986, supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) in the cases of Art. 301, para. 3 - imprisonment from three to fifteen years, a fine of up to twenty-five thousand leva and confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty party, and the court shall also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

Art. 302a. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) For bribery in especially large amounts, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to thirty years, a fine of up to thirty thousand leva, confiscation of all or part of the property of the guilty party and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

#### Art. 303.

(Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, repealed - SG, No. 74 of 2015)

#### Art. 304.

(Amended - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) Whoever offers, promises or gives a gift or any benefit to an official, in order for them to perform or not perform an official act or because they have performed or not performed such an act, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(2) If in connection with the bribery the official has violated their official duties, the punishment is imprisonment for up to eight years and a fine of up to seven thousand leva, when such violation does not constitute a more severely punishable crime.

(3) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever offers, promises or gives a bribe to a foreign official.

Art. 304a. (New - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever offers, promises or gives a bribe to an official holding a responsible official position, including a judge, lay judge, prosecutor or investigator, or to a police authority, or to an investigating police officer, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to ten years and a fine of up to fifteen thousand leva.

Art. 304b. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) Whoever requests or accepts a gift or any benefit to which they are not entitled, or accepts an offer or promise of a gift or benefit, in order to exercise influence in the decision-making by an official or foreign official in connection with their duties, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to six years or a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(2) Whoever offers, promises or gives a gift or any undue benefit to a person who claims to be able to exercise influence under para. 1 shall be punished with imprisonment for up to three years or a fine of up to three thousand leva.

Art. 304c. (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) The punishments under the preceding articles shall also be imposed when with the consent of the official, the foreign official or the person who claims to be able to exercise influence, the gift or benefit has been offered, promised or given to another.

#### Art. 305.

(Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) The punishments for bribery under the preceding articles shall also be imposed on an arbitrator or expert appointed by a court, institution, enterprise or organization, when they commit such acts in connection with their activity, as well as on whoever offers, promises or gives such a bribe.

(2) The punishments for bribery under the preceding articles shall also be imposed on a defense attorney or trustee when they commit such acts in order to help resolve a criminal or civil case in favor of the opposing party or to the detriment of the principal, as well as on whoever offers, promises or gives such a bribe.

Art. 305a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever mediates for the commission of any of the acts under the preceding articles, if this does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to three years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

#### Art. 306.

(Amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The person who has offered, promised or given a bribe shall not be punished if they have been extorted by the official, the arbitrator or the expert to do so and if they have immediately and voluntarily reported it to the authorities.

#### Art. 307.

(Amended - SG, No. 51 of 2000) Whoever intentionally creates a situation or conditions to provoke the offering, giving or receiving of a bribe with the purpose of harming the one who gave or accepted the bribe, shall be punished for provocation to bribery with imprisonment for up to three years.

Art. 307a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The subject of the crimes under this section shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and when it is missing, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

Chapter Eight "a".

CRIMES AGAINST SPORT (NEW - SG, NO. 60 OF 2011)

Art. 307b. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) Whoever, through violence, fraud, threat or other unlawful means, induces another to influence the development or result of a sports competition administered by a sports organization, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva.

Art. 307c. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) (1) Whoever promises, offers or gives to another a benefit that is not due to them, in order to influence or for having influenced the development or result of a sports competition administered by a sports organization, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on the one who requests or accepts any benefit that is not due to them, or accepts an offer or promise of a benefit with the purpose of influencing or for having influenced the development or result of a sports competition, as well as when with their consent the benefit is offered, promised or given to another.

(3) Whoever mediates the commission of any of the acts under para. 1 and 2, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to three years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(4) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who secures or organizes the offering or giving of the benefit.

(5) The perpetrator shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55 if they voluntarily report to the competent authority about a crime committed under para. 1 - 4.

Art. 307d. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) (1) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva, when the act under Art. 307b or Art. 307c is committed:

- 1. in relation to a participant in a competition who has not reached 18 years of age;
- 2. in relation to two or more participants in a competition;
- 3. in relation to or by a person from a management or supervisory body of a sports organization, sports referee, delegate or other person, at or in connection with the performance of their service or function;
- 4. repeatedly.
  - (2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva, when the act under Art. 307b or Art. 307c:
- 1. is committed by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 2. is committed under conditions of dangerous recidivism;
- 3. constitutes a particularly serious case;
- 4. relates to a competition included in a gambling game with bets on the development or results of sports competitions.

Art. 307e. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) (1) In the cases under Art. 307b, 307c and 307d, the court may order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

(2) In the cases under Art. 307d, the court may also order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.

Art. 307f. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) The subject of the crime under this chapter shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

Chapter Nine.

DOCUMENT CRIMES

#### Art. 308.

(1) Whoever composes a false official document or alters the content of an official document with the purpose of it being used, shall be punished for forgery of a document with imprisonment for up to three years

(2) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) When the subject of the act under para. 1 is certificates of heirs or civil status acts, notarial acts or notarial certifications, Bulgarian or foreign identity documents, documents for completed education or for acquired qualification, driver's licenses for vehicles, for registration of vehicles, visa stickers, other documents certifying the transfer or establishment of ownership or other property rights, legal capacity, personal or registration data, the punishment shall be imprisonment for up to eight years.

(3) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amended - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) The punishment shall be imprisonment for up to ten years, when:

- 1. (amended - SG, No. 101 of 2017) the act under para. 1 is with the purpose of facilitating the commission of a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1, 2, 6 or 7;
- 2. the act under para. 2 is with the purpose of property benefit.
  - (4) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, former para. 2, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) In minor cases the punishment shall be:
- 1. (amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) under para. 1 - imprisonment for up to six months or probation;
- 2. under para. 2 - imprisonment for up to two years;
- 3. under para. 3 - imprisonment for up to three years.
  - (5) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) For preparation for a crime under para. 1, the punishment shall be imprisonment for up to one year. For preparation for a crime under para. 2 and 3 or for association with the purpose of committing any of the crimes provided therein, the punishment shall be imprisonment for up to six years.
  - (6) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) The participant in an association who, before the completion of composing a false official document or altering the content of an official document (if the association was formed with such purpose) or before the beginning of the distribution of false or altered documents (if the association was formed for this), renounces the act and reports it to the authorities, shall not be punished.
  - (7) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Whoever prepares, holds or conceals objects, materials or instruments, which they know or assume are intended or have been used for composing or altering a document under para. 2 and 3, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to six years.

#### Art. 309.

(1) Whoever, by themselves or through another, composes a false private document or alters the content of a private document and uses it to prove that a right or obligation, or a legal relationship, exists or does not exist or has been terminated or modified, shall be punished for forgery of a document with imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) If the crime has as its subject securities, the punishment shall be imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 101 of 2017) When the act under para. 1 is committed to prove that rights over shares, shares in a commercial company or part or whole commercial enterprise as a totality of rights, obligations and actual relationships exist or do not exist or have been terminated or modified, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years.

(4) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017) When an act under para. 3 has caused damage in particularly large amounts, constituting a particularly serious case, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to ten years. The court may order confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person and deprive them of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

(5) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017) In minor cases under para. 1 and 2, the punishment shall be probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and under para. 3 - imprisonment for up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Art. 310.

(1) (Amended and supplemented - SG, issue 26 of 2004) If the crime under Art. 308, para. 1 and 309, para. 1 and 2, is committed by an official within the scope of their duties, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years, and in the cases under Art. 308, para. 2 and 3 - imprisonment up to twelve years, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.

(2) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) In minor cases the punishment is imprisonment up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 311.

(1) (Amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An official who, within the scope of their duties, compiles an official document in which they certify false circumstances or statements, with the purpose that this document be used as evidence for those circumstances or statements, is punished with imprisonment up to five years, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) In minor cases the punishment is imprisonment up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 312.

(1) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) A doctor who provides someone with a false certificate about their state of health, when not acting as an official, is punished with imprisonment up to two years or probation.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Under the same conditions, a veterinarian who issues a document with false content about the health condition of an animal is punished with imprisonment up to one year or probation.

#### Art. 313.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002) Whoever confirms an untruth or conceals the truth in a written declaration or communication sent electronically, which by virtue of law, decree or regulation of the Council of Ministers are given before an authority for certifying the truthfulness of certain circumstances, is punished with imprisonment up to three years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (New - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) When the act under para. 1 is committed with the purpose of avoiding payment of due taxes, the punishment is imprisonment up to three years or a fine up to one thousand leva.

(3) (Previous para. 2 - amended, SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002) The punishment under para. 1 is also imposed on the one who confirms an untruth or conceals the truth in a private document or communication sent electronically, in which by express provision of law, decree or regulation of the Council of Ministers is specifically obligated to certify the truth, and uses this document as evidence for the falsely certified circumstances or statements.

(4) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997) Whoever, in connection with a public offering of securities in a prospectus or review of economic condition, uses untrue favorable data or conceals unfavorable ones that are of essential significance in making a decision to acquire securities, is punished with imprisonment up to three years and a fine up to five hundred leva.

Art. 313a. (New - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amended - SG, issue 99 of 1989, repealed, issue 10 of 1993, previous Art. 313b - issue 10 of 1993) (1) Whoever, in a declaration under Art. 4, para. 2 of the Law on the Property of the Bulgarian Communist Party, the Bulgarian Agrarian National Union, the Fatherland Front, the Dimitrov Communist Youth Union, the Union of Active Fighters Against Fascism and Capitalism and the Bulgarian Trade Unions, confirms an untruth or conceals the truth with the purpose of completely or partially thwarting the confiscation of illegally possessed state property, is punished with imprisonment from three to eight years.

(2) Whoever, after being requested, refuses to give a declaration under Art. 4, para. 2 of the law specified in para. 1, is punished with imprisonment from two to six years.

(3) In the cases under para. 1 and 2, the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

(4) The perpetrator under para. 1 and 2 is not punished if, in revealing the truth, they would accuse of a crime themselves, their spouse, descendants, ascendants, brothers or sisters.

Art. 313b. (New - SG, issue 102 of 2006) (1) Whoever destroys, conceals, alters or damages a document of State Security or of the intelligence services of the Bulgarian People's Army, is punished with imprisonment from three to six years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva.

(2) The same punishment is imposed on the one who holds a document in violation of the Law on Access to and Disclosure of Documents and on Declaring the Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens with State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army.

#### Art. 314.

(Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever intentionally causes false circumstances or statements to be entered in an official document, compiled in accordance with the established procedure on the basis of an application by a private person, is punished with imprisonment up to two years or probation.

#### Art. 315.

(1) Whoever compiles a document by filling in a sheet bearing the signature of the issuer with content that does not correspond to the will of the signatory, is punished in accordance with the distinctions in Art. 308 and 309.

(2) In accordance with the same distinctions is punished the one who, through fraud, induces another to sign a document with content that does not correspond to the will of the signatory.

#### Art. 316.

The punishment provided in the preceding articles of this chapter is also imposed on the one who knowingly uses a false or altered document, a document with false content or one under the preceding article, when they cannot be held criminally liable for its compilation.

#### Art. 317.

(Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever unlawfully uses a document, knowing that the issuer signed it without the intention to be bound by it, is punished with imprisonment up to two years or probation.

#### Art. 318.

(Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully uses an official document issued for another person, with the purpose of deceiving an official or representative of the public, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, is punished with imprisonment up to two years or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Art. 319.

(Amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever destroys, hides or damages another's or a document not exclusively belonging to him with the purpose of causing harm to another or obtaining benefit for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years or probation.

Chapter Nine "a".

COMPUTER CRIMES (NEW - SG, NO. 92 OF 2002)

Art. 319a. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever unlawfully gains access to an information system or parts thereof, in non-negligible cases shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years and a fine up to three thousand leva.

(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance to commit such an act, the punishment is imprisonment up to six years and a fine up to five thousand leva.

(3) (Suppl. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed repeatedly or with respect to data for creating an electronic signature, the punishment is imprisonment up to seven years and a fine up to twenty thousand leva.

(4) (Amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, suppl. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the acts under paras. 1 - 3 are committed with respect to information constituting state or other secret protected by law or against an information system that is part of critical infrastructure, the punishment is from four to eight years of imprisonment, if not subject to a more severe punishment.

(5) (Amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If serious consequences have resulted from the act under para. 4, the punishment is from five to twelve years.

Art. 319b. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever unlawfully adds, copies, uses, alters, transfers, deletes, damages, deteriorates, hides, destroys computer data in an information system or stops access to such data, in non-negligible cases shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years and a fine up to ten thousand leva.

(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 has caused significant damages or other serious consequences have occurred, the punishment is imprisonment up to seven years and a fine up to twenty thousand leva.

(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed with the purpose of property benefit, the punishment is imprisonment from five to seven years and a fine up to ten thousand leva.

(4) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed through a computer program, password, access code or other access data to an information system or to a part thereof, intended to affect more than one information system, and the consequences under para. 2 have occurred, the punishment is imprisonment from five to eight years and a fine up to twenty thousand leva.

(5) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) The punishment is imprisonment from nine to twelve years and a fine up to thirty thousand leva, when the act under para. 1:

- 1. is committed by a person who acts on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 2. is committed against an information system that is part of critical infrastructure.

Art. 319v. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Suppl. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever commits an act under Art. 319b with respect to data that is provided by virtue of law, electronically or on an electronic or other medium, shall be punished by imprisonment up to seven years and a fine up to five thousand leva.

(2) (Suppl. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 has the purpose of preventing the fulfillment of an obligation, the punishment is imprisonment from five to seven years and a fine up to seven thousand leva.

Art. 319g. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever introduces a computer virus into an information system or computer network shall be punished by imprisonment up to seven years and a fine up to five thousand leva.

(2) (New - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on one who introduces another computer program that is intended to disrupt the activity of an information system or computer network or to ascertain, erase, delete, alter or copy computer data without permission, when such is required, insofar as the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime.

(3) (Previous para. 2, amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If significant damages have resulted from the act under paras. 1 and 2 or it has been committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment up to nine years and a fine up to twenty thousand leva.

(4) (New - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under paras. 1 and 2 is committed against an information system or computer network that is part of critical infrastructure, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twelve years and a fine up to twenty thousand leva.

Art. 319d. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022, suppl. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) Whoever creates, procures for himself or for another, imports, exports, transfers, transports, provides or otherwise distributes computer programs, passwords, codes or other similar access data to an information system or part thereof with the purpose of committing a crime under Art. 171, para. 3, Art. 319a, Art. 319b, Art. 319v or Art. 319g, shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years.

(2) (Suppl. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, amend. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) When the act under para. 1 has disclosed personal data, classified information or other secret protected by law, insofar as the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, the punishment is imprisonment from four to seven years.

(3) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) For an act under para. 1, committed with mercenary purpose or by a person who acts on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, or if it has caused significant damages or other serious consequences have occurred, the punishment is imprisonment up to nine years.

Art. 319e. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 85 of 2017, suppl. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever, when providing information services, violates the provisions of Art. 6, para. 2, item 5 of the Electronic Document and Electronic Identification Services Act, shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years and a fine up to five thousand leva, if not subject to a more severe punishment.

Chapter Ten.

CRIMES AGAINST ORDER AND PUBLIC PEACE

#### Art. 320.

(1) (Previous text of Art. 320 - SG No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG No. 67 of 2023) Whoever publicly incites the commission of a crime through preaching before a multitude of people, through mass media or in another similar manner, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years, but not with a more severe punishment than that provided for the crime itself.

(2) (New - SG No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG No. 101 of 2017, repealed - SG No. 84 of 2023)

(3) (New - SG No. 67 of 2023) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed when the act under para. 1 has been committed with racist, xenophobic or motives related to sexual orientation.

Art. 320a. (New - SG No. 41 of 1985, amd. and suppl. - SG No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amd. - SG No. 101 of 2017, amd. - SG No. 84 of 2023) Whoever threatens to commit a crime under Art. 330, Art. 333, Art. 334, Art. 336a, Art. 340, Art. 341a, Art. 341b, Art. 341v, Art. 342, para. 3, Art. 344, Art. 349, Art. 350, Art. 352, para. 1, Art. 356e, Art. 356k, Art. 356l or Art. 356m and this threat could cause reasonable apprehension of its realization, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years.

#### Art. 321.

(1) (Amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002) Whoever forms or leads an organized criminal group shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002) Whoever participates in such a group shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.

(3) (New - SG No. 62 of 1997, amd. - SG No. 21 of 2000, amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG No. 27 of 2009, amd. and suppl. - SG No. 26 of 2010) When the group is armed or established for profit or for the purpose of committing crimes under Art. 142, Art. 142a, Art. 143a, Art. 243, 244, 253, 280, 337, Art. 339, para. 1 - 4, Art. 354a, para. 1 and 2 and Art. 354b, para. 1 - 4 or when an official participates in it, the punishment is:

- 1. under para. 1 - imprisonment from five to fifteen years;
- 2. under para. 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years.
  - (4) (New - SG No. 62 of 1997) A participant in the group shall not be punished if he voluntarily surrenders to the authorities and discloses everything known to him about the group, before a crime has been committed by him or by it.
  - (5) (New - SG No. 62 of 1997) A participant in the group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities, discloses everything known to him about the group and thereby substantially facilitates the detection and proving of crimes committed by it, shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55.
  - (6) (New - SG No. 92 of 2002) Whoever conspires with one or more persons to commit in the country or abroad crimes for which a punishment of imprisonment of more than three years is provided and which aim to obtain property benefits or to exercise unlawful influence on the activity of a state authority or local self-government, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years.

Art. 321a. (New - SG No. 62 of 1997) (1) Whoever participates in the leadership of an organization or group which, through the use of force or instilling fear, concludes transactions or extracts benefits, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to eight years.

(2) Whoever participates in such an organization or group shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.

(3) Property acquired by the organization, by the group or by the participants therein from this activity shall be confiscated in favor of the state, if the persons from whom it was acquired, or their heirs, are unknown.

(4) In the cases under the preceding paragraphs, the provision of Art. 321, para. 4 and 5 shall apply.

#### Art. 322.

(Amd. - SG No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever does not prevent the commission of an obviously serious crime, if he could have done so without significant difficulty and without danger to himself or to another, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or by probation.

#### Art. 323.

(Amd. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 62 of 1997) Whoever arbitrarily, not in the manner established by law, exercises his own or another's actual or presumed right contested by another, shall be punished in non-trivial cases by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of up to one thousand leva.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 62 of 1997) Whoever arbitrarily occupies immovable property from the possession of which he has been removed in due order, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to five hundred leva.

(3) The perpetrator shall not be punished if, after a warning from the competent state authority, he immediately restores the initial factual situation.

(4) The provision of the preceding paragraph shall not apply if the perpetrator, after the restoration, commits the same act again.

(5) (New - SG No. 50 of 1995) When the act under para. 1 has been carried out through force or threat, the punishment is imprisonment of up to 6 years.

Art. 323a. (New - SG No. 27 of 1973, amd. No. 28 of 1982, corr. No. 31 of 1982, amd. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) (1) Whoever constructs a building on arable land, agricultural land or pasture, without having the right to do so, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.

(2) In case of repeated commission of the crime under para. 1, as well as in case of continuation of the construction, after it has been stopped by the competent authorities, the punishment is imprisonment from one to three years and a fine from two to five thousand leva, as well as public reprimand.

#### Art. 324.
