(Amd. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 1 of 1991) (1) (Amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002) Whoever practices a profession or trade without having the corresponding legal capacity shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 89 of 1986, amd. - SG No. 10 of 1993, suppl. - SG No. 83 of 1998, amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG No. 76 of 2005, effective from 01.01.2007, amd. - SG No. 75 of 2006) When the profession or trade is related to the health care of the population, the punishment is imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva. The same punishment shall be imposed on a physician, dentist or master pharmacist who practices his profession in violation of the established procedure.

(3) (New - SG No. 89 of 1986, amd. - No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.

#### Art. 325.

(1) (Amd. - SG No. 103/2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever commits indecent acts grossly violating public order and expressing clear disrespect to society shall be punished for hooliganism by deprivation of liberty of up to two years or probation, as well as by public reprimand.

(2) When the act is accompanied by resistance against an authority or a representative of the public performing duties to maintain public order, or when by its content it is characterized by exceptional cynicism or audacity, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty of up to five years.

(3) (New - SG No. 95/2016) When the act under para. 1 or 2 is committed while operating a motor vehicle, the punishment shall be: under para. 1 - deprivation of liberty of up to three years and deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7; under para. 2 - deprivation of liberty of up to five years and deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

(4) (New - SG No. 28/1982, effective 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 92/2002, effective 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26/2004, effective 01.01.2004, amd. - SG No. 103/2004, effective 01.01.2005, previous para. 3, suppl. - SG No. 95/2016) When the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be: under para. 1 - deprivation of liberty of up to three years; under para. 2 and 3 - deprivation of liberty from one to five years.

(5) (Previous para. 3, amd. - SG No. 28/1982, effective 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 92/2002, effective 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26/2004, effective 01.01.2004, amd. - SG No. 103/2004, effective 01.01.2005, previous para. 4, amd. - SG No. 95/2016) When the act under para. 1 - 3 constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to six years.

Art. 325a. (New - SG No. 27/2009, amd. - SG No. 33/2011, effective 27.05.2011) (1) (Amd. - SG No. 67/2023) Whoever organizes or participates in conducting animal fights, breeds, trains or provides animals for fights, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to six years and a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 67/2023) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine from six thousand to fifty thousand leva, when:

- 1. the act is committed repeatedly;
- 2. the act is committed by a veterinarian or technician, by a person engaged in activities or profession related to breeding animals or caring for them;
- 3. a minor or juvenile is present at the fights;
- 4. the fights are recorded for the purpose of distribution.
  - (3) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on whoever organizes or participates in betting at animal fights.
  - (4) The perpetrator shall not be punished if he voluntarily reports to a competent authority the crime committed under para. 1 and 3.
  - (5) (New - SG No. 67/2023) When the act under para. 1 - 3 constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to seven years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.

Art. 325b. (New - SG No. 33/2011, effective 27.07.2011) (1) (Amd. - SG No. 67/2023) Whoever, by showing cruelty to a vertebrate animal, unlawfully causes its death, serious or permanent injury, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to four years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 67/2023) The punishment shall be from two to four years and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva, when the act under para. 1 is committed:

- 1. by a person engaged in activities or profession related to breeding animals or caring for them;
- 2. by a method or means dangerous to the life of humans or animals, in a particularly agonizing manner for the animal or with particular cruelty;
- 3. in a public place or in the presence of a minor or juvenile;
- 4. repeatedly.
  - (3) (New - SG No. 67/2023) When the act under para. 2 and 3 constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to five years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.

Art. 325c. (New - SG No. 33/2011, effective 27.07.2011) (1) Whoever fails to exercise sufficient care for a vertebrate animal under his supervision, as a result of which it causes medium or serious bodily injury to a person, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to three years or probation and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(2) In the cases under para. 1, if death has resulted, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty of up to five years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva.

#### Art. 326.

(1) (Amd. - SG No. 28/1982, effective 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 41/1985, amd. - SG No. 92/2002, effective 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26/2004, effective 01.01.2004, amd. - SG No. 103/2004, effective 01.01.2005, previous text of Art. 326 - SG No. 26/2010) Whoever transmits by radio, telephone or otherwise false calls or misleading signals for help, accident or alarm, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to two years.

(2) (New - SG No. 26/2010, amd. - SG No. 28/2020, effective 24.03.2020) If significant harmful consequences result from the act under para. 1, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty of up to five years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.

#### Art. 327.

(Amd. - SG No. 28/1982, effective 01.07.1982, amd. - SG No. 10/1993, amd. - SG No. 92/2002, amd. - SG No. 60/2011) (1) Whoever organizes a game of chance not in the manner established by law shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to six years and a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years and a fine from two thousand to fifty thousand leva, when the act under para. 1:

- 1. is committed under conditions of dangerous recidivism;
- 2. constitutes a particularly serious case;
- 3. involves bets on the development or outcome of a sporting competition administered by a sports organization.
  - (3) Whoever participates in a game of chance organized not in the manner established by law shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to one year or probation.
  - (4) When the act under para. 3 is committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty of up to three years and a fine from five hundred to five thousand leva.
  - (5) The money or things that are the subject of the game of chance shall be forfeited to the state, and if they are missing or have been alienated, their equivalent value shall be awarded.
  - (6) The perpetrator under para. 3 shall not be punished if he voluntarily reports to a competent authority the crime committed under para. 1 and 2.

#### Art. 328.

(Amd. - SG No. 28/1982, effective 01.07.1982, rep. - SG No. 26/2010)

#### Art. 329.

(Amd. - SG No. 95/1975) (1) (Amd. - SG No. 92/2002, effective 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26/2004, effective 01.01.2004, amd. - SG No. 103/2004, effective 01.01.2005, declared unconstitutional by Decision No. 13/2022 of the Constitutional Court - SG No. 79/2022) An adult able-bodied person who does not engage in socially useful work for a prolonged period of time while receiving non-labor income in an unlawful or immoral manner shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to two years or probation.

(2) (Amd. - SG No. 92/2002, effective 01.01.2005, amd. regarding entry into force - SG No. 26/2004, effective 01.01.2004, declared unconstitutional by Decision No. 13/2022 of the Constitutional Court - SG No. 79/2022) Whoever systematically engages in begging shall be punished by probation for a term of up to two years.

Chapter Eleven.

PUBLICLY DANGEROUS CRIMES

### Section I.

Crimes committed in a publicly dangerous manner or by publicly dangerous means

#### Art. 330.

(1) Whoever sets fire to a building, inventory, goods, agricultural or other products, forest, machines, mine or other property of significant value, shall be punished for arson with imprisonment from one to eight years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years:

- 1. if the fire posed a danger to someone's life;
- 2. if there was a danger of it spreading to other properties, such as those mentioned above;
- 3. if the burned property has historical, scientific or artistic value or if objects having historical, scientific or artistic value are kept in the burned premises;
- 4. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002) if the arson was committed by a person acting on order or in execution of a decision of an organization or group under Art. 321a or an organized criminal group;
- 5. (new - SG, issue 26 of 2004) if the burned forest or other property of significant value is located in a protected territory;
- 6. (new - SG, issue 67 of 2023) if the arson was committed on racist or xenophobic grounds.
  - (3) If in the cases under the previous paragraph significant damages have resulted, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years, and if someone's death has resulted, when the perpetrator did not intend and did not allow this, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

#### Art. 331.

(1) Whoever through negligence sets fire to another's property under the previous article, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.

(2) (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002) Whoever sets fire to stubble, as a result of which causes a fire in the forest fund, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years and a fine from five hundred to five thousand leva.

(3) (Previous para. 2 - SG, issue 92 of 2002) If death or significant damages have resulted, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years.

#### Art. 332.

For arson the perpetrator shall not be punished if of his own accord he extinguished the fire immediately, before significant damages have occurred.

#### Art. 333.

When the objects under Art. 330 are damaged or destroyed by explosion, the respective punishments provided in that article shall be imposed.

#### Art. 334.

(1) Whoever causes flooding and thereby endangers the life or property of another, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to twelve years.

(2) If in this case significant damages have resulted, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and if someone's death has resulted, when the perpetrator did not intend and did not allow this, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to fifteen years.

#### Art. 335.

(1) Whoever causes flooding through negligence and thereby endangers the life or property of another, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.

(2) If death or significant damages have resulted, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years.

#### Art. 336.

(Repealed - SG, issue 41 of 1985)

Art. 336a. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever creates danger to another's life or for causing serious or moderate bodily injury, or significant property damage, by in violation of the established rules:

- 1. using explosive substance, radioactive material, biological, chemical or nuclear weapon or other nuclear device in an aircraft against, or on a ship, or on a fixed platform located on the continental shelf;
- 2. discharging, pouring, throwing or unloading from an aircraft, from a ship or from a fixed platform located on the continental shelf, explosive substance, radioactive material, biological, chemical or nuclear weapon or other nuclear device;
- 3. discharging, pouring, throwing or unloading from an aircraft, from a ship or from a fixed platform located on the continental shelf, oil, liquefied natural gas, dangerous or harmful substance, outside the cases under item 2;
- 4. using an aircraft or ship, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
  - (2) With the punishment under para. 1 shall also be punished whoever unlawfully transports on an aircraft or on a ship weapon, dangerous substance, material, information technology or other object provided for in an international treaty that has entered into force for the Republic of Bulgaria.
  - (3) When by an act under para. 1 and 2 serious or moderate bodily injury has been caused to another, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years.
  - (4) When by an act under para. 1 and 2 death has been caused to another, the punishment is imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years or life imprisonment.
  - (5) In the cases under para. 1 - 4 the court shall also impose deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

Art. 336b. (New - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever with terrorist purpose delivers, places, activates or detonates a substance or other explosive device or explosive materials in a place of public use, object of the public transport system or infrastructure object and from this significant damages, moderate or serious bodily injury have resulted, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and when death has been caused - with imprisonment from fifteen to thirty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Art. 337.

(1) (Amended - SG, issue 41 of 1985, previous text of Art. 337, amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, supplemented - SG, issue 47 of 2016, supplemented - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever manufactures, processes, modifies, repairs, develops, researches, stores, trades, transports, imports or exports explosives, firearms, non-firearms, chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, ammunition and pyrotechnic products or destroys cluster munitions, anti-personnel mines and other products within the meaning of the Law on Implementation of the Convention on Cluster Munitions and the Convention on the Prohibition of the Use, Stockpiling, Production and Transfer of Anti-Personnel Mines and on Their Destruction, without having the right to do so by law or without a permit issued by the competent authority, when such is required, or does this not in accordance with the permit given to him, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years if the act was committed:

- 1. by an official who took advantage of his official position;
- 2. repeatedly, in non-trivial cases.
  - (3) (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) When the object of the crime is in large amounts, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
  - (4) (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) When the object of the crime is in particularly large amounts and the case is particularly serious, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
  - (5) (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) For preparation for a crime under para. 1 - 4 the punishment is imprisonment up to two years.

#### Art. 338.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 33 от 2011 г., в сила от 27.05.2011 г.) Whoever, while possessing, transporting, dispatching or working with explosives, firearms, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic articles, does not take the necessary safety measures and especially the measures provided in the proper regulations, ordinances or instructions, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years or a fine from five hundred to three thousand leva.

(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 33 от 2011 г., в сила от 27.05.2011 г.) Whoever provides explosives, weapons, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic articles to a person under 18 years of age shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.

(3) (Предишна ал. 2, изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., изм. и доп. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) If the acts under para. 1 and 2 have resulted in causing to one or more persons medium or serious bodily harm, or death, or significant damage to property, when the perpetrator did not intend and did not allow this, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years, and in especially serious cases the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 47 от 2016 г.) When the subject of the crime is cluster munitions, anti-personnel mines and other articles within the meaning of the Law on the Implementation of the Convention on Cluster Munitions and the Convention on the Prohibition of the Use, Stockpiling, Production and Transfer of Anti-Personnel Mines and on Their Destruction, the punishment is:

- 1. in cases under para. 1 - imprisonment from two to eight years;
- 2. in cases under para. 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva;
- 3. in cases under para. 3 - imprisonment from five to twelve years, and in especially serious cases - imprisonment from six to fifteen years.

#### Art. 339.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 41 от 1985 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 33 от 2011 г., в сила от 27.05.2011 г.) (1) (Доп. - ДВ, бр. 84 от 2023 г.) Whoever acquires by any means, examines, possesses or transfers to another explosives, firearms, chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic articles, without having proper authorization for this, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) If it concerns explosives, firearms, chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic articles in large quantities, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.

(3) Whoever alienates or transfers explosives, firearms, chemical, biological or nuclear weapons or pyrotechnic articles to a person who does not have permission to acquire them shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(4) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed on one who alienates or transfers to another ammunition for firearms without the latter having permission to carry the corresponding firearm.

(5) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who, without appropriate authorization, takes possession of found explosives, firearms, chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic articles.

(6) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 47 от 2016 г.) When the subject of the crime is cluster munitions, anti-personnel mines and other articles within the meaning of the Law on the Implementation of the Convention on Cluster Munitions and the Convention on the Prohibition of the Use, Stockpiling, Production and Transfer of Anti-Personnel Mines and on Their Destruction, the punishment is:

- 1. in cases under para. 1 - imprisonment from three to ten years;
- 2. in cases under para. 2 - imprisonment from five to twelve years;
- 3. in cases under para. 3, 4 and 5 - imprisonment from three to ten years.

Art. 339a. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) Whoever, without proper authorization required by law, manufactures, uses, sells or possesses special technical means intended for covert collection of information shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) (Предишна ал. 3, изм. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) If the act under para. 1 is committed by an official in connection with his service, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) (Предишна ал. 2 - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) The special technical means shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

Art. 339b. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) Whoever manufactures, transports, possesses, acquires or transfers to another dual-use items or technologies defined by law or by an act of the Council of Ministers with the purpose of being used for carrying out transactions in violation of prohibitions, restrictions or sanctions imposed by the Security Council of the United Nations, by the Organization for Security and Cooperation in Europe or by the European Union, or arising from an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine of up to two hundred thousand leva.

### Section II.

Crimes against transport and communications

#### Art. 340.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г.) Whoever damages a moving railway train or railway, aircraft, automobile, electric transport vehicle (trolleybus, tram and other such intended for mass transport) or facilities or accessories to them, tunnel, bridge or retaining wall on roads, or damages or allows to be damaged, run aground or sink a ship and thereby creates danger to the life of another or for significant damage to another's property, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г.) Whoever destroys an aircraft in operation, or causes it damage that renders it unfit for flight or is of a nature to endanger its safety in flight, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to twenty years.

(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 101 от 2017 г.) With the punishment under para. 2 shall also be punished one who destroys a ship or causes damage to the ship or its cargo that is of a nature to endanger the safety of the ship during navigation, or destroys a fixed platform, including when located on the continental shelf, or causes it damage that is of a nature to endanger its safety.

(4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 101 от 2017 г.) With the punishment under para. 2 shall also be punished one who, through the use of a device, substance or weapon, destroys or causes significant property damage to the facilities of an airport or to an aircraft located on its territory that is not in operation, or interrupts the activities of the airport services, where this endangers or is of a nature to endanger the safety of the airport.

(5) (Предишна ал. 2, изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 101 от 2017 г.) If in the cases under the preceding paragraphs has resulted: a) medium or serious bodily harm to one or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from eight to fifteen years; b) (доп. - ДВ, бр. 50 от 1995 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г.) death of one or more persons regardless of whether the consequences under letter "a" have occurred, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Art. 341.

(Amended - SG No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG No. 101 of 2017) When the act under Art. 340, para. 1-4 is committed through negligence and has resulted in: a) significant property damage; b) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons regardless of whether the consequences under the previous item have occurred; c) death of one or more persons regardless of whether the consequences under items "a" and "b" have occurred, the punishment is: under item "a" - imprisonment up to three years; under item "b" - imprisonment up to six years; under item "c" - imprisonment from one to ten years.

Art. 341a. (New - SG No. 95 of 1975) (1) Whoever places on an aircraft a device or substance that can destroy it or cause damage to it that renders it unfit for flight or creates danger to its safety in flight, if not subject to more severe punishment, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) Whoever endangers the safety of an aircraft in flight by: a) destroying or damaging an installation or facility for air traffic control; b) communicating information or giving a signal which he knows to be false, placing a false sign or removing or moving a sign intended to ensure traffic safety, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to fifteen years.

(3) Whoever commits violence against a person on board an aircraft in flight, if the act is of a nature to endanger the safety of that aircraft and does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to ten years.

(4) (New - SG No. 101 of 2017) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed on whoever, through the use of a device, substance or weapon, commits violence against a person on the territory of an airport, if the act is of a nature to endanger the health or life of bystanders and does not constitute a more serious crime.

(5) (Previous para. 4, amended - SG No. 101 of 2017) When in the cases under the preceding paragraphs medium or serious bodily injury or death of one or more persons has resulted, the punishments provided for in Art. 340, para. 5 shall be imposed respectively.

(6) (Previous para. 5 - SG No. 101 of 2017) When the act under para. 1 is committed through negligence and consequences under Art. 341 have occurred, the punishments provided for in that article shall be imposed respectively.

Art. 341b. (New - SG No. 95 of 1975) (1) (Amended and supplemented - SG No. 101 of 2017) Whoever unlawfully seizes an aircraft on the ground or in flight, or a ship in the maritime areas or inland waterways of the Republic of Bulgaria or on the high seas, or on a fixed platform located on the continental shelf, or establishes control over such means, shall be punished by imprisonment up to ten years.

(2) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed with force or threat, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years.

(3) (New - SG No. 84 of 2023) The punishments under the preceding paragraphs shall also be imposed on whoever unlawfully seizes other means of public transport or freight carriage.

(4) (Previous para. 3 - SG No. 84 of 2023) If the act under the preceding paragraphs has resulted in: a) (supplemented - SG No. 101 of 2017) significant damage to the aircraft, ship or platform; b) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons regardless of whether the consequences under the previous item have occurred; c) (amended - SG No. 153 of 1998, amended - SG No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) death of one or more persons regardless of whether the consequences under items "a" and "b" have occurred, the punishment is: under items "a" and "b" - imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and under item "c" - imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment without commutation, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6-10.

Art. 341c. (New - SG No. 95 of 1975, repealed - SG No. 41 of 1985, new - SG No. 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever places on a ship in the exclusive economic zone of the Republic of Bulgaria or on the high seas a device or substance that can destroy or damage it or damage the ship's cargo, if the act is of a nature to endanger the safe navigation of the ship, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to twelve years.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever places on a fixed platform located on the continental shelf a device or substance that can destroy it or is of a nature to endanger its safety.

(3) Whoever endangers the safe navigation of a ship by:

- 1. destroying, damaging or disrupting the operation of maritime or river navigation facilities;
- 2. communicating information or giving a signal which he knows to be false, placing a false sign or removing or moving a sign intended to ensure traffic safety, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to fifteen years.
  - (4) Whoever commits violence against a person on board a ship, if the act is of a nature to endanger the safe navigation of the ship and does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to ten years.
  - (5) The punishment under para. 4 shall also be imposed on whoever commits violence against a person on board a fixed platform located on the continental shelf, if the act is of a nature to endanger the safety of the platform and does not constitute a more serious crime.
  - (6) If in the cases under para. 1-5 the following has resulted:
- 1. medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from eight to fifteen years;
- 2. death of one or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
  - (7) When the act under para. 1 is committed through negligence and has resulted in:
- 1. significant property damage;
- 2. medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons regardless of whether consequences under item 1 have occurred;
- 3. death of one or more persons regardless of whether consequences under items 1 and 2 have occurred, the punishment is: under item 1 - imprisonment from five to ten years; under item 2 - imprisonment from eight to fifteen years; under item 3 - imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Art. 342.

(Amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever, while operating railway rolling stock, an aircraft, a motor vehicle, a watercraft, a combat or special machine, violates the traffic rules, thereby allowing the infliction of bodily injury or death of another, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to two years or by probation.

(2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) The same punishment shall also be imposed on a worker or employee in transport who violates the operational rules or the requirements for good quality of repair of the rolling stock, roads or facilities, thereby allowing the infliction of bodily injury or death of another.

(3) (Previous para. 2, amended, - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) If by acts under the preceding paragraphs death, bodily injury or significant property damage to another have been intentionally caused, the punishment is: a) for causing significant property damage - deprivation of liberty from one to ten years; b) for causing moderate or severe bodily injury to one or more persons with or without property damage - from three to twelve years; c) (Amended - SG, No. 85 of 1998) for causing death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under letters "a" and "b" - deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years, and in particularly severe cases - from fifteen to twenty years or life imprisonment.

(4) (Previous para. 3, amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) In the cases under paragraph 3, letters "a" and "b", the court shall deprive the guilty party of the rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7. In the cases under letter "c", the deprivation of these rights is for life.

#### Art. 343.

(Amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) When by acts under the previous article through negligence there have been caused: a) (amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) significant property damage, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to one year or probation; б) (amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) severe or moderate bodily injury, regardless of whether the consequences under letter "a" have occurred, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to four years for severe bodily injury and up to three years or probation for moderate bodily injury; в) (amended - SG, issue 60 of 2012, effective from 08.09.2012, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) death, regardless of whether the consequences under letter "a" have occurred, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to eight years, and in particularly severe cases - deprivation of liberty from five to twelve years.

(2) (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 86 of 2005, effective from 29.04.2006, amended - SG, issue 95 of 2016) The criminal proceedings are terminated if the victim requests this:

- 1. for crimes under para. 1, letter "a";
- 2. for crimes under para. 1, letter "б", when criminal proceedings against the perpetrator have not been terminated on this ground in the last five years.
  - (3) (Supplemented - SG, issue 21 of 2000, former para. 2 - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, supplemented - SG, issue 60 of 2012, effective from 08.09.2012, supplemented - SG, issue 67 of 2023) If the act was committed in an intoxicated state or after use of narcotic substances or their analogues or the person without medical reasons refuses to be tested for alcohol and/or narcotic substances, or their analogues or as a result bodily injury or death of more than one person has occurred, or the perpetrator has fled from the scene of the incident, or has operated without having the necessary legal capacity, when such is required by law, as well as after exceeding the permitted speed in a populated area by more than 50 km/h or if the act was committed after passing through a red signal of the traffic light device, or on a pedestrian crossing, the punishment is: a) (amended - SG, issue 60 of 2012, effective from 08.09.2012, amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015) for severe or moderate bodily injury - deprivation of liberty from one to six years, and in particularly severe cases - deprivation of liberty from two to ten years; б) (amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) for death - deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years, and in particularly severe cases - deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years.
  - (4) (Former para. 3 - SG, issue 92 of 2002) The punishment under letter "б" of the previous paragraph is also imposed when death of one or more persons and bodily injury of one or more persons have occurred.
  - (5) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) In the cases under para. 3 and 4, the court may confiscate in favor of the state the aircraft, motor vehicle, watercraft or special machine, which has been used for committing the crime and is property of the perpetrator, and when the perpetrator is not the owner - to award its equivalent value.

Art. 343a. (1) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, former text of Art. 343a - SG, issue 92 of 2002) If the perpetrator after the act under the previous article has done everything in his power to provide assistance to the victim or victims, the punishment is: a) (amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) under para. 1, letter "б" - deprivation of liberty up to three years for severe bodily injury and deprivation of liberty up to two years or probation for moderate bodily injury; б) under para. 1, letter "в" - deprivation of liberty up to four years; в) under para. 2, letter "a", when moderate or severe bodily injury to more than one person has been caused - deprivation of liberty up to four years, and in particularly severe cases - up to six years; г) under para. 2, letter "б", when death of more than one person has occurred - deprivation of liberty from two to ten years, and in particularly severe cases - from three to twelve years.

(2) (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 86 of 2005, effective from 29.04.2006) For a crime under para. 1, letter "a", the criminal proceedings are terminated if the victim requests this.

Art. 343b. (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever operates a motor vehicle with a blood alcohol concentration above 1.2 per thousand, established in the proper manner, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from one to three years and a fine from two hundred to one thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever operates a motor vehicle with a blood alcohol concentration above 0.5 per thousand, established in the proper manner, after having been convicted by a final judgment for the act under para. 1, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from one to five years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand five hundred leva.

(3) (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000, amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015, supplemented - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever operates a motor vehicle after use of narcotic substances or their analogues, established in the proper manner, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from one to three years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand five hundred leva.

(4) (New - SG, issue 74 of 2015) If the act under para. 3 is committed repeatedly, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to five years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand five hundred leva.

(5) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) In the cases under para. 1 - 4, the court confiscates in favor of the state the motor vehicle, which has been used for committing the crime and is property of the perpetrator, and when the perpetrator is not the owner - to award its equivalent value.

(6) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever operates a motor vehicle and refuses to undergo a check in the proper manner for establishing the use of alcohol and/or narcotic substances or their analogues, after having been punished for any of these acts by administrative order, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to six months and a fine of two thousand leva.

(7) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) The perpetrator shall not be punished if the refusal under para. 6 is due to reasons beyond his control.

Art. 343c. (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever operates a motor vehicle during the term of serving the punishment of deprivation of the right to operate a motor vehicle, after having been punished for the same act by administrative order, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to three years and a fine from two hundred to one thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever within a one-year period from being punished by administrative order for operating a motor vehicle without the relevant driving license commits such an act, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from one to three years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand two hundred leva.

(3) (New - SG, issue 95 of 2016) The punishment under para. 1 is also imposed on a person who operates a motor vehicle during the term of serving a compulsory administrative measure of temporary withdrawal of the driving license for a motor vehicle.

Art. 343d. (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) In all cases of Art. 343, 343a, 343b and 343c, para. 1, the court also orders deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 7 and may order deprivation of the right under point 6.

#### Чл. 344.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) Whoever removes or relocates a sign or signal intended to ensure the safety of railway, water and electric transport traffic, places such a false sign or gives a false signal and thereby endangers the life or property of someone, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to seven years.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) For the act under para. 1, affecting signs of automobile transport, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to two years or a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.

#### Чл. 345.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 2016 г.) (1) Whoever uses a license plate with a registration number issued for another motor vehicle, or a license plate not issued by the relevant authorities, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or by a fine from five hundred to one thousand leva.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on one who operates a motor vehicle that is not registered in due order.

Чл. 345а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 21 от 2000 г.) (1) Whoever in violation of the established procedure erases or falsifies the identification number of a motor vehicle shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years and by a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva.

(3) The punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years or a fine of up to three thousand leva, if numbers on parts of a motor vehicle have been erased or falsified.

#### Чл. 346.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 107 от 1996 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) Whoever unlawfully takes another's motor vehicle from the possession of another without his consent with the intention to use it shall be punished by imprisonment from one to eight years.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., попр. - ДВ, бр. 90 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 107 от 1996 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to ten years if:

- 1. damage to the vehicle has resulted or it has been abandoned without supervision, or
- 2. the act has been committed in a state of intoxication or more than twice, or repeatedly, or
- 3. the taking has been committed under the conditions of Art. 195, para. 1, points 1 - 6;
- 4. (нова - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) the taking has been committed with the purpose of financial gain from the return of the motor vehicle.
  - (3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on a person who offers assistance for the return of the taken vehicle in exchange for receiving financial gain.
  - (4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) In the cases of the preceding paragraphs the court shall order deprivation of the right to operate a motor vehicle.
  - (5) (Предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., предишна ал. 4 - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) If force or threat has been used for the taking of the vehicle or for maintaining possession of it, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and deprivation of the right to operate a motor vehicle, and the court shall also order confiscation of not less than 1/2 of the perpetrator's property.
  - (6) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., предишна ал. 5 - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) The punishment under the preceding paragraph shall also be imposed when the act has been committed by a person specified in Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8, or on the order of an organization or group, or when an attempt has been made to export the motor vehicle across the border of the country or when its serial and registration numbers have been altered.

Чл. 346а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) If before the conclusion of the judicial inquiry in the first instance court the taken motor vehicle is returned, the punishment shall be:

- 1. in the cases under Art. 346, para. 1 - imprisonment of up to five years;
- 2. in the cases under Art. 346, para. 2, point 4, if the return has been effected before receiving the financial gain - imprisonment of up to eight years.

Чл. 346б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 21 от 2000 г., предишен текст на чл. 346а - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 7 от 2019 г.) Whoever unlawfully enters another's motor vehicle without the consent of the owner shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years.

#### Чл. 347.

(1) Whoever damages telegraph, telephone or telex equipment or lines, television or radio equipment or electrification equipment or lines and thereby interrupts or obstructs communications shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) If the act has been committed through negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Чл. 348.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г.) Whoever: а) constructs, holds or uses a radio device that transmits on the air without having written authorization for this; б) uses a radio device that transmits on the air without having previously registered it with complete establishment data or uses it for purposes not permitted under the authorization given to it; в) without prior written authorization arbitrarily alters the registered data of a radio device that transmits on the air; г) (изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) obstructs or interferes with the operation of a radio communication, radio broadcasting, television or radio relay station or radio relay center, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and the radio device shall be confiscated in favor of the state.

Чл. 348а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) (1) Whoever by fraud or by other illegal means uses a telecommunications network, facility or service in order to generate or redirect in his own or another's interest a directed transmission of signals, written text, images, sound, data or messages of any kind via conductor, radio waves, optical or other transmission medium, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva.

(2) When the act under para. 1 has been committed:

- 1. by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission, when it does not constitute a minor case;
- 2. through the use of an unregistered telecommunications device;
- 3. repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to eight years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
  - (3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) In minor cases under para. 1 the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

Чл. 348б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., предишен текст на чл. 348а - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) When the property damage and bodily injury under Art. 343 have been caused to a spouse, ascendant, descendant, brother or sister, criminal prosecution shall be initiated by complaint of the injured party. In these cases the perpetrator may be deprived by administrative order of the right to operate a motor vehicle even when no complaint has been filed or the filed complaint has been withdrawn.

### Раздел III.

Crimes against public health and against the environment (Загл. доп. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.)

#### Чл. 349.

(1) Whoever intentionally places or mixes a substance dangerous to life or health in a well, spring, water supply system or other installation intended for public use, from which or by means of which drinking water is drawn, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) (Suppl. - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amend. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) If the act has resulted in grievous bodily harm, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years, and if death has resulted, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 41 of 1985) In accordance with the differences in the previous paragraphs, the person who, with the intent to infect people, disseminates causative agents of an epidemic disease shall also be punished.

Чл. 349а. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) Whoever violates rules established for the taking and provision of human organs or tissues for transplantation shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years.

(2) The punishment is imprisonment from three to five years if the act was committed for profit.

#### Чл. 350.

(Amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) (1) Whoever prepares food or beverages intended for public use in such a way that dangerous substances are created or enter into them, as well as whoever sells, offers for sale or otherwise puts into circulation such food or beverages, shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years.

(2) Whoever violates rules regulating the extraction, production, processing, storage or trade in animals, raw materials, food or beverages intended for public use, and thereby endangers the health or life of another, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.

(3) If the act under para. 1 and 2 has resulted in medium bodily harm to another - the punishment is imprisonment up to six years, if grievous bodily harm to another has resulted - imprisonment from one to eight years, and if death has resulted - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.

Чл. 350а. (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Whoever in violation of law produces or places on the market food, animal feed or veterinary medicinal products, or beverages, and thereby endangers the life or health of another, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.

#### Чл. 351.

(1) (Amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) For acts under Art. 349 and 350 committed by negligence, the punishment is imprisonment up to two years or probation.

(2) If in this case death has resulted to someone, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years.

#### Чл. 352.

(1) (Amend. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amend. - SG, No. 86 of 1991, amend. - SG, No. 85 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever pollutes or allows to be polluted soil, air, water streams, basins, groundwater, territorial or internal sea waters or sea waters in zones defined by an international agreement in which the Republic of Bulgaria participates, and thereby makes them dangerous for people or animals and plants or unfit for their use for cultural-domestic, health, agricultural and other economic purposes, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to thirty thousand leva.

(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) The same punishment shall be imposed on an official who has allowed, in the design, construction or operation of drainage or irrigation systems, that necessary measures are not taken to prevent dangerous pollution of water-bearing zones for drinking water supply or the raising of the groundwater level in populated and resort areas.

(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) When the acts under para. 1 or 2 have caused:

- 1. death or grievous bodily harm to one or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva;
- 2. non-negligible harm to the environment, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.
  - (4) (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) If the act under para. 1 or 2 has been committed by negligence, the punishment is imprisonment up to three years and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.

Чл. 352а. (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amend. - SG, No. 86 of 1991) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 85 of 1997, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever pollutes or allows to be polluted with petroleum products or derivatives territorial or internal sea waters or sea waters in zones defined by an international agreement in which the Republic of Bulgaria participates, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva. When the act is committed by a ship's captain, the court shall also order deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.

(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever pollutes or allows to be polluted the waters under para. 1 with harmful liquid substances in bulk, defined in an international agreement in which the Republic of Bulgaria participates.

(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) When the act under para. 1 or 2 has been committed by negligence, the punishment is imprisonment up to three years and a fine from two thousand to fifteen thousand leva.

(4) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) A ship's captain or captain of another vessel who does not immediately report to the nearest port the discharge into the waters specified in para. 1 of petroleum products or derivatives, or other substances dangerous to people, animals or plants, shall be punished with a fine up to five hundred leva.

(5) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) A captain or other person from the command staff of a ship who does not fulfill the obligation to record in the ship's documents an operation with substances dangerous to people, animals or plants, or enters false information in them about such operations, or refuses to present these documents to the relevant officials, shall be punished with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.

#### Art. 353.

(1) (Am. - SG, issue 95 of 1975, am. - SG, issue 86 of 1991, am. - SG, issue 92 of 2002) An official who commissions or orders to be commissioned an enterprise or thermal power plant before the necessary treatment facilities have been put into operation shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred levs.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on an official who fails to fulfill their obligations to construct treatment facilities, as well as to ensure the serviceability and continuous proper operation of such facilities, as a result of which they have not been able to enter into operation fully or partially or have ceased to operate.

(3) (Am. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, am. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) If the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be probation or a fine of one hundred to three hundred levs.

(4) (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, am. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, am. - SG, issue 92 of 2002) In minor cases the punishment shall be: under para. 1 and 2 - a fine of one hundred to three hundred levs, and under para. 3 - a fine of one hundred to three hundred levs, imposed by administrative procedure.

Art. 353a. (New - SG, issue 86 of 1991, am. - SG, issue 85 of 1997) An official who, within the scope of their official duties, conceals or discloses false information about the state of the environment and its components - air, water, soil, marine areas - and this results in significant harm to the environment, life and human health, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of one hundred to one thousand levs.

Art. 353b. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, suppl. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever manages waste not in accordance with the established procedure and thereby creates a danger to the life or health of another or of causing significant harm to the environment shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to thirty thousand levs.

(2) When the act under para. 1 has caused:

- 1. death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twenty years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand levs;
- 2. significant harm to the environment, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand levs.
  - (3) Whoever violates or fails to fulfill their obligations to ensure the serviceability and proper functioning of an installation or facility for disposal or recovery of waste and thereby causes death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons shall be punished by imprisonment from five to twenty years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand levs, and if significant harm to the environment has been caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand levs.
  - (4) When the acts under para. 1 - 3 have been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from two thousand to fifteen thousand levs.

Art. 353c. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, am. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever manages hazardous waste not in accordance with the established procedure shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand levs.

(2) When the act under para. 1 creates a danger to the life or health of another or of causing significant harm to the environment, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand levs.

(3) When the act under para. 1 has caused death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years and a fine from fifteen thousand to fifty thousand levs, and if significant harm to the environment has been caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand levs.

(4) An official who violates or fails to fulfill their obligations for the management of hazardous waste shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years.

(5) When the acts under para. 1 - 3 have been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from three thousand to twenty thousand levs.

Art. 353d. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever transports waste across the border of the country in violation of the established procedure, when the act committed does not constitute a minor case, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to four years and a fine from two thousand to five thousand levs.

(2) Whoever transports across the border of the country hazardous waste, toxic chemical substances, biological agents, toxins and radioactive substances in violation of international treaties to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand levs.

(3) When the acts under para. 1 or 2 have been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to two years or probation. Art. 353e. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever stores hazardous substances or mixtures not in accordance with the established procedure and thereby creates a danger to the life or health of another or of causing significant harm to the environment shall be punished by imprisonment of up to four years and a fine from two thousand to five thousand levs.

(2) Whoever not in accordance with the established procedure commissions or orders to be commissioned an enterprise or installation for the functioning of which hazardous substances or mixtures are used, and thereby creates a danger to the life or health of another or of causing significant harm to the environment, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand levs.

(3) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on the person who not in accordance with the established procedure commissions or orders to be commissioned an enterprise or installation, the functioning of which may create a danger to the life or health of another or of causing significant harm to the environment.

(4) If in the cases under para. 2 and 3 death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons has been caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment from eight to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand levs, and if significant harm to the environment has been caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand levs.

(5) When the acts under para. 1 - 4 have been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to two years or probation. Art. 353f. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever in violation of the established procedure produces, uses, distributes, imports or exports across the border of the country substances that deplete the ozone layer shall be punished by imprisonment of up to four years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand levs.

(2) When the act under para. 1 has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year or probation. Art. 353g. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004, previous art. 353d - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever in violation of law constructs a water intake facility or facility for the use of surface or underground waters shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand levs.

Art. 353h. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004, previous art. 353e - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever in violation of law uses mineral water for economic activity shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year and a fine of up to five thousand levs.


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations | > Section III.

#### Art. 354.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever without proper authorization produces, acquires, possesses, alienates or transfers to another a potent or toxic substance which is not a narcotic substance placed under an authorization regime, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) If the crime under para. 1 is committed systematically, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(3) The object of the crime shall be forfeited to the state.

(4) (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever violates rules established for the production, acquisition, storage, accounting, dispensing, transportation or carrying of substances under para. 1 shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or by probation or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

Art. 354a. (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) (1) Whoever without proper authorization produces, processes, acquires or possesses narcotic substances or their analogues with the intent to distribute, or distributes narcotic substances or their analogues, shall be punished for high-risk narcotic substances or their analogues - by imprisonment from two to eight years and by a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva, and for risk narcotic substances or their analogues - by imprisonment from one to six years and by a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva. When the object of the crime is precursors or equipment or materials for the production of narcotic substances or their analogues, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) When the narcotic substances or their analogues are in large quantities, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva. Whoever without proper authorization in a public place acquires or possesses with the intent to distribute, or distributes narcotic substances or their analogues, as well as when the narcotic substances or their analogues are in particularly large quantities or the act is committed:

- 1. by a person who acts upon order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
- 2. by a doctor or pharmacist;
- 3. by an educator, teacher, head of an educational institution or official in or on the occasion of performing his/her service;
- 4. under conditions of dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva.
  - (3) Whoever without proper authorization acquires or possesses narcotic substances or their analogues shall be punished:
- 1. for high-risk narcotic substances or their analogues - by imprisonment from one to six years and by a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva;
- 2. for risk narcotic substances or their analogues - by imprisonment of up to five years and by a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
  - (4) Whoever violates rules established for the production, acquisition, storage, accounting, dispensing, transportation or carrying of narcotic substances shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and by a fine of up to five thousand leva, and the court may also impose deprivation of the rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
  - (5) In minor cases under para. 3 and 4, the punishment shall be a fine of up to one thousand leva.
  - (6) In cases under para. 1 - 5, the object and means of the crime shall be forfeited to the state.

Art. 354b. (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever induces or assists another toward the use of narcotic substances or their analogues shall be punished by imprisonment from one to eight years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000) When the act under para. 1 is committed:

- 1. in respect of a minor, juvenile or insane person;
- 2. in respect of more than two persons;
- 3. (supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) by a doctor, pharmacist, educator, teacher, head of an educational institution or official in places of deprivation of liberty in or on the occasion of performing his/her service;
- 4. (amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) in a public place;
- 5. (amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) through mass media;
- 6. (amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) under conditions of dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva, and the court in cases under item 3 shall also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
  - (3) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever gives to another a narcotic substance or its analogue in quantities that may cause death and such occurs shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years and a fine from one hundred thousand to three hundred thousand leva.
  - (4) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, former para. 3 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever systematically provides premises to various persons for the use of narcotic substances or organizes the use of such substances shall be punished by imprisonment from one to ten years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.
  - (5) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, former para. 4 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) A doctor who, in violation of the established procedure, knowingly prescribes to another narcotic substances or their analogues or medicines containing such substances shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and by a fine of up to three thousand leva, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
  - (6) (Former para. 5 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2004) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years, a fine of up to five thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

Art. 354c. (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever sows or cultivates plants of opium poppy and coca bush or plants of the cannabis genus in violation of the rules established in the Law on Control of Narcotic Substances and Precursors shall be punished by imprisonment from two to five years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever organizes, leads or finances an organized criminal group for the cultivation of plants under para. 1 or for obtaining, producing or processing narcotic substances shall be punished by imprisonment from ten to twenty years and by a fine from fifty thousand to two hundred thousand leva.

(3) (Supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever participates in an organized criminal group under the preceding paragraph shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.

(4) (Supplemented - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) A participant in an organized criminal group who has voluntarily reported to the authorities all facts and circumstances known to him/her regarding the activities of the criminal group shall not be punished.

(5) In minor cases under para. 1, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year and a fine of up to one thousand leva.

#### Art. 355.

(1) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 23 of 2020, amended - SG, issue 28 of 2020, effective from 24.03.2020) Whoever violates an ordinance, rules or measures issued against the spread or occurrence of an infectious disease in humans, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of one thousand to ten thousand leva.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 23 of 2020) If the act is committed during an epidemic, pandemic or state of emergency related to deaths, the punishment is imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of ten to fifty thousand leva.

(3) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever violates an ordinance issued for the prevention of food poisoning, shall be punished by probation or a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Art. 356.

(1) Whoever violates the established construction, sanitary or fire safety rules in the design, supervision or execution of construction works and thereby endangers the life of another, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years.

(2) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) If the violation of the rules under the preceding paragraph is committed through negligence, the punishment is imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

### Section IV.

Other generally dangerous crimes (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985) Art. 356a. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000, supplemented - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) For preparation for a crime under Art. 330, 333, 334, 340, 341a, 341b, Art. 342, para. 3, Art. 344, 349, 350, Art. 352, para. 1, Art. 353zh, 353z, Art. 354, 354a and 354v, if the committed act does not constitute a more serious crime, the punishment is imprisonment from three to eight years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.

Art. 356b. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985) (1) A foreign national who on the territory of the republic prepares to commit abroad an act under Art. 242, para. 2 and 3 or any of the acts specified in Art. 356a, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.

(2) If for the same purpose an organization or group has been created, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years, and for the organizers and leaders - imprisonment from three to eight years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.

Art. 356v. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985, repealed - SG, issue 21 of 2000)

### Section V.

Crimes in the Use of Atomic Energy for Peaceful Purposes (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985, corr. - SG, issue 80 of 1985) Art. 356g. (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) An official who orders or permits an activity to be commenced or carried out without a permit provided for in the Law on the Use of Atomic Energy for Peaceful Purposes being issued or before such permit is issued, or in deviation from the permit, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

(2) If the act under the previous paragraph is committed repeatedly or an immediate danger has been created for the life or health of another, the punishment is imprisonment for up to three years.

Art. 356d. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985, amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) An official who appoints or permits a person to work with nuclear material, nuclear facilities or other sources of ionizing radiation without the necessary qualification, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

Art. 356e. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) Whoever destroys or damages a nuclear facility, nuclear material or a source of ionizing radiation and thereby creates a danger for the life or health of another, for harm to the environment or for the occurrence of significant property damage, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) If in the cases under the previous paragraph there has resulted: a) (new - SG, issue 101 of 2017) harm to the environment or significant property damage, the punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years; b) (former letter "a" - SG, issue 101 of 2017) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from eight to fifteen years; c) (suppl. - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amend. - SG, issue 153 of 1998, former letter "b" - SG, issue 101 of 2017) death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under letter "a" the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

Art. 356zh. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985) When by the act under the previous article by negligence there have been caused: a) significant property damage; b) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under letter "a"; c) death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under letters "a" and "b", the punishment is: under letter "a" - imprisonment for up to five years; under letter "b" - imprisonment for up to eight years; under letter "c" - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.

Art. 356z. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985) (1) Whoever violates the rules for nuclear or radiation safety, admitting that bodily injury or death of another may result, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) Whoever violates the rules for nuclear or radiation safety and intentionally causes significant property damage, bodily injury or death of another, shall be punished: a) for causing significant property damage - by imprisonment from five to fifteen years; b) for causing medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under letter "a" - by imprisonment from five to twenty years; c) (suppl. - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amend. - SG, issue 153 of 1998) for causing death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under letters "a" and "b" - by imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.

Art. 356i. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985) When by an act under the previous article by negligence there have been caused: a) significant property damage; b) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under letter "a"; c) death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under letters "a" and "b", the punishment is: under letter "a" - imprisonment for up to five years; under letter "b" - imprisonment for up to eight years; under letter "c" - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.

Art. 356y. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) In the cases under Art. 356e, para. 2, Art. 356zh, letters "b" and "c", Art. 356z, para. 2 and Art. 356i, letters "b" and "c" the court shall deprive the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.

Art. 356k. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) (1) (Suppl. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) Whoever without proper authorization produces, processes, modifies, neutralizes, receives, acquires in any manner, possesses, uses, disperses, destroys, buries, transports, carries, moves, dispatches or transfers to another nuclear material or sources of ionizing radiation or components therefor, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.

(2) If in the cases under para. 1 there has resulted:

- 1. (new - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) danger of causing harm to the environment or significant property damage, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years;
- 2. (new - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) harm to the environment or significant property damage, the punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years;
