- 3. (former item 1 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) danger for the life or health of another, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years;
- 4. (former item 2 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, when the perpetrator did not desire and did not admit this, the punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years;
- 5. (former item 3 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) death of one or more persons, when the perpetrator did not desire and did not admit this, the punishment is imprisonment from eight to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
  - (3) (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) When the act under para. 1 is committed by negligence, the punishment is imprisonment for up to three years.

Art. 356l. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever unlawfully commits an act directed against a nuclear facility or constituting interference with the operation of a nuclear facility, intending or admitting that death, serious or medium bodily injury, significant property damage or harm to the environment may result through exposure to radioactive radiation or the release of radioactive substances, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years.

(2) If by the act under para. 1 there have been caused:

- 1. significant property damage or harm to the environment, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years;
- 2. medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under item 1, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years;
- 3. death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under item 1, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
  - (3) When by an act under para. 2 by negligence there have been caused:
- 1. significant property damage or harm to the environment;
- 2. medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under item 1;
- 3. death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under items 1 and 2, the punishment is: under item 1 - imprisonment for up to five years; under item 2 - imprisonment for up to eight years; under item 3 - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.

Art. 356m. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever by the use of force or threat demands to be provided with nuclear material or another source of ionizing radiation, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to ten years and confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.

(2) If in the cases under para. 1 the perpetrator threatens that:

- 1. he will use nuclear material or another source of ionizing radiation to: a) cause death, serious or medium bodily injury; b) cause significant property damage or harm to the environment;
- 2. he will take nuclear material by theft or robbery;
- 3. he will commit a crime under Art. 356l, in order to compel another to commit, omit or endure something contrary to his will, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years and confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.

Chapter Twelve.

CRIMES AGAINST THE DEFENSE CAPABILITY OF THE REPUBLIC, AGAINST INFORMATION CONSTITUTING A STATE SECRET, AND AGAINST FOREIGN CLASSIFIED INFORMATION (TITLE AMEND. - SG, ISSUE 26 OF 2004)


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Раздел I.

Crimes against information constituting state secrets and against foreign classified information (Загл. изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.)

#### Чл. 357.

(1) (Зал., предишна ал. 2, изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) Whoever discloses information constituting a state secret, which has been entrusted to them or became known to them by service or work, as well as one who discloses such information while being aware that this may result in harm to the interests of the Republic of Bulgaria, if not subject to a more severe punishment, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(2) (Предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) If the act has resulted or may result in particularly grave consequences for the security of the state, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) The punishment under para. 1 and 2 shall also apply to whoever discloses foreign classified information received under an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party.

Чл. 357а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) Чл. 357б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 41 от 2001 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.)

#### Чл. 358.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever loses documents, publications or materials containing information constituting a state secret, or foreign classified information received under an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or probation.

(2) If the act has resulted or may result in particularly grave consequences, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to five years.

#### Чл. 359.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever causes the disclosure of information constituting a state secret, or foreign classified information received under an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, through negligence, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or probation.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) If this has occurred due to non-compliance with the legally established requirements for the protection of classified information, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years.

#### Чл. 360.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever discloses information of a military, economic or other nature, which is not a state secret, but whose disclosure is prohibited by law, order or other administrative ordinance, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or probation.

### Раздел II.

Crimes against the performance of military service

#### Чл. 361.

(1) (Отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 20 от 2012 г., в сила от 10.06.2012 г.)

#### Чл. 362.

(Отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

#### Чл. 363.

(Отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

#### Чл. 364.

(1) Whoever evades military service during mobilization shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) (Отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.) If the act under para. 1 is committed during wartime, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.

#### Чл. 365.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

#### Чл. 366.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 20 от 2012 г., в сила от 10.06.2012 г.) Whoever violates their obligations under the military registration of Bulgarian citizens and equipment, outside the cases provided for in the special laws on military registration, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, and during wartime - by imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Чл. 367.

(1) Whoever does not execute the orders related to the preparation and conduct of mobilization, or obstructs its normal conduct, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to eight years, and in particularly grave cases - by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed through negligence, the punishment shall be probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

#### Чл. 368.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. и доп. - ДВ, бр. 20 от 2012 г., в сила от 10.06.2012 г.) Whoever deliberately violates their obligations during medical examination of servicemembers, reservists and reserves, or during inspection of equipment in connection with military registration, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to eight years, and during wartime - by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

### Раздел II "а".

Crimes against the performance of peacetime alternative service (Нов - ДВ, бр. 132 от 1998 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

Чл. 368а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 132 от 1998 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

Чл. 368б. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 132 от 1998 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

Чл. 368в. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 132 от 1998 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

Чл. 368г. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 132 от 1998 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

Чл. 368д. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 132 от 1998 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

Чл. 368е. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 132 от 1998 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

Чл. 368ж. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 132 от 1998 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 38 от 2007 г., в сила от 01.12.2007 г., изм. относно влизането в сила - ДВ, бр. 89 от 2007 г.)

### Раздел III.

Other crimes

#### Чл. 369.

(1) Whoever during wartime does not fulfill their obligations in connection with air defense, atomic defense and chemical defense, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years.

(2) If grave consequences have resulted from the act, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to eight years.

#### Чл. 370.

Whoever during wartime, being labor-mobilized, evades or does not fulfill their obligations in connection with the work assigned to them, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.

Глава тринадесета.

MILITARY CRIMES


This excerpt sits under: | Конституция | Правилници |

#### Art. 371.

(Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) Liable for crimes under this chapter are: a) servicemen under the Defence and Armed Forces of the Republic of Bulgaria Act; b) generals, officers and persons from the sergeant and regular composition from other ministries and departments; c) (am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2014) civil servants in the Ministry of Internal Affairs, when committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country or in connection with combat operations; d) (am. - SG, No. 20 of 2012, effective 10.06.2012) reservists during active service in the voluntary reserve and persons in wartime service; e) persons not mentioned in this article for complicity in crimes under this chapter.

### Section I.

Crimes against subordination and military honor

#### Art. 372.

(1) Whoever fails to execute or refuses to execute an order of his superior shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years.

(2) If this is committed by a group of persons or before formation, or in a demonstrative manner, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to five years.

(3) For crimes under the preceding paragraphs in particularly serious cases, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.

#### Art. 373.

The punishment under the respective paragraphs of the preceding article shall also be imposed for failure to execute or refusal to execute a lawful demand of a military official performing duties of military service.

#### Art. 374.

Whoever openly expresses discontent against an order or command of his superior shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year.

#### Art. 375.

Whoever threatens his superior or a military official performing duties of military service with bodily injury or murder shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.

#### Art. 376.

(1) Whoever resists his superior or a military official performing duties of military service shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever compels such person to violate his duties.

(3) If the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed with a weapon or by a group of persons, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to eight years, and in particularly serious cases - deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years.

#### Art. 377.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever commits a violent act against his superior, as well as against a military official during or in connection with the performance of a duty of military service, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to ten years.

(2) If serious consequences have resulted from the act, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years.

#### Art. 378.

(Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) Whoever insults or slanders a person specified in Art. 371, letters "a", "b", "c" and "d" shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year and public reprimand.

(2) When the act under the preceding paragraph is committed by a subordinate against a superior, by a superior against a subordinate, as well as by a military official or against a military official during or in connection with the performance of a duty of military service, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years and public reprimand.

(3) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on whoever, in the absence of his superior, says or does something of a nature to impair his honor or dignity.

#### Art. 379.

(Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) Whoever causes light bodily injury to a person specified in Art. 371, letters "a", "b", "c" and "d", if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year.

(2) For causing light bodily injury by a superior to a subordinate, by a military official during or in connection with the performance of a duty of military service or to more than one person, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years.

Art. 379a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever commits a violent or indecent act, grossly violating military order and expressing manifest disrespect for the military honor and dignity of a serviceman, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years.

### Section II.

Absence from military service

#### Art. 380.

(Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) Whoever without permission leaves his unit or place of service or fails to report for service upon assignment, transfer, secondment, from leave, medical or correctional facility for a period from one to three days, shall be punished for absence from military service by deprivation of liberty for up to two years.

(2) The punishment under the preceding paragraph shall also be imposed on whoever absents himself for less than one day, if the act is committed systematically, by a group or if the person has been convicted of absence from military service.

#### Art. 381.

(Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever absents himself from military service for more than three days or if the act under para. 1 of the preceding article is committed systematically, repeatedly or by a group, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.

#### Art. 382.

Whoever absents himself with intent to evade forever the performance of his duty of military service shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.

#### Art. 383.

(1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever absents himself from a duty of military service by simulating illness, forging a document or by another fraudulent means, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years.

(2) Whoever absents himself from a duty of military service by causing health disorder shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.

(3) If the act under the preceding paragraphs constitutes a particularly serious case, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.

#### Art. 384.

(Repealed - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982)

#### Art. 385.

Whoever refuses to perform duties of military service or systematically avoids their performance shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.

#### Art. 386.

(1) (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986) Whoever absents himself from military service, with intent to leave the territory of the country without permission of the competent authorities, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.

(2) (Former para. 1 - SG, No. 89 of 1986) Whoever absents himself from military service, by leaving the territory of the country without permission of the competent authorities or failing to return to it within the specified time, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.

(3) (Former para. 2, am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) For preparation under the preceding paragraphs, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to five years.

### Section III.

Official crimes

#### Чл. 387.

(1) Whoever abuses their power or official position, fails to fulfill their duties of office or exceeds their authority and harmful consequences result from this, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) If serious consequences have resulted from the act or it is committed systematically by a superior towards a subordinate, the punishment is imprisonment from one to eight years.

(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) If the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed with the purpose that the perpetrator obtain for himself or for another a material benefit or to cause harm to another, the punishment is: imprisonment from one to five years under para. 1, from three to eight years under para. 2, and in especially grave cases - from three to ten years, and the court shall also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.

(4) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., предишна ал. 3 - бр. 89 от 1986 г.) When the harmful consequences are caused by negligence, the punishment is: under para. 1 - imprisonment for up to two years, under para. 2 - imprisonment for up to five years.

#### Чл. 388.

(1) (Предишен текст на чл. 388, изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) Whoever through negligence violates or fails to fulfill their official duties and harmful consequences result from this, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) If serious consequences have resulted from the act, the punishment is imprisonment for up to five years.

### Раздел IV.

Crimes against guard, post, patrol, internal and border service

#### Чл. 389.

(1) Whoever intentionally or through negligence violates the statutory rules of guard, post or patrol service or orders issued on the basis of these rules, shall be punished with imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) If the crime is committed at a facility of especially important state or military significance, the punishment is imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) If the crime is accompanied by the occurrence of harmful consequences, for the prevention of which the guard, post or patrol was established, the punishment is imprisonment from one to eight years.

(4) If the act under para. 2 and 3 constitutes an especially grave case, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.

#### Чл. 390.

With the punishment under the respective paragraphs of the preceding article shall also be punished a person on duty who violates the established rules on monitoring or notification in connection with ensuring anti-aircraft, anti-atomic, anti-chemical or sanitary defense, as well as the defense of water areas.

#### Чл. 391.

(1) A person from the daily duty roster who intentionally or through negligence violates the statutory rules of internal service shall be punished in non-trivial cases with imprisonment for up to six months.

(2) If the crime is accompanied by the occurrence of harmful consequences, for the prevention of which the duty was established, the punishment is imprisonment for up to two years.

(3) If the act constitutes an especially grave case, the punishment is imprisonment for up to three years.

#### Чл. 392.

(1) A person from the border duty detail who intentionally or through negligence violates the rules on protection of the state border shall be punished with imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) If the act consisted of unauthorized abandonment or failure to take position at the guarded facility or section of the state border, the punishment is imprisonment from one to eight years.

(3) For the crimes under the preceding paragraphs in especially grave cases, the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years.

### Раздел V.

Other military crimes

#### Чл. 393.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) Whoever discloses in any manner information of a military nature constituting a state secret, or foreign classified information received under an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, if not subject to a more severe punishment, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.

#### Чл. 394.

(Зал. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.)

#### Чл. 395.

Whoever abandons or loses items given to him for personal use in service shall be punished in non-trivial cases with imprisonment for up to one year.

### Раздел VI.

Military crimes committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country (Загл. доп. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.)

#### Чл. 396.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) For military crimes committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country, the punishment is:

- 1. under Art. 387, para. 3, first sentence, 388, para. 1 and 391 - imprisonment for up to five years;
- 2. under Art. 372, para. 1, 373 in conjunction with Art. 372, para. 1, 374, 375, 380 and 387, para. 3, second sentence, 388, para. 2 - imprisonment for up to eight years;
- 3. under Art. 381, 385, 386, para. 2, 387, para. 1 - imprisonment from three to ten years;
- 4. under Art. 373, in conjunction with Art. 372, para. 2 and 3, 387, para. 2, 389, para. 2 and 3, and 390 - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.

#### Чл. 397.

(1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) For the crimes under Art. 372, para. 2 and 3, 376, 377, para. 2, 382, 383, para. 3, committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years or life imprisonment without commutation.

(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) For the crime under Art. 101, para. 2 and 386, para. 1, committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country, the punishment is imprisonment of twenty years or life imprisonment without commutation.

Чл. 397а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Доп. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) Whoever in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country unlawfully leaves his place of service or fails to report for duty for not more than one day and night shall be punished with imprisonment for up to five years.

(2) (Доп. - ДВ, бр. 75 от 2006 г., в сила от 13.10.2006 г.) Whoever in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country through negligence fails to report for duty for more than one day and night shall be punished with imprisonment for up to five years.

### Раздел VII.

Crimes in connection with military operations

#### Чл. 398. (1) A commander who:

a) does not destroy or render inoperative means for waging war entrusted to him or does not take appropriate measures for their destruction or rendering inoperative when they are threatened by imminent danger of capture by the enemy, or б) although not having received an order for military operations, but when it was necessary to act, does not timely call for the issuance of such an order or in urgent circumstances does not act on his own initiative, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to fifteen years, insofar as the act does not constitute a more serious crime.

(2) If the act is committed through negligence, the punishment is imprisonment for up to five years.

#### Чл. 399.

(Изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г.) A commander of a perishing military vessel who abandons the vessel without having fulfilled his official duty to the end, as well as a person from the vessel's crew who abandons the vessel without an order from his commander, shall be punished with imprisonment from ten to twenty years or with life imprisonment without commutation.

#### Article 400.

(Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) Whoever unauthorizedly leaves the battlefield during combat or surrenders as a prisoner due to fear or cowardice, or refuses to act with weapons during combat, shall be punished by imprisonment of twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.

#### Article 401.

Whoever, while in captivity, voluntarily participates in work that has direct military significance, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.

#### Article 402.

Whoever, while in captivity, as a senior person treats another prisoner of war cruelly, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.

#### Article 403.

Whoever, while in captivity, in order to obtain benefits or secure lenient treatment from the enemy, commits acts harmful to another prisoner of war, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years.

#### Article 404.

Whoever robs, steals, appropriates, damages, destroys or unlawfully takes property from the population located in the area of military operations, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to fifteen years, and in particularly serious cases - by imprisonment from ten to fifteen years.

#### Article 405.

Whoever on the battlefield takes things from a wounded person, prisoner or killed person with the intention of unlawfully appropriating them, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished for looting by imprisonment from three to fifteen years, and in particularly serious cases - by imprisonment from ten to fifteen years.

Supplementary Provision

#### Article 406.

(1) (Amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998, amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) For the crimes under this chapter for which a penalty of imprisonment of more than ten years or life imprisonment without substitution is provided, the court shall also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6, 7, 9 and 10.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) For the crimes under Art. 386 and 393, the court may impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 9 and 10.

(3) (Para. 3 repealed, former para. 4, amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 86 of 2005, effective from 29.04.2006)

(4) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 86 of 2005, effective from 29.04.2006)

Chapter Fourteen.

CRIMES AGAINST PEACE AND HUMANITY

### Section I.

Crimes against peace

#### Article 407.

Whoever conducts war propaganda in any manner shall be punished by imprisonment of up to eight years.

#### Article 408.

Whoever, directly or indirectly, through print, word, radio or in another manner, seeks to provoke armed attack by one state against another, shall be punished for incitement to war by imprisonment from three to ten years.

#### Article 409.

(Amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) Whoever plans, prepares or wages aggressive war shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.

### Section II.

Crimes against the laws and customs of warfare

#### Article 410. Whoever in violation of the rules of international law on the conduct of war:

a) commits or orders to be committed against wounded, sick, shipwrecked persons or medical personnel murder, torture or inhuman treatment, including biological experiments, causes or orders to be caused to such persons severe suffering, mutilation or other injury to health; b) commits or orders to be committed significant destruction or appropriation of medical materials or installations, (amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) shall be punished by imprisonment from five to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.

#### Article 411. Whoever in violation of the rules of international law on the conduct of war:

a) commits or orders to be committed against prisoners of war murder, torture or inhuman treatment, including biological experiments, causes or orders to be caused to such persons severe suffering, mutilation or other injury to health; b) compels a prisoner to serve in the armed forces of the hostile state or c) deprives a prisoner of the right to be tried by a regular court and according to regular procedure, (amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) shall be punished by imprisonment from five to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.

#### Article 412. Whoever in violation of the rules of international law on the conduct of war:

a) commits or orders to be committed against the civilian population murder, torture, inhuman treatment, including biological experiments, causes or orders to be caused severe suffering, mutilation or other serious injury to health; b) takes or orders to be taken hostages; c) commits or orders to be committed unlawful deportations, persecutions or detentions; d) compels a civilian person to serve in the armed forces of a hostile state; e) deprives a civilian person of the right to be tried by a regular court and according to regular procedure; f) unlawfully or arbitrarily commits or orders to be committed destruction or appropriation of property on a large scale, (amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) shall be punished by imprisonment from five to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.

#### Article 413.

Whoever without having the right wears the emblem of the Red Cross or Red Crescent or whoever abuses the flag or emblem of the Red Cross or Red Crescent, or the color designated for medical evacuation vehicles, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years.

#### Article 414.

(1) Whoever in violation of the rules of international law on the conduct of war destroys, damages or renders unfit cultural or historical monuments and objects, works of art, buildings and facilities with cultural, scientific or other humanitarian purpose, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to ten years.

(2) The same penalty shall also be imposed on whoever steals, unlawfully appropriates or conceals objects referred to in the preceding paragraph, or imposes contribution or confiscation in respect of such objects.

#### Article 415.

(1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever in violation of the rules of international law on the conduct of war uses or orders to be used nuclear, chemical, bacteriological, biological or toxic weapons or prohibited means or methods of warfare, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

(2) (Amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) If particularly serious consequences have resulted from this, the penalty is imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.

Article 415a. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever undertakes military preparations for the use of nuclear, chemical, bacteriological, biological or toxic weapons as a method of warfare shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.

### Section III.

Destruction of groups of the population (genocide) and apartheid (Title supplemented - SG, No. 95 of 1975)

#### Art. 416. (1) Whoever with the purpose to destroy in whole or in part a particular national, ethnic, racial or religious group:

a) causes death, serious bodily injury or permanent impairment of consciousness to a person belonging to such a group; b) places the group in such living conditions that lead to its complete or partial physical destruction; c) takes measures aimed at preventing births within such a group; d) forcibly transfers children from one group to another, (amd. - SG, no. 153 from 1998) shall be punished for genocide by imprisonment from ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.

(2) (Previous Art. 417 - SG, no. 95 from 1975) Whoever commits preparation for genocide shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.

(3) (Previous Art. 418 - SG, no. 95 from 1975) Whoever publicly and directly incites genocide shall be punished by imprisonment from one to eight years.

#### Art. 417.

(New - SG, no. 95 from 1975) Whoever with the purpose to establish or maintain domination or systematic oppression of one racial group of people over another racial group of people: a) causes death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons from this group of people or b) imposes living conditions of a nature to cause complete or partial physical destruction of a racial group of people, (amd. - SG, no. 153 from 1998) shall be punished for apartheid by imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.

#### Art. 418.

(New - SG, no. 95 from 1975) Whoever with the purpose under the previous article: a) unlawfully deprives of liberty members of a racial group of people or subjects them to forced labor; b) puts into effect measures to prevent the participation of a racial group of people in the political, social, economic and cultural life of the country and to deliberately create conditions that obstruct the full development of such a group of people, in particular by depriving its members of the fundamental freedoms and rights of citizens; c) puts into effect measures to divide the population by racial criteria through the creation of reservations and ghettos, through prohibition of mixed marriages between members of different racial groups or through expropriation of land property belonging to them; d) deprives fundamental rights and freedoms of organizations or persons because they oppose apartheid, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.

Supplementary Provisions (Title amd. - SG, no. 33 from 2011, effective from 27.05.2011)

#### Art. 419.

In accordance with the distinctions under the previous articles, he who knowingly allows his subordinate to commit an offense provided for in this chapter shall also be punished.

Art. 419a. (New - SG, no. 33 from 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, amd. - SG, no. 67 from 2023) (1) Whoever publicly justifies, denies or grossly trivializes a committed offense under this chapter and the act is committed in a manner that may create a danger that violence will be exercised or hatred will be created against individual persons or groups of people united by race, color, religion, origin, national or ethnic affiliation, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years.

(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed when the offense under this chapter was committed during and in connection with the National Socialist regime.

(3) Whoever incites another to an offense under para. 1 and 2 shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year.

Transitional Provisions

#### Art. 420.

This code enters into force on May 1, 1968 and repeals:

- 1. The Penal Code of 1951,
- 2. The Law on the Protection of Peace of 1951,
- 3. The Decree on Strengthening the Fight Against Persons Evading Socially Useful Labor and Leading an Antisocial, Parasitic Way of Life.

#### Art. 421.

Persons sentenced to deprivation of the right to be voters or to be elected or of the right to receive a particular pension (Art. 28, points 1 and 4 of the Penal Law of 1951) shall be released from this punishment from the day this code enters into force.

#### Art. 422.

The death penalty of persons sentenced for offenses for which such a penalty is not provided for under this code shall be replaced by imprisonment for a term of twenty years. This provision enters into force from the day of promulgation of this code.

#### Art. 423.

The measures imposed before this code entered into force under the Decree on Strengthening the Fight Against Persons Evading Socially Useful Labor and Leading an Antisocial, Parasitic Way of Life shall be executed according to the procedure established by it, with the maximum term of these measures under Art. 1, para. 1, letter "b" reduced to three years.

#### Art. 424.

(Amend. - SG, No. 92 of 1969, amend. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amend. - SG, No. 3 of 1977, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1979, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 1 of 1991) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, suppl. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) For the acts under Art. 135, para. 5, Art. 218b, Art. 225b, para. 3, Art. 228, para. 3, Art. 231, para. 2, Art. 232, para. 3, Art. 242, para. 6, Art. 352a, para. 5 and Art. 353, para. 4, the provisions of the Law on Administrative Violations and Penalties shall apply.

(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 3 of 1977, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1979, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982; suppl. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) The penal decrees shall be issued: a) (amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) under Art. 135, para. 5 - by the Minister of Health; б) (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, SG, No. 10 of 1993; suppl. - SG No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amend. - SG, No. 14 of 2015) under Art. 225b, para. 3 - by the Minister of Finance; under Art. 228, para. 3 and Art. 231, para. 2 - by the Minister of Economy or by the Chairman of the State Agency for Metrological and Technical Surveillance; в) (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amend. - SG, No. 14 of 2015) under Art. 232, para. 3 - by the Minister of Economy, by the Minister of Finance or by the Chairman of the State Agency for Metrological and Technical Surveillance; г) (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) under Art. 242, para. 6 - by the Minister of Finance; д) (amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 93 of 2009, in force from 25.12.2009, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) under Art. 352a, para. 5 and Art. 353, para. 4 - by the Minister of Environment and Waters, and when the violation is in connection with pollution of marine waters - by the Minister of Environment and Waters or by the Minister of Transport, Information Technology and Communications. е) (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986; deleted - SG, No. 10 of 1993) ж) (new - SG, No. 89 of 1986; repealed - SG, No. 1 of 1991)

(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) In the cases under the preceding paragraph, the penal decrees may also be issued by officials designated by the heads of the respective departments.

(4) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, repealed - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011)

(5) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) The administrative penalty of fine under Art. 218b shall be imposed by penal decree by the mayor, on the basis of materials sent to him by the prosecutor, or on the basis of an act from the administration of the enterprise, institution or from the control authorities. When the violation is established by the bodies of the Ministry of Internal Affairs, the penal decree shall be issued by the Minister of Internal Affairs or by a person authorized by him.

(6) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 86 of 2005, in force from 29.04.2006) With respect to military personnel, as well as officers and sergeants and privates from other departments, the administrative penalties provided for in this Code shall be imposed by the respective commanders and chiefs who have the right to impose disciplinary penalties on them. In this case, appeals against the penal decrees shall be reviewed by a military court.

(7) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 86 of 2005, in force from 29.04.2006)

(8) (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend., SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) In minor cases of violations under Art. 225b, para. 3, established at the time of their commission, the authorized control authorities may impose on the spot a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva under the procedure of Art. 39, para. 2 of the Law on Administrative Violations and Penalties.

#### Art. 425.

(New - SG, No. 10 of 1993, repealed - SG, No. 51 of 1999)

#### Art. 426.

(New - SG, No. 50 of 1995) Persons who, on the basis of the former Art. 343b, second sentence, have been deprived of the right to drive a motor vehicle for life, may restore their right not earlier than three years from serving the sentence of imprisonment and after passing an examination for acquiring the right to drive a motor vehicle.

AMENDMENTS TO OTHER LAWS

#### § 1. In the Law on Bulgarian Citizenship:

In Art. 26, the words "and in particularly important cases - with imprisonment" shall be deleted.

#### § 2. In the Law on Forests:

1. In Art. 42, para. 1, after the words "cut down or fallen" the words "when the amount of damage is below one hundred leva" shall be added. Paragraph 2 of the same article shall be repealed.

2. In Art. 43, the words "paragraph 1" and the words at the end "and in the cases under para. 2 of the same article - with imprisonment of up to three years or with correctional labor" shall be deleted.

3. In Art. 45, para. 1, after the words "forest nursery" the words "when the amount of damage is below one hundred leva" shall be added. Paragraph 2 of the same article shall be repealed.

4. In Art. 52, para. 2, the words "with imprisonment of up to three years or with correctional labor" shall be replaced by the words "with a fine of up to five hundred leva".

#### § 3. In the Law on Protection of Plants from Diseases and Pests:

In Art. 10, para. 1, after the words "quarantine of plants" the words "insofar as the act committed does not constitute a crime" shall be added, and the words "with imprisonment of up to five years, and in minor cases" shall be deleted.

#### § 4. In the Law on Customs:

1. In Art. 51, para. 1, after the words "in the established order" the words "insofar as the act committed does not constitute a crime" shall be added.

2. Paragraph 2 of Art. 51 shall be amended as follows:

"The goods that are the object of smuggling, as well as the transport and carrying means that served primarily for their transportation or carrying across the border, regardless of whose property they are, shall be forfeited to the State, and if they are missing or have been alienated, their equivalent value at the respective State retail prices shall be awarded".

3. In Articles 52, 53, 54 and 59, the words "shall be confiscated" shall be replaced by the words "shall be forfeited to the State", and in Art. 61 the words "to confiscation" shall be replaced by the words "to forfeiture to the State".

4. Articles 55 and 57 shall be repealed.

#### § 5. In the Law on Transactions with Foreign Currency Values and Foreign Currency Control:

1. In Art. 37 and 38, para. 2, the words "under Art. 240" shall be replaced by the words "under Art. 250".

#### 2. Article 39 shall be amended as follows:

"For the acts under Art. 250, para. 3 of the Penal Code, the provisions of Chapter XXVIII of the Code of Criminal Procedure shall apply.

The penal decrees shall be issued by the Minister of Finance or by officials designated by him.

If the value of the subject matter of the act is up to twenty leva, a fine in double amount shall be imposed by the authority that established the violation, or by another duly authorized person. The imposition of the fine shall be subject to appeal before the Ministry of Finance.

#### § 6. In the Law on Rents:

In Art. 37, the words "with imprisonment of up to one year or" shall be deleted.

#### § 7. In the Law on Protection of Air, Waters and Soil from Pollution:

Articles 18 and 19 shall be repealed.

#### § 8. In the Law on Planned Development of Populated Places:

In Art. 67, para. 1, the words "with imprisonment of up to one year" shall be replaced by the words "with a fine of up to one thousand leva".


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations | > Section III.

#### § 9. In the Hunting Act:

#### Article 21 is amended as follows:

"Whoever kills or captures without proper permission large game, such as deer, doe, wild goat, moose, roe deer, bear, wild boar, shall be punished under Art. 237, para. 1 of the Penal Code.

Whoever without holding a hunting license, kills or captures hare, marten, squirrel, capercaillie, pheasant, partridge or chukar, as well as one who, even if holding a hunting license, kills or captures such game in prohibited time, in prohibited place or with prohibited means, shall be punished under Art. 237, para. 2 of the Penal Code.

Whoever, without holding a hunting license, kills or captures wild goose, wild duck, bustard, widgeon, woodcock, dove, turtle dove, quail and all other types of useful game, subject to hunting, shall be punished with a fine of up to fifty leva.

With the punishment under the previous paragraph shall be punished also one who, even if holding a hunting license, kills or captures such game in prohibited time, in prohibited place or with prohibited means.

The killed or captured game shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if missing or alienated, its equivalent value shall be paid".

#### § 10. In the Fisheries Act:

1. Paragraph 2 of Art. 23 is repealed.

2. Articles 24 and 35 are repealed.

#### § 11. In the Act on Control of Explosives, Weapons and Ammunition:

In Art. 14 the words "deprivation of liberty" are deleted.

The implementation of this code is assigned to the Minister of Justice.

Final Provisions

TO THE LAW ON AMENDMENT AND SUPPLEMENT OF THE PENAL CODE

(PROM. - SG, ISSUE 28 OF 1982, IN FORCE FROM 01.07.1982)

#### § 151.

For the crimes which according to this law are prosecuted upon complaint of the injured party, the time limits under Art. 84, para. 1 and 2 begin to run from the entry into force of the law. Pending court proceedings are completed under the existing procedure.

#### § 159. This law enters into force from 1 July 1982.

Transitional and Final Provisions

TO THE LAW ON AMENDMENT AND SUPPLEMENT OF THE PENAL CODE

(PROM. - SG, ISSUE 10 OF 1993)

#### § 43.

Throughout the text of the Penal Code the words "People's Republic of Bulgaria" are replaced with "Republic of Bulgaria", the words "the People's Republic" are replaced with "the Republic", the words "the People's Militia" are replaced with "the police", the words "the socialist economy" and "the national economy" are replaced with "the economy", the words "the rules of socialist community living" are replaced with "good morals" and the words "the State Council" are replaced with "the President".

Transitional and Final Provisions

TO THE LAW ON AMENDMENT AND SUPPLEMENT OF THE PENAL CODE

(PROM. - SG, ISSUE 62 OF 1997)

#### § 75.

The provisions of Art. 255 and 257 enter into force three months after the promulgation of this law in the "State Gazette". If within this period the undeclared and unpaid tax obligations, together with the due interest, are paid into the budget, preliminary proceedings are not instituted.

Transitional and Final Provisions

TO THE LAW ON AMENDMENT AND SUPPLEMENT OF THE PENAL CODE

(PROM. - SG, ISSUE 21 OF 2000)

#### § 26.

Pending proceedings as of the day of entry into force of the law for crimes under Art. 146 - 148a are completed under the existing procedure, if within a three-month period from the entry into force of the law the injured party requests this.

Supplementary Provisions

TO THE LAW ON AMENDMENT AND SUPPLEMENT OF THE PENAL CODE

(PROM. - SG, ISSUE 92 OF 2002, AMD. - SG, ISSUE 26 OF 2004, IN FORCE FROM 01.01.2004, AMD. - SG, ISSUE 103 OF 2004, IN FORCE FROM 23.11.2004)

#### § 87.

Throughout the Special Part the punishment fine of up to one hundred leva is replaced with "fine from one hundred to three hundred leva".

(Ed. note - In this case it concerns the introduction of a minimum amount of the punishment fine, in the amount of one hundred to three hundred leva, and not the replacement of the words "fine of up to one hundred leva". This is the opinion of participants in the development of the amendment whom the editorial office consulted, due to the unclear wording of the amendment.)

#### § 88.

Throughout the General Part the words "mandatory resettlement", "the mandatory resettlement", "deprivation of the right to reside in a specific settlement" and "deprivation of the right to reside in a specific settlement" are deleted, and in the Special Part the words "mandatory resettlement" and "the mandatory resettlement" are replaced respectively with "probation" and "the probation".

#### § 89.

(Amd. - SG, issue 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004) Paragraphs 1, 3, 5, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12 - with respect to the punishment probation, § 13 - 17, § 18, item 2 - 5, § 19, 20, 21, § 42, item 4, § 43 and 88 enter into force from 1 January 2005.

#### § 90.

(Suppl. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 23.11.2004, amd. - SG, issue 86 of 2005, in force from 29.04.2006) Recidivists convicted with final sentences under the conditions of Art. 26 in force until the entry into force of this law (in force from 8 August 1997), serve punishment for each conviction, but not more than the maximum amount provided by law for the most serious of the crimes for which they have been convicted. The punishment is determined according to the procedure of Art. 306 of the Criminal Procedure Code.

#### § 91.

Pending proceedings as of the day of entry into force of the law for crimes under Art. 172, para. 2, Art. 182, para. 2 and Art. 183 are completed under the existing procedure, if within a three-month period from the entry into force of the law the injured party requests this.
